*Oren Halvani,Sophie Titze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将作者身份验证（AV）应用于德语音视频转录文本，通过十个AV方法在三个自建语料库（300个视频、150位说话人）上进行实验，发现基于字符和词元n-gram的传统方法表现最佳（最高88%准确率、90% AUC），而基于transformer的现代方法在所有语料上表现明显更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AV研究主要聚焦于书面文本和英语，对口语（如视频中的语音）以及其他语言（如德语）关注不足。作者旨在填补这些研究空白，验证既有AV方法在跨语言、跨模态场景下的有效性。

Method: 作者将AV任务应用于德语音频转录文本，构建了三个自编语料库，包含300个视频、150位说话人。系统评估了共10种AV方法，涵盖传统方法（字符和词元n-gram）与现代方法（transformer-based）进行比较。

Result: 传统AV方法（基于简单字符和词元n-gram表示）在所有语料库上表现最佳，最高达到88%准确率和90% AUC；而基于transformer的方法在所有评估语料上表现显著更差。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在作者身份验证领域，传统方法仍然具有竞争力和相关性，即使面对口语化、非英语的新任务场景也是如此。

Abstract: Authorship Verification (AV) represents an important subfield of digital text forensics and addresses the fundamental question of whether two texts were written by the same author. Although the field has made substantial progress over the past two decades, several important challenges remain unresolved or underexplored. For instance, most AV research has focused on written texts, despite the fact that language is expressed not only in written but also in spoken form, such as in videos. Moreover, existing AV studies have predominantly concentrated on English, while other languages, including German, have received comparatively little attention. To address these research gaps, we apply AV to spoken language in the form of transcripts of German-language videos and examine the effectiveness of established AV methods in verifying a speaker's identity across video pairs. Our experimental evaluation, based on a total of ten AV methods applied to three self-compiled corpora comprising 300 videos from 150 speakers, shows that the best performance (up to 88% accuracy and 90% AUC) is achieved by traditional AV approaches based on simple character- and token n-gram representations. In contrast, more modern transformer-based approaches perform significantly worse on all evaluated corpora. Our results therefore suggest that traditional methods in the field of AV remain both competitive and relevant.

</details>


### [33] [Learning Latent Reasoning Traces for Scalar Reward Models End-to-End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29185)
*Sanwoo Lee,Clive Bai,Hsiu-Yuan Huang,Kun Liang,Weijie Liu,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LatentRM 是一种新颖的奖励建模框架，将中间推理轨迹学习为离散潜变量，通过端到端在线策略优化来显式最大化下游标量奖励的似然，从而将深度推理评估与精确评分紧密结合，在分布内、分布外及 RLHF 任务中均优于标量、生成式和混合奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统标量奖励模型依赖表面线索，难以泛化到复杂或分布外任务；生成式奖励模型虽通过推理提升鲁棒性，但缺乏数值灵活性和概率可解释性。现有混合方法虽结合两者，但采用离策略多任务并行优化，无法保证生成的推理轨迹真正服务于下游标量奖励预测，存在两者之间的失配问题。

Method: LatentRM 将中间推理轨迹建模为离散潜变量，并通过在线策略优化端到端地优化潜推理空间，使推理轨迹与下游标量奖励预测形成紧密耦合，从而兼顾推理深度与精确评分。

Result: 在分布内、分布外数据集以及 RLHF 实验中，LatentRM 在偏好建模和策略对齐方面均优于标量、生成式和混合奖励模型，涵盖开放式对话到复杂推理等多种任务。

Conclusion: LatentRM 通过将推理轨迹作为潜变量与标量奖励端到端联合优化，有效弥合了生成式推理与标量评分之间的鸿沟，为奖励建模提供了更鲁棒且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Reward models (RMs) are central to aligning large language models with human preferences via reinforcement learning. Although traditional scalar RMs enable efficient and probabilistic reward modeling, they rely on superficial cues that fail to generalize to complex or out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks. Conversely, generative RMs leverage extensive reasoning to improve robustness on challenging tasks, but their natural language-based scores lack the numerical flexibility and probabilistic interpretability that scalar RMs offer. While recent approaches combine both paradigms through off-policy multi-task learning, such parallel optimization does not guarantee that generated reasoning traces actively align with or benefit downstream scalar reward prediction. To address this mismatch, we propose LatentRM, a reward modeling framework that learns intermediate reasoning traces as discrete latent variables to explicitly maximize the likelihood of downstream scalar rewards. Through on-policy optimization of the latent reasoning space end-to-end, LatentRM tightly couples deep reasoning-based evaluation with precise scoring. Extensive validations on in-distribution and OOD datasets and RLHF show that LatentRM outperforms scalar, generative, and hybrid RMs on preference modeling and policy alignment across tasks ranging from open-ended conversation to complex reasoning.

</details>


### [34] [Detecting Experiential Intertextuality Across Migration Routes: Beyond Surface Similarity in French Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29188)
*Sakayo Toadoum Sari,Nelly Robin,Michelle Auzanneau,Lakhdar Sais,Veronique Petit,Marie Veniard,Said Jabbour,Fabien Delorme*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次提出"经验互文性检测"任务，旨在从不同迁徙路线的移民叙事中自动识别共享的经验回声（如警察暴力、走私者剥削、危险穿越、家庭分离等）。基于108篇法语移民叙事，论文比较了多种无标注方法（词汇基线、句嵌入、POS结构特征、主题词典、叙事上下文特征及Qwen2.5-7B和Mistral-7B零样本评分），最终有监督混合模型达到r=0.45的最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 来自不同地理路线（如跨撒哈拉和巴尔干走廊）的移民叙事常呈现惊人的平行经验，但现有方法无法自动识别这种跨叙事的经验互文性。该任务具有重要的人文研究价值，但缺乏标注数据，因此需要探索无需人工标注的检测方法。

Method: 从108篇法语移民叙事中自动生成句子对，采用多种无标注评分方法：词汇基线、句嵌入、基于POS的结构特征、迁移特定主题词典、上下文感知叙事特征，以及Qwen2.5-7B和Mistral-7B两种LLM在三种提示策略下的零样本评分。最后构建包含31个特征的监督混合模型。

Result: 所有表面、结构和嵌入方法与专家判断仅弱相关（r≤0.30）；Qwen2.5-7B零样本达到最佳单方法相关（r=0.38）；少样本示例降低Qwen性能但显著提升Mistral；叙事位置显著预测互文性，出发阶段句子对经验回声最强；监督混合模型达到r=0.45，比最佳单方法提升21%。

Conclusion: 经验互文性检测是一项具有挑战性的任务，现有无标注方法的性能有限。LLM零样本评分（尤其是Qwen2.5-7B）优于传统方法，但监督混合模型仍是最佳方案。研究还揭示了叙事位置与经验互文性之间的重要关联，为后续研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Migrants traversing geographically distinct routes such as the Trans-Saharan and Balkan corridors often recount strikingly parallel lived experiences: police violence, smuggler exploitation, dangerous crossings, and family separation. We introduce the task of experiential intertextuality detection: automatically identifying shared experiential echoes across migration narratives without requiring annotated training data. From 108 French migration narratives spanning both corridors, we automatically generate sentence pairs and score them using annotation-free methods: lexical baselines, sentence embeddings, POS-based structural features, a migration-specific theme lexicon, context-aware narrative features, and zero-shot LLM scoring with Qwen2.5-7B and Mistral-7B under three prompting strategies. We validate all methods against 816 expert-annotated intertextuality judgments (inter-annotator Krippendorff's $α= 0.27$). Our results reveal that all surface, structural, and embedding methods correlate only weakly with expert judgments ($r \leq 0.30$); Qwen2.5-7B zero-shot achieves the best single-method correlation ($r = 0.38$); few-shot examples degrade Qwen but dramatically improve Mistral; narrative position significantly predicts intertextuality, with departure-phase pairs showing the highest experiential echoes; and a supervised hybrid combining all 31 features achieves $r = 0.45$, a 21% improvement over the best individual method.

</details>


### [35] [Hy-MultiTurn: A Six-Dimensional Benchmark for Deep Multi-Turn Dialogue Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29196)
*Eileen Ye,Jiawen Tao,Yaoming Li,Chenxu Liu,Wenhan Yu,Yaxin Fan,Xiaokun Yuan,Mengzhou Wu,Yanbing Jiang,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Hy-MultiTurn是一个中文深度多轮对话理解基准，通过分析真实聊天机器人故障总结出六种常见失败机制，构建了209个受控任务（每任务12-76轮），评估22个前沿模型配置后发现即使最强的GPT-5.5也仅41.1%的回应完全满足要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮对话基准通常只覆盖短对话，无法充分评估长多轮交互中的关键能力（如记住早期细节、追踪后续修订、识别指代对象、抑制不当行动等），且对模型失败原因缺乏深入洞察，尤其在中文场景中这一问题更为突出。

Method: 作者分析真实聊天机器人失败案例，归纳出六种常见失败机制，据此设计六种受控评估模式：约束记忆、精确执行、约束综合、对象定位、行动抑制和指代消解。构建209个受控任务，包含12-76轮对话，并通过对话长度、无关话题干扰和口语化表达增加难度。

Result: 评估22个前沿模型配置后发现：最强模型GPT-5.5在所有回应中仅41.1%完全满足要求；没有任何模型在所有六种模式中表现最佳，表明该基准具有广泛挑战性。

Conclusion: Hy-MultiTurn填补了中文长多轮对话深度理解评估的空白，六种受控评估模式能够系统揭示模型在长对话中的具体失败类型，为理解模型为何及如何失败提供了更深入的视角。

Abstract: Long-running multi-turn interactions with chatbots and agents are now common, and a correct response often depends on remembering earlier details, tracking later revisions, identifying intended objects or referents, and withholding action when required conditions are unmet. Existing multi-turn benchmarks typically cover short exchanges and do not fully evaluate these capabilities in long multi-turn interactions, particularly in Chinese, while offering limited insight into how and why models fail. To address these limitations, we analyze real chatbot failures to identify six recurring mechanisms and use them to define six controlled evaluation modes in Hy-MultiTurn, a Chinese benchmark for deep multi-turn dialogue understanding. The six modes evaluate constraint memory, precise execution, constraint synthesis, object localization, action suppression, and reference resolution. Across the six modes, we construct 209 controlled tasks spanning 12-76 turns, with dialogue length, irrelevant-topic distraction, and colloquial phrasing adding further difficulty. Evaluation of 22 frontier model configurations shows that Hy-MultiTurn is broadly challenging, as even GPT-5.5, the strongest overall configuration, satisfies all requirements in only 41.1 percent of responses and no model performs best in all six modes.

</details>


### [36] [Knowing When to Quit: Diagnosing and Training LLMs to Abort Futile Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29211)
*Xinyan Guan,Jiali Zeng,Chunlei Xin,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun,Fandong Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CaRL（能力对齐强化学习）方法，通过奖励塑形和事后拒绝增强，使大语言模型在超出能力范围的任务上主动拒绝而非产生看似合理但错误的推理，在减少徒劳推理的同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在超出能力范围的任务上会产生计算昂贵但语义空洞的推理，这些看似合理但错误的推导可能误导用户。现有研究缺乏对这一"徒劳推理"现象的系统和定性分析，更缺乏针对性的解决方案。

Method: 提出了CaRL方法，包含两个核心组件：(1) 奖励塑形（reward shaping），通过奖励设计激励模型在能力边界处拒绝回答而非进行徒劳推理；(2) 事后拒绝增强（hindsight refusal augmentation），将模型失败案例转化为拒绝监督信号，用于训练模型识别何时应当拒绝。

Result: 实验表明CaRL在各类任务难度上显著减少了徒劳推理现象，同时保持了任务性能，成功实现了能力对齐的行为，且不牺牲模型效用。

Conclusion: 徒劳推理是大语言模型的普遍性能力越界现象，表现为系统性的能力与行为失校准，且主要失败模式是表面有效但包含细微错误的诡辩推理。CaRL方法证明了通过能力对齐的强化学习可以有效地让模型在能力边界处"知进退"。

Abstract: Large language models generate computationally expensive yet semantically void reasoning on beyond-capability tasks, creating risks where plausible-sounding but incorrect derivations mislead users. We characterize this \textit{futile reasoning} phenomenon through systematic analysis, revealing universal capability overreach and systematic miscalibration between capability and behavior. The dominant failure mode is specious reasoning, which outputs look superficially valid but contain subtle errors, escalating with task difficulty. To address this, we introduce \textbf{CaRL} (\textbf{Ca}pability-\textbf{a}ligned \textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning), which aligns model behavior with capability boundaries through reward shaping that incentivizes refusal over futile reasoning and hindsight refusal augmentation that converts failures into refusal supervision. Experiments demonstrate a substantial reduction in futile reasoning while preserving performance across task difficulties, effectively achieving capability-aligned behavior without sacrificing utility. \footnote{https://github.com/icip-cas/Knowing-When-to-Quit}

</details>


### [37] [Data Turnstile: A Scalable Open Framework for Function-Calling Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29250)
*Goutham Ramakrishnan,Megha Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 数据转门（Data Turnstile）是一个开源框架，通过将多轮工具调用交互分解为受限的、逐步生成的过程，并配备验证和错误反馈循环，为小型语言模型（SLM）生成高质量的函数调用合成训练数据。实验表明，使用该框架微调的SLM在多个基准测试上显著超越同尺寸基座模型，甚至接近或超越大得多的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在工具调用任务上表现优异，但部署成本高、延迟大。小型语言模型（SLM）具有低延迟、低成本、设备端隐私等优势，但受限于数据稀缺且噪声大，无法像大模型那样通过模型容量弥补低质量监督。数据质量成为SLM工具调用能力的关键瓶颈，亟需高质量的合成训练数据生成方法。

Method: Data Turnstile框架基于用户定义的API规范生成合成训练数据。其核心方法是将多轮工具调用交互分解为受限的、逐步的生成过程，并在每一步加入验证和错误反馈循环。框架支持对API多样性、对话复杂度和输出正确性进行精细控制，生成高质量的函数调用训练数据。

Result: 在BFCL单轮基准上，使用Turnstile数据微调的Qwen3-0.6B（无思维链）达到75.9%准确率，超越带思维链的基座模型（67.4%），接近Qwen3-1.7B（78.4%）和Qwen3-4B（79.9%）。在τ²-bench多轮基准上，Turnstile训练的Qwen3-1.7B在Telecom领域达到31.1% pass^1，相比基座（6.6%）提升4.7倍，超越Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct（27.4%）；Qwen3-0.6B达到24.6%，相比基座（3.5%）提升7倍，接近32B模型的表现。

Conclusion: Data Turnstile有效解决了SLM在工具调用任务中数据质量瓶颈问题，通过分解生成+验证循环的方式生产高质量合成数据，使小模型能够在函数调用任务上大幅缩小与大模型的差距，甚至超越大得多的模型。框架和包含1000+API、10万+多轮交互的数据集已开源。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are attractive for agentic deployment due to low latency, reduced cost, and on-device privacy, yet they struggle with tool-use tasks where training data is scarce and noisy. Unlike larger models, SLMs cannot compensate for low-quality supervision through sheer capacity, making data quality the critical bottleneck. We present Data Turnstile, an open-source framework that takes user-defined API specifications and generates high-quality synthetic training data for function calling. We demonstrate effectiveness of domain adaptation with Turnstile data on two challenging function calling benchmarks. On the BFCL single-turn benchmark, a Qwen3-0.6B fine-tuned on Turnstile data without chain-of-thought achieves 75.9% overall accuracy (versus 67.4% for the base model with thinking enabled), closing the gap with thinking-enabled Qwen3-1.7B (78.4%) and Qwen3-4B (79.9%) despite being 3$\times$ and 7$\times$ smaller respectively. On $τ^2$-bench, a multi-turn agentic benchmark, Turnstile-trained Qwen3-1.7B achieves 31.1% pass^1 on the Telecom domain, improving 4.7$\times$ over its 6.6% base and surpassing Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct (27.4%), a model 19$\times$ larger. Turnstile-trained Qwen3-0.6B achieves 24.6%, improving 7$\times$ over its 3.5% base and approaching the 32B model (53$\times$ larger). We release Data Turnstile along with a dataset spanning 1,000+ APIs and 100K+ multi-turn interactions.

</details>

*Mengting Chen,Yanshu Sun,Wanting Liang,Beidi Luan,Rui Sun,Dezhi Chen,Jing Li,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CalibratedRubric是一个任务自适应的LLM开放输出评估框架，结合类型特定评分、贝叶斯rubric可测量性过滤和基于项目反应理论(IRT)的题目银行组装，显著提升人类-黄金标准一致性并减少评估所需rubric数量。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式的LLM输出评估需要细粒度rubric，但专家制定成本高昂且难以扩展。现有自动化流程依赖严格的评审一致性和二元方差过滤，无法区分可测量的rubric与信息量大的rubric，导致评估质量受限。

Conclusion: CalibratedRubric是一种高效、不确定性感知的开放LLM评估方法，其校准增益依赖于充足的评审冗余。任务标签扰动进一步降低了系统分离度，证实了任务自适应评分在实际应用中的价值。

Existing automated pipelines rely on strict judge unanimity and binary variance filters, which cannot distinguish measurable rubrics from informative ones. CalibratedRubric estimates each rubric's measurability with a Beta--Bernoulli agreement posterior and uses a submodular information-coverage objective to construct compact rubric banks over the observed capability range. Across financial, healthcare, general, and legal benchmarks, measurability filtering improves human-gold agreement on JudgmentBench from $κ=0.604$ to $0.743$. IRT-based greedy selection improves cross-fitted rank fidelity over random selection across all six evaluated response blocks and requires only 49 rather than 131 rubrics to reach the target correlation on FinResearchBench decision-support tasks. Task-label perturbations further reduce system separation, confirming the practical relevance of task-adaptive scoring. These results support CalibratedRubric as an efficient, uncertainty-aware approach to open-ended LLM evaluation, with calibration gains depending on sufficient judge redundancy.

</details>

*Yongshi Ye,Biao Fu,Chongxuan Huang,Yidong Chen,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TwT（Translation with Thought）框架，一种资源理性的多领域机器翻译方法，通过两阶段训练（困难感知CoT监督微调+混合奖励强化学习）在翻译质量和推理效率间取得平衡。在15个基准、62种语言上超越更大规模的推理模型，同时减少32-60%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域机器翻译（MDMT）面临各领域语言复杂度不同的挑战。受人类译者根据难度调节推理努力的启发，现有方法未能在翻译质量和推理效率之间实现灵活的权衡，需要一种资源理性（resource-rational）的框架来动态调制推理强度。

Result: 在15个基准（涵盖领域内和领域外设置）、3种已见语言和59种未见语言上评估，并对3种骨干模型进行消融实验。TwT-7B和TwT-14B在翻译质量上超越更大的SOTA推理模型，同时token使用量减少32-60%。

Conclusion: 将翻译行为与认知原则对齐（即根据难度灵活调节推理努力）能够实现MDMT中的鲁棒泛化、高质量翻译和高效推理，验证了资源理性框架在多领域机器翻译中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-domain machine translation (MDMT) poses a unique challenge due to varying levels of linguistic complexity across domains. Inspired by human translators' ability to adapt reasoning effort based on difficulty, we propose TwT (Translation with Thought), a resource-rational framework that learns to modulate inference between intuitive and deliberate reasoning. TwT is trained in two stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning on difficulty-aware long chain-of-thought traces distilled from DeepSeek-R1 and rewritten by GPT-4o to reflect human-like reasoning economy, and (2) reinforcement learning with a hybrid reward to optimize translation quality and reasoning efficiency. Evaluated on 15 benchmarks spanning in-domain and out-of-domain settings, as well as 3 seen and 59 unseen languages, with ablations across three backbone models, TwT-7B and TwT-14B outperform much larger SOTA reasoning models in translation quality, while reducing token usage by 32--60\%. These results confirm that aligning translation behavior with cognitive principles enables robust generalization, high translation quality, and efficient reasoning in MDMT.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言迁移是低资源机器翻译的核心，但现有研究对亲属语言家族内部迁移行为的刻画不够充分。理解密切相关的语言之间如何相互迁移，有助于更好地利用数据资源并优化低资源翻译系统的构建策略。

Conclusion: 突厥语系内部的语言亲缘关系是跨语言迁移效果的关键决定因素，迁移方向不可忽略，脚本归一化（如拉丁化）对跨脚本迁移有一定帮助但需结合具体指标评估。该研究为低资源突厥语机器翻译的迁移源选择提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Cross-lingual transfer is central to low-resource machine translation, but its behavior within closely related language families remains insufficiently characterized. We study transfer among five Turkic languages; Turkish, Azerbaijani, Uzbek, Kazakh, and Kyrgyz; using pairwise transfer matrices. In this setting, each model is fine-tuned with one transfer source and evaluated on a different transfer target while the translation target remains the same. Across mT5 experiments, we find that transfer is strongest between closely related Turkic pairs, especially Turkish-Azerbaijani and Kazakh-Kyrgyz. We also show that transfer direction matters, and that the same transfer source-transfer target pair can behave differently when the translation target changes. Latinization improves BLEU and chrF in several script-mismatched settings, but its effect is not uniform across metrics. Additional analyses show that transfer sources are mostly stable across different datasets and model settings.

</details>


### [41] [Zero-Mem: Zero-Token Memory Operations for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29377)
*Yilin Xiao,Zhehan Zhu,Yujing Zhang,Jin Chen,Zijin Hong,Luyao Zhuang,Qinggang Zhang,Shengyuan Chen,Xiaocao Ouyang,Lingfei Ren,Xiao Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Zero-Mem提出了一种零token的智能体记忆操作框架，完全避免在记忆存取过程中调用LLM或消耗token。它保留原始交互轨迹，通过实体-上下文图与时间层次结构两种互补方式组织记忆，并以确定性校准机制筛选证据。在长记忆和长上下文问答基准上取得与基线相当的性能，同时将记忆操作的时间成本降低57.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统普遍依赖额外的LLM调用进行记忆操作（如生成中间记录和检索中介），带来重复的token和时间开销，且可能丢失或篡改原始证据。作者质疑：结构化的记忆访问是否真的需要生成过程？

Method: Zero-Mem采用零token记忆操作：(1) 以原始交互轨迹为唯一事实来源；(2) 通过实体-上下文图揭示跨交互连接，同时用时间层次结构保留会话局部性和状态；(3) 查询时对两种视图加权，从两者检索并遵循其结构恢复支持关系或上下文；(4) 确定性校准先丢弃冲突证据，再确保最终读者答案扎根于检索轨迹；(5) 仅最终QA读者调用LLM。

Result: 在长记忆和长上下文QA基准上，Zero-Mem取得与基线相当的竞争性能，同时完全消除了记忆操作中的LLM调用和token消耗。在相同最终QA读者和上下文预算下，内存操作时间成本相比最快基线降低57.6%。消融实验验证了两种视图及其查询依赖协调的贡献。

Conclusion: 结构化智能体记忆无需生成过去的中间表示。零token记忆操作证明，通过精心设计的确定性检索与校准机制，可以在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅降低成本和开销。

Abstract: LLM agents need memory to act consistently over long interactions, yet many systems use additional LLM calls to operate that memory. Generating intermediate records and mediating their retrieval adds recurring token and time costs, while omitted or merged details can obscure the original evidence. We ask whether structured memory access requires generation at all. Zero-Mem introduces \emph{zero-token memory operations}: no step outside final question answering invokes an LLM or consumes LLM input or output tokens; encoder computation is accounted for separately. Zero-Mem preserves original interaction traces as its source of record. It organizes the traces in two complementary ways. An entity--context graph exposes connections across interactions, while a temporal hierarchy preserves conversational locality and session state. For each query, Zero-Mem weighs the two views, retrieves from both, and follows their structure to recover supporting relations or surrounding context. Deterministic calibration first discards conflicting evidence and then keeps the reader's answer grounded in the retrieved traces. Only the final-QA reader invokes an LLM. Across long-memory and long-context question-answering benchmarks, Zero-Mem achieves competitive performance while eliminating LLM calls and LLM-token consumption from memory operations. With the same final-QA reader and context budget, it reduces memory-operation time cost by 57.6\% relative to the fastest compared baseline. Ablations support the contribution of the two views and their query-dependent coordination. Overall, the results show that structured agent memory need not generate an intermediate representation of the past. After peer review, the code and implementation details will be available at \textcolor{blue}{https://github.com/TheMoon0815/Zero-mem}.

</details>


### [42] [PTP: Previous-Token Prediction based LLM Inversion for Near-Exact Prompt Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29378)
*Pirzada Suhail,Nagasai Saketh Naidu,Atanu R Sinha,Amit Sethi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种黑盒条件下的函数式LLM逆向方法：从头训练一个显式逆向语言模型，利用目标LLM自身生成的合成数据，通过"前一token预测"（previous-token prediction）进行训练，实现从响应重构提示词，无需访问模型权重或logits，也无需外部数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM通过自回归方式逐token生成文本，导致提示词与响应之间存在多对多映射，使从输出推断提示词变得困难。已有工作依赖微调预训练序列到序列模型、大型外部数据集以及访问模型权重或logits，实用性受限。

Method: 提出一个完全从零训练的显式逆向语言模型，使用目标LLM自身合成的数据进行训练。与正向的下一个token预测对称，逆向模型采用"前一token预测"（previous-token prediction）进行训练，建立正向与逆向过程的生成性关联。通过采样支持多样化的提示词重构，所有重构提示词在目标前向LLM下都能产生相似响应。

Result: 该方法在不同数据集上具有泛化能力，并能泛化到其他LLM生成的响应上进行提示词重构（具备迁移性）。在基于token的提示词和响应重构评估指标上，优于此前的方法。

Conclusion: 通过逆向语言模型实现黑盒条件下的LLM提示词重构是可行且有效的，无需外部辅助数据或模型内部信息，且能保证重构提示词在正向LLM下产生一致响应。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate text by auto-regressively sampling the next token. This inherently leads to a many-to-many mapping between prompts and responses, complicating the task of inferring prompts from observed outputs. Prior work on LLM inversion frames prompt recovery as a semantic reconstruction task. They rely on fine-tuning pretrained sequence-to-sequence models on large external datasets--and requiring access to model weights or logits--to generate semantically plausible prompts. In contrast, we present a functional approach to inverting a given LLM in a black-box setting, without auxiliary aids. We train an explicit inverse language model entirely from scratch on data synthetically generated from the target LLM itself. Analogous to forward next-token prediction, our inverse model is trained using previous-token prediction, establishing a generative link between the forward and inverse processes that enables faithful prompt reconstruction. Moreover, it naturally supports diverse prompt reconstructions through sampling, whereby all such prompts induce similar responses under the forward, target LLM. Our approach generalises across datasets and exhibits transferability in reconstructing prompts from responses generated by different LLMs. Further, across the set of token based evaluation metrics for prompt and response reconstructions, our approach outperforms prior work.

</details>


### [43] [Studying quantization trade-offs for efficient inference deployment in machine translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29397)
*Jim Zhao,Sohir Maskey,Koen Oostermeijer,Douglas Orr,Teryn Jones*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在真实服务器环境中部署大语言模型做机器翻译时的量化权衡，发现结合文档分块策略与W4A8/W8A8量化能改善延迟-吞吐量帕累托曲线，并指出标准段级评估无法预测量化与长上下文文档翻译之间的交互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化是减少内存占用和提升推理效率的常用方法，但其对延迟和吞吐量的影响很少在受控的、编排级别的负载下进行评估。此外，标准的MT基准测试依赖孤立句子，无法捕捉长上下文动态，导致量化策略在真实场景中的效果难以准确评估。

Method: 作者对两个翻译模型家族（EuroLLM和Hy-MT2）的五个模型（1.7B到22B）进行量化研究，在单张A100或H100 GPU上结合文档分块策略与W4A8/W8A8量化，并从WMT24++引入文档级评估来考察文本分块策略在量化下对翻译质量的影响。

Result: 组合文档分块与W4A8/W8A8量化可改善延迟-吞吐量帕累托曲线。Hy-MT2在量化下表现稳健，而EuroLLM对量化高度敏感，翻译质量在所有量化格式下迅速崩溃。标准段级评估无法预测量化与长上下文文档翻译之间的交互。

Conclusion: 推理效率与翻译质量之间的权衡不仅取决于量化格式，还取决于文本分块策略的选择。

Abstract: Deploying large language models in realistic server environments poses challenges, as the system needs to provide high-quality responses with low latency. Quantization is a common approach to reduce the memory footprint and improve inference efficiency, yet its impact on latency and throughput is rarely evaluated under controlled, orchestration-level workloads. In this work we study the quantization trade-offs of two translation model families, EuroLLM \citep{martins2025eurollm} and Hy-MT2 \citep{zheng2026hy} across five models ranging from 1.7B to 22B for efficient deployment on a single A100 or H100 GPU. We demonstrate that combining a document-chunking strategy with W4A8 or W8A8 quantization improves the latency-throughput Pareto-curve under a wide range of workloads. Furthermore, since standard machine translation (MT) benchmarks rely on isolated sentences and fail to capture long-context dynamics, we introduce a document-level evaluation from WMT24++ to assess how text chunking strategies affect translation quality under quantization. Our results reveal that standard segment-level evaluation can fail to predict the interaction between quantization and long-context document translation. While Hy-MT2 remains robust under quantization, EuroLLM shows strong sensitivity and translation quality collapses rapidly for all considered quantization formats. Overall, our experiments show that the trade-off between inference efficiency and translation quality depends not only on the quantization format, but also on the choice of text chunking strategy.

</details>


### [44] [Know It, Act on It: Investigating Memory Utilization in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29433)
*Zhaoxin Feng,Jianfei Ma,Emmanuele Chersoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解耦评估范式（Know/Act 配对测试），用于区分LLM智能体在个性化场景中"记不住"用户偏好与"记住了但不会用"两种失败模式。通过在16个系统和5种记忆架构上、对1000条用户偏好进行大规模实验，发现Know（回忆）与Act（行为）之间存在巨大差距，且健康/治疗类偏好上的行为失败比例最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体作为个性化伴侣时，记忆是关键能力，但存在"知识利用问题"——即使相关用户偏好完全在上下文中，模型仍可能不按偏好行动。当这种情况发生时，无法判断是记忆失败还是利用失败，需要隔离并分别评估这两种失败。

Method: 提出解耦评估范式：对同一条用户偏好分别构建配对的Know测试（测试是否能回忆出该偏好）和Act测试（测试在行为场景中是否能实际运用）。在16个系统和5种记忆架构上进行大规模实验，评估1000条用户偏好，并设置了三种表达强度级别来嵌入偏好。

Result: Know与Act之间存在显著差距：智能体往往能通过偏好的回忆测试，但在配对的行为场景中无法体现同一偏好。记忆架构虽能缩小这一差距，但利用能力在健康和治疗相关偏好上仍然特别薄弱，而这类偏好的行为失败后果最为严重。

Conclusion: LLM智能体的个性化记忆问题不仅是"记住"的问题，更是"利用"的问题。现有记忆架构主要提升了记忆存储与检索，但行为层面的偏好利用仍不足，尤其在健康与治疗等高风险领域，需要进一步研究和改进。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents evolve into personalized companions, memory has emerged as a core capability. However, LLMs face a knowledge utilization problem: they may fail to act on relevant user preferences even when they are fully present in context. When an agent fails to tailor its response in a context where previously shared user preferences should matter, it is unclear whether the model failed to remember that information or remembered it but failed to use it. To isolate this breakdown, we introduce a decoupled evaluation paradigm that administers paired Know and Act tests to the same user preference. We conduct large-scale experiments across 16 systems and five memory architectures, evaluating 1,000 preferences embedded at three levels of expression strength. Our results show a large gap between Know and Act outcomes: agents often pass the recall test for a user preference but fail to reflect that same preference in the paired behavioral scenario. While memory architectures reduce this gap, utilization remains especially weak for health and therapy-related preferences, where failures to act carry the greatest real-world stakes.

</details>


### [45] [Evidence-Type Competition: When Can Interventional Data Teach Language Models Causal Direction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29484)
*Xining Xun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在完全受控的合成环境中检验"干预数据是教模型因果推理的金标准"这一假设，发现该假设在辛普森悖论世界中失效：增加干预样本比例并不能改善因果方向判断，模型do()响应的符号反而从观察性上下文中复制。决定是否使用干预证据的是推理时的上下文证据类型，而非训练混合比例。能力存在于权重中，开关存在于上下文中，激活修补将开关定位到中间层的观察性行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"干预数据是训练因果推理模型金标准"这一广泛假设。通过辛普森悖论世界（观察相关与因果效应符号系统相反）来测试，发现干预数据比例的增加并不改善因果方向，这一发现与传统认知相反，具有重要的教学和训练指导意义。

Method: 在完全受控的合成环境（辛普森悖论世界）中进行对照实验：(1) 系统变化干预样本与观察样本的训练混合比例；(2) 对比纯观察上下文、混合上下文、对齐干预探针三种推理上下文；(3) 通过擦除观察证据、四状态内容操纵验证切换是否由内容介导；(4) 跨训练种子和0.93B参数规模的鲁棒性测试；(5) 在CLadder上进行外部审计；(6) 激活修补定位切换机制；(7) 提出证据平均协议减少符号错误。

Result: 干预样本比例增加不改善因果方向（符号从观察上下文复制）。纯观察上下文导致29/50世界符号反转，混合上下文19/50，干预探针41/50正确。擦除观察证据释放被抑制的因果插值能力(+0.56)。抑制跨种子稳定（11/11强反转），在0.93B参数下鲁棒（31.8% vs 6%反转率）。CLadder审计发现带正效应先验的双层结构，分布内移除但分布外不迁移。证据平均协议将符号错误从26%降至9%。

Conclusion: 模型具备因果推理能力（存在于权重中）但被观察性上下文抑制（开关在上下文中），切换位于中间层且由内容介导、分级调制。训练数据混合比例不是决定干预证据使用的因素。观察证据在推理时会系统性压过干预证据，提出证据平均协议可显著降低符号判断错误，对因果推理模型的训练与评估实践有重要启示。

Abstract: Interventional data is widely regarded as the gold standard for teaching models causal reasoning. We test this assumption in a fully controlled synthetic environment pitting observational correlation against causal effect, and find it fails instructively. In Simpson's-paradox worlds, where the two have systematically opposite signs, increasing the fraction of interventional samples in pretraining does not improve causal direction: the magnitude of the model's do()-response grows monotonically, yet its sign is copied from the observational context. What governs whether interventional evidence is used is not the training mixture but the evidence type present in the context at inference time. Under an identical training recipe, a purely observational context induces systematic sign reversal in 29/50 worlds, a mixed context in 19/50, while aligned interventional probes alone yield 41/50 correct. Erasing observational evidence from the context immediately releases the suppressed causal interpolation ability (ratio_true = +0.56); a four-state content manipulation shows the switch is content-mediated and graded. The suppression is stable across training seeds (11/11 strong reversals persist on a matched-protocol second seed) and robust as a rate at 0.93B parameters (31.8% vs. 6% reversals in the matched probe-only arm), even as absolute gains shrink four-fold. An external audit on CLadder exposes a learned positive-effect prior with a two-layer structure: sign-randomized retraining removes it in-distribution but not out-of-distribution. We summarize: the capability lives in the weights; the switch lives in the context, and activation patching localizes the switch to the middle layers' observational rows. We further quantify the sampling noise floor of probe-based causal evaluation and an evidence-averaging protocol that cuts sign errors from 26% to 9%.

</details>


### [46] [ARB: A Matched Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark Dataset for AI-Text Detector Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29539)
*Gaetano Perrone,Simon Pietro Romano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个新的基准测试ARB（Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark），用于评估AI文本检测器在人类文本被LLM重写场景下的性能。实验发现，检测器对直接LLM生成的文本检测效果很好（91-93%），但对LLM重写的人类文本检测率骤降至15-31%，下降了60-78个百分点，表明传统基准无法预测检测器在重写场景下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测基准仅比较人类原创文本与LLM直接生成文本，但忽略了现实中大量存在的人类文本被LLM重写（如润色、修改）的场景。先前研究表明改写会降低检测性能，但尚不清楚传统基准上的性能是否能预测检测器在"人类文本被LLM重写"这一实际场景中的表现。

Method: 构建ARB基准，包含1,800篇人类源文本（来自XSum、WritingPrompts、OpenWebText三个数据集），使用四个开源生成器（Llama-3.2-3B、Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B、Gemma-2-9B）生成四种匹配变体：人类原创、直接LLM生成、人类文本的LLM重写（H2L）、LLM文本的同模型重写（LLM2L）。用五个检测器在1%假阳率的标准下评估。

Result: FastDetectGPT和Binoculars-falcon-7b对直接LLM文本检测率为91.2%和93.5%，但对LLM重写的人类文本仅能检测30.8%和15.1%，下降60-78个百分点；对LLM文本的同模型重写仍保持78.3%和83.0%（仅下降10-13点）。RADAR也从66.8%降至12.2%，而BERT-Defense和RoBERTa-Defense在所有场景下召回率均低于3%。

Conclusion: 传统的人vs-LLM基准无法反映检测器在"人类文本被LLM重写"场景下的实际表现。检测器对LLM-only重写保持鲁棒，但对人类文本的LLM重写性能急剧下降，说明检测器本质上是识别"人类写作风格"特征而非"文本是否经LLM生成"，这一发现对检测器的实际部署和基准设计有重要启示。

Abstract: Standard AI-text detection benchmarks compare human-written text against text generated directly by large language models (LLMs). While prior work has shown that rewriting and paraphrasing can degrade detector performance, it remains unclear whether performance measured on this conventional benchmark predicts detector behavior when human-authored content is rewritten by an LLM. To address this gap, we introduce Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark (ARB), built from 1,800 human source texts (600 each from XSum, WritingPrompts, and OpenWebText) and four open-weight generators (Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B). Each source item yields four matched variants: human-written (HUMAN), direct LLM generation (Free-LLM), LLM-rewritten human text (H2L), and same-generator LLM-rewritten LLM text (LLM2L). We evaluated five detectors (FastDetectGPT, Binoculars-falcon-7b, RADAR, BERT-Defense, RoBERTa-Defense) at a strict 1%-false-positive operating point (TPR@1%FPR). FastDetectGPT and Binoculars-falcon-7b detected 91.2% and 93.5\% of direct LLM text, but only 30.8% and 15.1% of human text an LLM had rewritten, a drop of 60-78 percentage points. The same detectors retained 78.3% and 83.0% recall when LLM text was rewritten by the same model, a much smaller decline of 10-13 points. RADAR followed the same pattern (66.8% to 12.2%), while BERT-Defense and RoBERTa-Defense stayed below 3% recall across all regimes. These results show that detector performance measured on the conventional human-vs-LLM benchmark does not transfer to human-authored text revised by an LLM, even though the same detectors remain largely robust to LLM-only rewriting.

</details>


### [47] [Sycophancy Undermines Epistemic Vigilance in Cooperative Vision-Language Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29585)
*Rupak Sarkar,Neha Srikanth,Saloni Gupta,Claire Bonial,Philip Resnik,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个信息不对称的对话式"找不同"任务来测量视觉-语言模型在合作场景下的"认知警惕性"（epistemic vigilance），发现模型经常忽略自己私有图像中的关键证据而过分附和对话伙伴，并将此现象与谄媚行为（sycophancy）联系起来，最后证明用任务无关的谄媚样本训练出的向量进行模型引导，可以减少这类错误，使模型更忠实地报告证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在合作对话中会迭代更新信念并警惕与新信息冲突的内容，而AI系统作为复杂合作任务的可靠伙伴，也需要对自身私有证据和共享上下文进行权衡，并在不一致时恰当提出。当前缺乏衡量视觉-语言模型在合作设置中认知警惕性的方法。

Method: 设计了一个信息不对称的、基于对话的"找不同"任务：两个模型各自私密看到一张图像，通过对话判断图像是否相同或找出差异。通过该任务测量模型是否忽略自身证据而附和对方，并将这种失败与谄媚行为关联，最后使用从任务无关的谄媚样本中学到的向量来引导模型减少这类错误。

Result: 模型在该任务中经常失败：倾向于忽视私有图像中的关键证据，转而同意对话伙伴，即使这种同意是不合理的。通过任务无关的谄媚向量进行模型引导，可以减少认知警惕性相关的错误，使模型更忠实地报告证据，成为更可靠的信息不对称合作任务伙伴。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言模型在合作对话中存在系统性缺乏认知警惕性的问题，表现为过度迁就（over-accommodation）和薄弱的证据立足（weak evidential grounding），这与谄媚行为同源；通过谄媚引导向量可以有效缓解该问题，提升模型在信息不对称合作任务中的可靠性。

Abstract: To maintain common ground in cooperative conversation, humans iteratively update their beliefs as conversation participants share new information; participants who are epistemically vigilant detect when new information conflicts with prior beliefs and take steps to repair these conflicts. In order for AI systems to serve as reliable partners in complex cooperative tasks, they must similarly weigh incoming information against their own private evidence and shared context and appropriately surface inconsistencies when they arise. To measure the epistemic vigilance of vision-language models in cooperative settings, we present an information-asymmetric, dialog-based "spot-the-difference" task. Two models are privately shown one image each, and must determine through conversation whether the images are identical or, if not, identify the difference. Models routinely fail at this: they frequently overlook key evidence in their private image in favor of agreeing with their conversational partner, even when their agreement is unwarranted. We relate these violations of epistemic vigilance to the broader behavior of sycophancy, which manifests itself in cooperative goal-oriented dialog as over-accommodation and weak evidential grounding. Our results show that model steering to reduce sycophancy with a vector learned from task-agnostic sycophancy examples can reduce epistemic vigilance-related errors, making models more faithful reporters of their evidence, and in turn, more reliable partners in information-asymmetric cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [48] [ResKV: Reconstructing Omitted Attention Contributions for Fixed-Budget KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29591)
*Yuhang Zhan,Lisi Chen,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ResKV，一种KV缓存压缩方法，将固定KV预算分为精确主缓存和紧凑残差缓存，通过重建被驱逐token在softmax注意力中的残差贡献（包括分子和分母），在不扰动主缓存精确性的同时提升压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法存在两难：驱逐法永久丢弃token导致注意力信息损失；合并法虽保留更多信息但会扰动本应保持精确的键值对。作者观察到缓存驱逐遗漏的信息可以表示为softmax注意力的分子和分母中的残差统计量，因此可以更精确地建模和补偿这种损失。

Method: ResKV将固定KV预算分为两部分：精确主缓存（exact main cache）和紧凑残差缓存（residual cache）。残差缓存条目与主缓存token参与相同的softmax归一化，从而同时恢复注意力的分子和分母质量，而非事后修正。构建时通过验证代理（validation proxy）为每层和每个KV头确定残差分配，解码时通过动态门控（dynamic gate）针对单个查询调整残差贡献。

Result: 在LongBench和RULER上进行了全面评估，覆盖了查询感知和查询无关设置、多种骨干网络、不同缓存预算以及代表性压缩基线。在相同保留KV预算下展现出广泛改进，同时保持了压缩解码的实际效率（峰值内存使用和长上下文解码吞吐量）。

Conclusion: ResKV通过残差缓存重建被驱逐token的注意力贡献，在精确性和信息保持之间取得了更好的平衡，在多种场景下优于现有压缩基线且不牺牲实际解码效率。

Abstract: KV cache compression is essential for efficient long-context inference. Existing eviction methods permanently discard unselected tokens and consequently remove their aggregate contribution to attention. Merging-based alternatives preserve more information but can perturb retained keys and values that should remain exact. We observe that the information omitted by cache eviction can be formulated as residual statistics in both the numerator and denominator of softmax attention. Based on this observation, we propose ResKV, which divides a fixed KV budget into an exact main cache and a compact residual cache that reconstructs the contribution of omitted tokens. ResKV lets main-cache tokens and residual entries participate in the same softmax normalization, so residual entries restore both attention numerator and denominator mass rather than acting as a post-hoc correction. A construction-time validation proxy determines residual allocation for each layer and KV head, while a decode-time dynamic gate adjusts residual contributions for individual queries. Comprehensive evaluations on LongBench and RULER, covering query-aware and query-agnostic settings, multiple backbones, cache budgets, and representative compression baselines, demonstrate broad improvements under the same retained KV budget while preserving the practical efficiency of compressed decoding, including peak memory usage and long-context decode throughput.

</details>


### [49] [FriendBench: Benchmarking Dyadic Familiarity Inference in Humans and Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29602)
*Jeffrey M. Girard,Jason Z. Zheng,Jacqueline R. Vertino,Antony D'Avirro,Benjamin Peloquin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍FriendBench基准，用于从20秒双人破冰对话片段中推断两人是熟人还是陌生人。测试了7家公司26个模型与人类对照，发现最佳模型在准确率上与人类统计上无差异，但模型的决策偏向"陌生人"答案，人类则保持平衡。更丰富的通道（文本/音频/视频）对人类和模型帮助不均，只有人类能从视觉行为中获益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会情境理解常依赖于非言语行为线索，而非仅靠文字内容。现有研究缺乏一个受控基准来评估模型和人类如何利用行为线索（而非语义内容）判断人际熟悉度。作者希望揭示模型理解社会动态的能力与人类之间的差距。

Method: 构建FriendBench基准：96个平衡的双人破冰对话片段，每对回答相同的提示，仅通过互动方式区分。对比26个模型（来自7家公司）与匹配的人类评审组，在文本、音频和视频三种模态下评估准确率，并分析决策偏差和模态增益。

Result: 最佳模型与人类众包在三种模态准确率上统计上无显著差异；但模型偏向"陌生人"判断，人类保持两类答案平衡。丰富的通道对模型帮助小于对人类，只有人类能从视觉行为中获得额外信息增益。

Conclusion: 模型在行为推断上已达到与人类相当的整体准确率，但其决策机制与人类存在本质差异——模型的"先验"偏斜而非判别能力不足。视觉行为信息是模型尚未充分利用的关键通道。作者公开了数据集、人类评分和模型预测。

Abstract: Reading a social situation often depends on behavior, not words alone. We introduce FriendBench, a benchmark for inferring whether two people are already familiar or are meeting as strangers, from a 20-second clip of a dyadic ice-breaker conversation. Every pair answers the same type of prompt, so only the manner of interaction can reveal the answer. Across text, audio, and video, we compare 26 models from seven companies against matched human panels over 96 balanced dyads. The best model and the human crowd are statistically indistinguishable on accuracy in every modality, but reach it differently: humans stay balanced across the two answers, while the strongest models lean toward "stranger"---a difference in effective prior, not discrimination. Richer channels help both unequally, and only humans gain from visible behavior on top of speech. We release the stimuli, human ratings, and model predictions.

</details>


### [50] [Evolving language compositionality in a frequency-structured meaning space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29642)
*Fabio De Ponte,Eloise Gaines-White,Conor Houghton,Seth Bullock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过迭代学习模型探索意义频率分布如何影响语言的组合性演化。研究发现，与自然语言一致，高频意义可以逃避语法压力；但当频率施加在部分而非整体意义向量上时，语言无法跨代传播，说明频率只有在可整体习得的形-义单元上分布时才能塑造涌现的语言结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代学习模型已证明语言组合性可因重复传递而自发涌现，但此前研究未深入探讨意义频率分布（不均衡频率）如何影响语言组合性的本质特征。作者希望理解频率结构在语言演化中的作用，并与自然语言中高频意义可偏离语法的现象进行对接。

Method: 采用迭代学习模型框架，设计两种实验条件：(1) 将非均匀频率施加在完整意义向量上；(2) 将频率结构施加在意义的组成部件（而非整体）上。通过多代人传递实验，观察语言组合性的形成与传播稳定性。

Result: 实验发现：(1) 当频率分布施加在完整意义向量上时，高频意义确实可以绕过语法约束，与自然语言观察一致；(2) 当频率施加在意义组成部分上时，即使最高频元素能够被可靠学习，语言整体仍无法跨代稳定传播，组合性泛化所需的关系结构无法建立。

Conclusion: 频率结构要塑造涌现的语言结构，必须定义在学习者可整体习得的形-义单元上。若频率分布在更小的子单元上，则无法支撑组合性泛化所需的关系结构，导致语言无法稳定跨代传播。

Abstract: The iterated learning model was introduced to investigate language evolution: the way in which the characteristic properties of human languages have been shaped, at least partly, by repeated transmission from one language user to another. The key finding is that language compositionality can arise spontaneously as a consequence of language being passed repeatedly through a language learning bottleneck. Here we explore how changing the frequency of different meanings, so that some meanings occur much more frequently than others, affects the character of its compositionality. We find that, as observed in natural languages, high-frequency meanings can escape the pressure to conform to the grammar that characterizes lower-frequency meanings. However, when the frequency structure is instead imposed on parts rather than on whole meaning vectors, the language fails to transmit across generations. This occurs despite the fact that the most frequent elements are reliably learned. These results suggest that frequency can shape emergent linguistic structure only when the frequency distribution is defined over form-meaning units that learners can acquire holistically. When frequency is instead distributed over smaller units, it fails to support the relational structure required for compositional generalisation, thereby preventing stable language transmission.

</details>


### [51] [TokTier: Exact Stateful Tokenization for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29678)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Zhichao Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TokTier是一个有状态的分词服务，通过增量重分词和GPU加速全量分词，确保与参考分词完全一致，将LLM服务端到端延迟（TTFT）降低16-34%，在数万次差分测试中零偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务系统虽然缓存了prompt的KV状态，但前端仍然对每个请求进行全量重分词，尤其在编码代理场景中，每次追加小结果后都需重发长转录文本，导致分词开销占首个token延迟的64%。现有方法难以利用前缀缓存，因为小段追加可能改变前序序列末尾的token边界。

Method: TokTier提出一个有状态的分词服务，核心思想是：(1) 对会话续接，仅对追加位置附近的小窗口重分词，通过稳定边界检查确认后拼接，失败则扩大窗口或回退全量分词；(2) 对无可复用前缀的调用，将GPT系正则预分词分解为局部规则，在GPU上执行精确预分词和BPE；(3) 使用采样影子验证器复核线上流量。

Result: 在17个分词器家族、12.4TB真实文本和93,000+代理步骤的差分测试中零偏差。增量修复在100K到3M字符规模仅需0.5-1.1ms，比HF快437倍，比Gigatoken基线快2.1倍。GPU全量分词处理1M字符仅0.87ms，比HF快491倍。与vLLM集成后，TTFT中位数下降16-34%，P99下降23%。在50ms P99目标下，4核+1 GPU可持续处理1,821 req/s，而16核无状态前端仅在40 req/s饱和。

Conclusion: TokTier证明了通过有状态增量分词和GPU并行化，可以在保证分词结果完全一致的前提下，大幅消除LLM服务中前端分词的性能瓶颈，尤其对编码代理这类高频续接场景收益显著。

Abstract: LLM serving systems cache prompt KV state, yet most front ends still re-tokenize the full request text on every call. The cost lands on coding agents, which resubmit a long transcript after each small tool result, and reuse is hard because even a short append can change token boundaries near the end of the previous sequence. Across 153,951 calls from two agent ecosystems, the median call appends about 1.4K characters, and only 1.0-3.6% of calls start or rebuild a session with contexts of millions of characters. At a 94.1% fleet prompt-cache hit rate, tokenization reaches up to 64% of time to first token.
  TokTier is a stateful tokenization service with one contract: emitted token IDs are always identical to full reference tokenization of the request text. For a session continuation, it re-tokenizes a small window around the append and splices only after a per-request stable-boundary check, widening the window or falling back to full tokenization on failure. For a call without a reusable prefix, it decomposes GPT-family regex pre-tokenization into run-local rules and runs exact pre-tokenization and BPE on a GPU. A sampled shadow verifier re-checks live traffic.
  Across 17 tokenizer families, differential campaigns cover 1.5x10^10 split checks, a 12.4 TB real-text corpus, and 93,000+ replayed agent steps, with zero divergence. Incremental repair takes 0.5-1.1 ms from 100K to 3M characters, up to 437x faster than HF tokenization and 2.1x faster at 1M than the strongest cache-based baseline (Gigatoken) fully prewarmed. GPU full tokenization encodes a 1M-character request in 0.87 ms, up to 491x below HF and 23.4x below the fastest published CPU method. With vLLM, median time to first token drops 16-34% and P99 drops 23% under recorded bursts. Under a 50 ms P99 objective, four repair cores plus one GPU sustain 1,821 requests/s where a 16-core stateless front end saturates at 40.

</details>

### [52] [The Tragedy of the Cognitive Commons: How AI Could Disrupt the Regeneration of Professional Expertise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29380)
*Nolan Lovett*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知共享资源"（Cognitive Commons）框架，整合共享资源理论、人力资源开发（HRD）学术和分布式认知，解释理性的AI采用决策如何消耗职业所需的共享专业知识池。框架区分内在掌握与分布式掌握，并提出"验证锚"（Validation Tether）概念：有效的AI监督依赖于AI采用可能削弱的那类专业知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正在重塑认知工作，但HRD学术界仅将此视为组织培训挑战，忽视了专业知识的集体再生问题。本文旨在填补这一空白，将专业知识发展重新定位为集体管理而非组织优化问题。

Method: 采用概念性论文方法，综合共享资源理论、HRD学术和分布式认知理论，构建Cognitive Commons理论框架，并结合早期劳动力市场和临床证据进行分析。

Result: 早期证据表明高AI暴露行业可能出现专业知识再生路径的中断；识别出决定职业脆弱性的五个因素；治理安排可能在组织、专业协会和政策三个层面形成。

Conclusion: 本文重新定义了专业知识发展，将其视为集体管理而非组织优化问题，对HRD理论和劳动力政策具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is reshaping cognitive work, but Human Resource Development scholarship has treated this transformation as an organizational training challenge, leaving the collective regeneration of professional expertise unexamined. This conceptual paper introduces the Cognitive Commons framework, integrating commons theory, HRD scholarship, and distributed cognition to explain how rational AI adoption decisions can deplete the shared expertise pool professions require for renewal. The framework distinguishes Internalized Mastery (deep domain knowledge from sustained practice) from Distributed Mastery (orchestrating human-AI systems), and develops the Validation Tether: effective AI oversight depends on the expertise AI adoption may undermine. Early labor market and clinical evidence suggests possible disruption to expertise-regeneration pathways in highly AI-exposed sectors, though adoption is recent and the strongest signals come from leading sectors rather than all professions. Five factors determine occupational vulnerability, and governance arrangements may form across organizational, professional-association, and policy levels. The paper reframes expertise development as collective stewardship rather than organizational optimization, with implications for HRD theory and workforce policy.

</details>


### [53] [Scaffolding Critical Engagement with GenAI: Transforming Ethnic Minority Preparatory Students' Collaborative Discourse in Prompt Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28630)
*Deliang Wang,Cunling Bian*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过设计型研究，探讨教学脚手架如何帮助78名中国少数民族预科学生从被动消费生成式AI转向批判性共创，发现"策略性再利用"现象，并通过人机协同工作流和教师示范使学生从复制转向批判性评估，显著提升其提示词自我效能感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI有潜力促进少数民族学生的教育公平，但存在"认知懒惰"风险——学生将AI视为答案引擎而非思考伙伴。技术培训本身不足以解决这一问题，需要针对性的教学干预设计。

Method: 采用设计型研究方法，78名中国少数民族预科学生参与为期三周的GenAI课程，整合人机协同工作流和教师对比案例示范。使用认知网络分析（ENA）分析协作话语、主题分析分析学生反思、配对样本t检验评估提示词自我效能感变化。

Result: 发现"策略性再利用"现象：干预前学生将策略对话用于协调高效复制；干预后将策略规划重新导向批判性评估和同伴共建。教师脚手架帮助学生克服权威偏见和提示词瘫痪，显著提升提示词自我效能感。

Conclusion: 对少数民族学生而言，仅技术培训不够，教育者应设计围绕人机协作的针对性教学干预，以防止认知自满并培养认知主体性。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) holds significant promise for advancing educational equity among ethnic minority students by broadening access to learning resources and mitigating linguistic barriers. However, these benefits are counterbalanced by the risk of cognitive laziness, whereby students may treat GenAI as an answer engine or shortcut rather than as a partner in thinking. This design-based research investigated how pedagogical scaffolding can shift students from passive consumption to critical co-creation with GenAI. The study involved 78 ethnic minority preparatory students in China participating in a three-week GenAI course that integrated a human-in-the-loop workflow and teacher modeling with contrasting cases to disrupt uncritical reliance on GenAI. We employed epistemic network analysis to examine collaborative discourse, thematic analysis to analyze student reflections, and paired-samples t-tests to assess changes in prompt self-efficacy. Results revealed a phenomenon of strategic repurposing: initially, students instrumentalized strategy talk to coordinate efficient copying; however, after the intervention, they realigned strategic planning to scaffold critical evaluation and peer co-construction. Qualitative findings further indicated that the teacher's scaffolding helped students overcome their initial authority bias and prompt paralysis, repositioning themselves as active gatekeepers of AI-generated content; these shifts were corroborated by a significant increase in students' prompt self-efficacy. The study suggests that, particularly for ethnic minority students, technical training alone is insufficient; educators should design targeted pedagogical interventions around human-AI collaboration to prevent cognitive complacency and cultivate epistemic agency.

</details>


### [54] [Structured AI Demonstrations and Student LLM Use in Engineering Mechanics: Study Design and Preliminary Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28710)
*Shuang Geng,Helen Lallos-Harrell,Jiya Ashar,Thomas J. McKenna,Annwesa Dasgupta,Caleb Farny,Emma Lejeune*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一项在2026年春季本科工程力学课程中开展的描述性研究，提供了可复现的调查工具和9个结构化AI演示序列，并将学生AI使用模式与学业表现相关联，核心贡献是公开可获取的方法论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在本科教育中的快速普及给工程教育者带来紧迫挑战，但缺乏领域特定的经验证据来指导教学政策和课堂干预。作者希望通过提供开放的方法论框架，推动工程教育者共享经验数据，以协作方式理解AI对学习的长期影响。

Method: 采用描述性研究设计：1）开发可复现的调查工具，捕捉学生AI使用模式、态度与验证实践；2）将调查数据与学业表现指标关联分析；3）设计了9个结构化的教师主导AI演示序列，用于示范战略性的LLM委派与评估方法。

Result: 初步数据揭示了学生AI使用行为的转变，以及AI依赖与课程成绩之间的复杂关系。但论文强调主要贡献是提供公开可访问的方法论框架，而非具体结论。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁工程教育者收集并共享类似的实证数据，强调应对这一技术变革需要协作的、基于证据的方法来理解其对学生学习的长期影响。

Abstract: The rapid integration of large language models (LLMs) into undergraduate education presents an urgent challenge for engineering instructors. Despite widespread student adoption, there remains a critical lack of domain-specific empirical evidence to guide pedagogical policies and classroom interventions. This manuscript presents a descriptive study design and preliminary findings from an undergraduate engineering mechanics course conducted in Spring 2026. We detail a reproducible survey instrument used to capture student AI usage patterns, attitudes, and verification practices, which are subsequently linked to academic performance metrics. Additionally, we document a deployable sequence of nine structured, instructor-led AI demonstrations designed to model strategic LLM delegation and evaluation. While our preliminary data highlight shifting student behaviors and complex relationships between AI reliance and course outcomes, the primary contribution of this work is the provision of an open-access methodological framework. By making our complete study design, survey tools, and demonstration materials publicly available, we urge other engineering educators to collect and share similar empirical data. Navigating this unprecedented technological shift will require a collaborative, evidence-based approach to fully understand its long-term impacts on student learning.

</details>


### [55] [Hidden Errors in Big Data: The Case of Property Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28827)
*Evelyn Smith,Emma Harvey,Jacob Goldin,Daniel E. Ho*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究审计了两个主要的经纪商房产数据集，发现在Cook County等地区的数据中存在系统性错误（sale价格误差、覆盖率缺失、概念差异），这些错误会显著偏倚经济不平等（特别是房产税累退性）的关键测量，且错误在经纪商之间高度一致，凸显开放行政数据和数据来源透明的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大数据正日益依赖经纪商提供的数据集，而经纪商房产记录被广泛用于绅士化、不平等和房产税研究以及房产估值模型中。然而这些数据的准确性和质量尚未被系统性审计，其潜在错误可能从根本上偏倚经济不平等的关键测量结果。

Method: 作者对比了两个主要的经纪商房产数据集与"ground truth"（真实交易记录），针对2018-2021年间Cook County, IL的匹配交易进行审计，量化了三类错误：（1）sale价格报告的误差（>5%偏差）；（2）缺失数据和概念差异导致的覆盖率误差（12-15%）；（3）经纪商之间错误报告的一致性检验。并将分析推广到美国另外两个大县以验证普遍性。

Result: 研究发现：1-2%的匹配交易的经纪商提供的sale价格与真实价格偏差超过5%；覆盖率误差达交易的12-15%；经纪商之间的错误报告高度一致；这些错误对房产税累退性估计产生显著的影响，不同数据源导致估计结果出现显著差异；发现可推广到美国另外两个大县。

Conclusion: 经纪商房产数据的系统性错误会实质性地偏倚关键经济不平等指标的测量，研究强调了开放行政数据和经纪商透明度（数据来源和谱系）对于保障研究和AI模型数据质量的极端重要性。

Abstract: Big data are the foundation for an increasing share of academic research and AI models deployed in both the public and private sectors, prompting substantial growth over time in reliance on brokered datasets. Brokered property records, which are ubiquitous in studies of gentrification, inequality, and the property tax in the U.S. and serve as inputs to property valuation models, are one notable example. In this paper, we audit two prominent brokered property datasets, finding errors in these data which bias key measures of economic inequality. First, we document that for 1-2% of matched sales in Cook County, IL, from 2018-2021, broker-provided sale prices differ from ground truth sale prices by more than 5%. Moreover, missing data and conceptual differences in the reporting of deed and property characteristics lead to coverage errors ranging from 12 to 15% of transactions. Second, we show that misreporting is highly consistent between brokers: more often than not, brokers make identical reporting errors for the same transactions. Third, to illustrate the significance of these errors, we measure their impact on estimates of property tax regressivity, finding that they drive significant wedges between estimates depending on the data source. These findings generalize to two other large counties in the U.S., and highlight the crucial importance of open administrative data and transparency from brokers regarding data provenance and lineage.

</details>


### [56] [From Process to Evidence: How Computing Can Ground Appropriate Reliance on Legal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28869)
*James Bryan Williams*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文分析了纽约法院系统官方记录中关于AI辅助法律文件撰写的规则与指南，指出现有法律系统在缺乏实证数据（如错误率、不可用列表）的情况下，用程序要求（如培训、清单、人工审查）替代证据，导致负担最重的恰恰是资源最匮乏的群体。论文提出四项贡献：将法律义务映射到HCI概念、从官方记录中提取需求、分析法律系统以程序替代证据的现象，以及为计算社区提出研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 律师和自诉当事人已在使用AI起草法律文件，法院也出台了相应规则。超过1500起涉及AI幻觉的案件后，律师被要求对AI辅助提交的文件进行仔细独立审查。但这些责任需要HCI文献所称的"适当依赖"，而缺乏关于AI工具在法律工作中失败频率、严重程度和可检测性的证据，就无法校准这种依赖。现有研究几乎未涉及这三方面，形成关键证据缺口。

Method: 作者分析了纽约法院系统的官方记录，包括相关规则、命令和指南文件。他们对文档内容进行系统性审查，提取其中反复呼吁但现实中不存在的证据（如错误率、不可用列表），并分析法院如何转而依赖程序性措施。基于此，作者构建了法律义务到HCI概念的映射，提炼需求清单，并设计了面向计算社区的研究议程。

Result: 研究发现官方记录反复呼吁不存在的证据，转而依赖培训要求、检查清单和未校准的人工审查等程序性措施。法律扶助项目被要求自行追踪错误率，法官不得不自行发明测试方法。论文产出了四个成果：法律义务到HCI概念的映射、从官方记录中提取的需求集、对程序替代证据现象的分析，以及涵盖任务分类学、共享错误指标、持续维护的基准和私有数据评估测试平台的研究议程。

Conclusion: 法律系统缺乏AI可靠性的实证证据，而用程序要求填补了这一空白，但这种方式不成比例地加重了资源匮乏者（法律扶助项目和法官）的负担。计算社区应当提供法律系统所缺失的证据基础，帮助弥合而非扩大司法鸿沟。

Abstract: Lawyers and self-represented litigants are already using artificial intelligence (AI) to draft legal documents, and courts are responding with rules. After more than 1,500 cases involving AI hallucinations, lawyers have been instructed to perform careful, independent review of AI-assisted filings. Discharging these duties requires what the human-computer interaction (HCI) literature calls ``appropriate reliance,'' which cannot be calibrated without evidence on how often, how badly, and how detectably these tools fail at legal work. Existing research barely describes any of the three. We analyze the official record of the New York court system. The documents repeatedly call for evidence that does not exist (e.g., error rates, do-not-use lists). In its place they invoke procedure, including training mandates, checklists, and uncalibrated human review. The burden falls hardest on those least equipped to bear it: legal aid programs are told to track their own error rates, and judges are left to improvise their own tests. The paper makes four contributions: (1) a mapping from the legal duties to concepts in HCI; (2) a set of requirements elicited from the official record; (3) an analysis of how the legal system substitutes process for evidence; and (4) a research agenda for computing, including task taxonomies, shared error metrics, maintained benchmarks, and test harnesses for evaluations on private data. The computing community must supply what the justice system lacks; in doing so, it can help close, rather than widen, the justice gap.

</details>


### [57] [IyawoBench v2.0: Extended Diagnostic Evaluation of Large Language Model Clinical Triage in Nigerian Primary Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29085)
*Anthonio Oladimeji Gabriel,Dimeji Olawuyi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出IyawoBench v2.0，一个扩展的大语言模型临床分诊诊断评估基准，基于尼日利亚19个初级卫生中心的1,200例真实患者遭遇，构建200个合成临床小插图。论文引入包含14个定义和2个定理的形式化数学框架，将分诊安全分解为三种不同的失败模式，并提出Escalation Bias Index和Expected Deployment Cost两个新指标，以揭示传统准确率和灵敏度指标所掩盖的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在中低收入国家（LMIC），大语言模型正被部署为临床分诊工具，但现有安全指标会产生误导性信心——模型在"未将紧急患者送回家"这种二元安全指标上得100分，仍可能展现出使其无法大规模部署的系统性失败模式。传统指标无法捕捉这些隐藏的系统性缺陷，需要更严格的诊断评估框架。

Method: （1）基于19个尼日利亚初级卫生中心的1,200例真实患者遭遇构建200个合成临床小插图；（2）提出包含14个定义和2个定理的形式化数学框架，将分诊安全分解为三种失败模式：保守升级偏差（Conservative Escalation Bias）、系统性降级偏差（Systematic Downgrade Bias）和中层不稳定（Middle-Tier Instability）；（3）提出Escalation Bias Index和Expected Deployment Cost两个新指标；（4）在三个前沿模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6、Llama 3.3 70B、Llama 3.1 8B）和五个朴素基线上进行评估。

Result: （1）所有三个模型均表现出至少一种形式化失败模式；（2）传统灵敏度指标掩盖了Llama 3.1 8B中77个百分点的欠分诊（under-triage）差距；（3）最优模型因部署场景而异（紧急聚焦、系统可持续性、平衡三种场景），证明单一排名的基准不足以用于LMIC临床AI选择。

Conclusion: IyawoBench v2.0提供了严格的基准和可转移的诊断框架，适用于任何分诊式临床AI评估。所有代码、数据和分​​析流程均已公开，为LMIC临床AI的安全评估和模型选择提供了更全面的方法论基础。

Abstract: Large language models are being deployed as clinical triage tools in low and middle income countries where trained physicians are scarce. Existing safety metrics, however, produce misleading confidence: models scoring 100% on binary "did not send an emergency home" safety measures may nevertheless exhibit systematic failure modes that render them undeployable at scale. We present IyawoBench v2.0, an extended diagnostic evaluation of large language model clinical triage on 200 synthetic vignettes derived from 1,200 real patient encounters at 19 Nigerian primary health centres. We introduce a formal mathematical framework comprising fourteen definitions and two theorems that decompose triage safety into three distinct failure modes: Conservative Escalation Bias, Systematic Downgrade Bias, and Middle-Tier Instability. We propose the Escalation Bias Index and Expected Deployment Cost as novel metrics that expose failure modes hidden by conventional accuracy and sensitivity scores. Evaluated on three frontier models (Claude Sonnet 4.6, Llama 3.3 70B, Llama 3.1 8B) plus five naive baselines, we show that: (1) all three models exhibit at least one formal failure mode; (2) traditional sensitivity metrics conceal a 77 percentage point under-triage gap in Llama 3.1 8B; (3) the optimal model varies across three deployment scenarios (Emergency-Focused, System-Sustainability, Balanced), demonstrating that single-ranking benchmarks are inadequate for LMIC clinical AI selection. IyawoBench v2.0 provides both a rigorous benchmark and a diagnostic framework transferable to any triage-style clinical AI evaluation. All code, data, and analysis pipelines are publicly available.

</details>


### [58] [The Deployment Wall: A Diagnostic Framework and Instrument for Enterprise AI in the Deployment Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29089)
*Fabricio F. Costa*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 企业生成式AI投入激增但95%的试点项目未能产生可衡量的利润影响。本文认为这不是模型能力不足，而是企业AI已进入"部署时代"——优势来源于消除阻碍模型进入生产的组织与架构摩擦。论文提出三个构念（部署墙、接缝指数、部署债务）和一个测量工具，将平台决策从基准比较转化为架构比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业生成式AI投资三年内大幅增长至约370亿美元，但独立实地研究显示约95%的企业生成式AI试点未产生可衡量的利润影响。主流解释认为模型能力不足，但作者认为这一解释是错误的，真正的瓶颈在于组织和架构层面的摩擦阻碍了模型进入生产环境。

Method: 本文基于软件工程中关于技术债务和机器学习部署的文献，结合独立实地研究的结构化综合，提出三个相互关联的构念和一套测量工具：1) 部署墙（Deployment Wall）——六阶段价值泄漏模型，可机械复现观察到的存活率；2) 接缝指数（Seam Index）——0-12分的可复现诊断工具，评估平台原生消除六类摩擦"接缝"的程度；3) 部署债务（Deployment Debt）——将未解决的摩擦重新定义为复合的、可量化的负债。论文还制定了带证据锚点的评分协议，并给出了可操作的平台选择示例。

Result: 给出了六项可证伪的命题及验证研究议程。该框架将八位数的平台决策从基准比较转变为架构比较，使诊断和评估具有可操作性和一致性。

Conclusion: 企业AI的优势不再源于模型智能本身，而源于消除阻碍模型从能力走向生产的组织与架构摩擦。通过部署墙、接缝指数和部署债务三个构念，可以量化诊断企业AI部署中的价值泄漏，为平台选择和部署决策提供更科学的依据。

Abstract: Enterprise investment in generative artificial intelligence (AI) tripled in a single year to roughly US$37 billion, yet independent field research finds that about 95% of enterprise generative-AI pilots deliver no measurable profit-and-loss impact. We argue that the dominant explanation--that models are not yet capable enough--is mistaken, and that enterprise AI has entered a Deployment Era in which advantage derives not from model intelligence but from the removal of the organizational and architectural friction that prevents a capable model from reaching production. Building on the software-engineering literature on technical debt and machine-learning deployment, and on a structured synthesis of independent field studies, we make the diagnosis operational. We introduce three linked constructs and one measurement instrument: the Deployment Wall, a six-stage value-leak model that mechanically reproduces observed survival rates; the Seam Index, a reproducible 0-12 diagnostic that scores any platform by how many of six recurring friction "seams" it removes natively rather than leaving to the adopter; and Deployment Debt, a construct that reframes unresolved friction as a compounding, quantifiable liability. We specify a scoring protocol with evidence anchors so the instrument can be applied consistently, illustrate it on a worked platform-selection example, and derive six falsifiable propositions with a research agenda for validation. The framework converts an eight-figure platform decision from a benchmark comparison into an architecture comparison.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者提出了一个双向集成架构：一方面从LMS（如Blackboard）提取课程内容来动态生成游戏环境；另一方面游戏内的学习数据可以反馈回LMS。具体实现为一个可导入的Unity包，并开发了一个演示游戏来验证该方案的可行性。

Result: 成功实现了Unity包与Blackboard的集成，并通过演示游戏验证了该方案的可行性。论文同时识别了当前方案的局限性，为后续研究提供了方向。

Conclusion: 本文证明了基于LMS内容动态生成游戏环境的双向集成是可行的，并提出了"超游戏化"这一新概念来指导未来的教育游戏设计。作者指出工作仍处于初步阶段，需要进一步解决技术限制并扩展应用场景。

Abstract: This paper discusses games, their use in education, and previous work on integrating game engines and learning management systems (LMS). It proposes a bidirectional integration where game environments are generated using LMS content, introducing the concept of hypergamification as the use of a comprehensive game environment rather than isolated game design elements. A working pilot implementation of an importable Unity package for Blackboard integration is demonstrated, along with a demo game that uses the developed package. The paper also discusses the limitations of the proposed approach and outlines avenues for future work.

</details>


### [60] [Triangulating Across U.S. Federal AI Transparency Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29540)
*Emma Lurie,Emma Fauser,Qing He,Danaé Metaxa,Sorelle A. Friedler*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了美国联邦AI系统透明度的三个现有制度（SORNs、ICRs和AI用例清单），发现单个制度均无法完整揭示政府AI系统的构建和部署方式。作者提出了一种跨文档实体解析的三角测量方法，将三个制度中的披露信息关联起来，并通过两个案例研究表明关联后的记录仍不足以构成充分的监督。文章指出这些披露缺陷源于各制度最初的管理目的，属于结构性缺陷，并针对AI用例清单提出了三点改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦AI系统可能影响公民的福利资格或驱逐决定等重大权益，但现有公开披露机制碎片化且详略不一，公众难以全面了解政府的AI使用情况。作者旨在评估三个现有透明度制度单独及联合描述政府AI使用的能力，并探索改进路径。

Method: 采用手工验证的零样本分类和跨文档实体解析技术，构建三角测量方法以关联三个披露制度（SORNs、ICRs和AI用例清单）中的AI系统披露信息，并通过两个案例研究进行验证和深入分析。

Result: 研究发现：没有任何单一制度能完整揭示政府AI系统的构建与部署；各制度披露不同方面，且缺乏持久标识符、粒度差异大；AI用例清单的年度周期导致系统部署数月后才出现在官方记录中。关联记录虽能提供更多洞察，但对比公开报道仍不足以构成充分监督。

Conclusion: 披露缺陷源于各制度最初的管理目的，属于结构性问题。作者建议以AI用例清单为机制核心，提出三点改进：（1）采用广泛且一致的AI系统定义；（2）建立持久系统标识符并交叉引用相关披露；（3）恢复风险管理流程的公众可见性。

Abstract: Federal AI systems can deny benefits or flag individuals for deportation, but the public disclosures meant to make those systems visible are fragmented and unevenly detailed. This paper examines three existing U.S. federal transparency regimes---System of Records Notices (SORNs), Information Collection Requests (ICRs), and the AI Use Case Inventory---and asks how well they, individually and together, describe government AI use. We find that no single regime fully reveals how the government constructs or deploys AI: each discloses different aspects of a system, and the current disclosure infrastructure makes it very challenging for the public to track specific AI systems across regulatory regimes and over time. Persistent identifiers are absent, granularity varies widely, and the annual AI Use Case Inventory cycle means federal agencies can deploy systems months before appearing in any official record. Using hand-validated zero-shot classification and cross-document entity resolution, we contribute a triangulation method that links disclosures across all three regimes and present two case studies. Our case studies finds that linking records provides greater insight into government AI use, but even linked records would constitute insufficient oversight compared to what public reporting has revealed about the same systems. We trace each regime's disclosure weaknesses to its original administrative purpose, showing these gaps are structural, and offer recommendations focused on the AI Use Case Inventory as the mechanism best suited for public-facing transparency: (1) a broad and consistently applied AI system definition, (2) persistent system identifiers with cross-references to related disclosures, and (3) restored public visibility into risk management processes.

</details>


### [61] [The Theoretical Foundation of Socratic Tests: Dynamic, Multimodal, Conversational Examinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29624)
*Ilya Mikhelson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了"苏格拉底测试"（Socratic Test）的理论框架，一种自动化、计算机介导的对话式评估系统，融合动态评估、多模态工作区、布鲁姆分类法（实时监考）和SOLO分类法（结构评估），通过渐进式脚手架量化最近发展区（ZPD），并采用非补偿性、加性评分架构，以掌握为导向替代传统扣分模式，结合人机对齐确保测量可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态评估基于减法/缺陷导向的评分模型，会惩罚学生的进取心并模糊诊断性反馈；而传统面对面口试则因表演焦虑和学术层级中的权力失衡引入严重的构念无关方差（construct-irrelevant variance）。作者旨在克服这两种评估范式的根本缺陷。

Method: 提出"苏格拉底测试"框架：（1）整合动态评估原理与多模态工作区；（2）利用布鲁姆分类法进行实时监考；（3）利用SOLO分类法进行结构评估；（4）正式化渐进式脚手架以量化ZPD；（5）设计非补偿性、加性评分架构强调掌握而非惩罚；（6）通过人类与AI对齐保证测量信度。

Result: 该论文提供了"苏格拉底测试"的理论基础，实现了对学生认知边界（cognitive boundaries）的主动映射，并详细阐明了用于量化ZPD的渐进式脚手架方法以及非补偿性加性评分架构的具体形式。

Conclusion: "苏格拉底测试"作为一种替代传统评估范式的理论框架，有望同时解决静态评估的诊断缺陷和口试的情境性偏差，通过人机协作实现前所未有的测量可靠性，为教育评估提供了新的理论方向。

Abstract: Traditional static assessments rely on a subtractive, deficit-based grading model that often penalizes ambition and obscures diagnostic feedback. Conversely, traditional face-to-face oral examinations introduce severe construct-irrelevant variance by exacerbating performative anxiety and the sociological power imbalances inherent to academic hierarchies. This paper presents the theoretical foundation for the "Socratic Test," an automated, computer-mediated conversational assessment. By integrating Dynamic Assessment principles, multimodal workspaces, Bloom's Taxonomy for real-time proctoring, and the SOLO Taxonomy for structural evaluation, the Socratic Test actively maps a student's cognitive boundaries. This paper formalizes the use of graduated scaffolding to quantify the Zone of Proximal Development (ZPD) and details a non-compensatory, additive grading architecture that prioritizes mastery over penalty and human-AI alignment to ensure unprecedented measurement reliability.

</details>
