### [62] [Sensitivity-Based System Strength Assessment: Mapping Power Flow and Network Topology Perturbations to System Eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28764)
*Trager Joswig-Jones,Shuan Dong,Jin Tan,Baosen Zhang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出基于灵敏度的系统强度指标，通过分析功率注入、电压和线路导纳的扰动来快速评估含高比例逆变器资源（IBR）电力系统的小信号稳定性，避免了重复特征值计算的负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着逆变器资源（IBR）在电力系统中占比不断增大，量化系统强度对于识别这些设备引发的稳定性问题至关重要。现有的基于导纳模型的系统强度指标需要在多个运行点进行重复评估，计算成本高，难以快速理解系统状态变化对稳定性的影响。

Method: 论文考虑系统稳定性对稳态运行点扰动的灵敏度，提出基于功率注入、电压和线路导纳灵敏度的系统强度指标。利用这些灵敏度可以识别触发失稳机制的系统状态变化（如线路跳闸或发电机出力增加），相比重复特征值计算大幅提升计算效率。

Result: 在14节点和118节点测试系统上进行了验证，结果表明所提出的灵敏度指标能为小信号稳定性问题提供关键见解，并可用于寻找校正措施，同时计算速度远快于重复特征值计算。

Conclusion: 基于灵敏度的系统强度指标能够快速有效地识别系统中引发失稳的关键状态变化，为含高比例IBR电力系统的小信号稳定性分析和校正措施制定提供了高效工具。

Abstract: As inverter-based resources (IBRs) contribute larger shares of generation in electrical power grids, quantifying system strength becomes increasingly important for identifying stability issues introduced by these devices. While admittance model based system strength metrics have been proposed to identify control interactions in systems with high levels of IBRs, these methods depend on repeated evaluations across many operating points to understand how the state of the system impacts system strength. To address this challenge, we consider the sensitivity of system stability to perturbations in the steady-state operating point, and propose system strength metrics based on sensitivities to power injections, voltages, and line admittances. Using these sensitivities we can identify changes in the system's state (e.g. a line tripping off or a generator increasing its power output) that trigger instability mechanisms. We show that these metrics provide critical insights into system stability and can be computed much faster than repeated eigenvalue calculations. We demonstrate our approach on 14-bus and 118-bus test systems to show how the metrics can be used to find remedial actions for small-signal stability issues.

</details>


### [63] [Resilient Control of Switched Vehicle Platoons under False Data Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28794)
*Ali Eslami,Jiangbo Yu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了受虚假数据注入攻击的领导者-跟随者车辆队列的弹性控制设计问题。车辆动力学采用切换三阶模型描述不同运行模式下的动力总成变化，通过辅助系统和专用观测器估计通信信道中的攻击信号，并设计弹性控制器来减轻攻击影响。对于有界速率但可能无界幅值的攻击，闭环队列被证明是一致最终有界的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆队列中的V2V通信容易遭受FDI攻击，此类攻击可能破坏队列的稳定性和安全性。现有的控制方法大多假设攻击信号具有已知特性或仅考虑简单的攻击模型，缺乏对模式依赖的动力总成动力学与攻击同时存在的场景的鲁棒控制研究。本文旨在解决这一实际问题，为受攻击的切换系统车辆队列提供弹性控制方案。

Method: 1) 建立切换三阶模型描述车辆纵向运动中动力总成的模式变化；2) 为每辆车配备辅助系统，并为每条通信链路设计专用观测器以估计攻击信号；3) 利用攻击估计值设计弹性控制器来抵消攻击影响；4) 对前车跟随拓扑下的标称切换队列建立弦稳定性分析，并分析攻击估计误差对加速度传播的影响。

Result: 1) 对于攻击速率有界但幅值可能无界的FDI攻击，闭环车辆队列被证明是一致最终有界的；2) 在前车跟随拓扑下，标称切换队列的弦稳定性得以建立；3) 非零攻击估计误差对加速度传播的影响被证明是有界的；4) 数值案例验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的弹性控制框架能够有效处理由模式依赖动力总成切换和FDI攻击同时存在带来的挑战，通过攻击观测与补偿相结合的策略，保证了车辆队列在受到攻击时的有界稳定性和弦稳定性，为智能网联车辆在实际通信攻击环境下的安全运行提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper investigates resilient control design for leader--follower vehicle platoons with mode-dependent powertrain dynamics subject to False Data Injection (FDI) attacks on vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) communication channels. The longitudinal motion of each vehicle is described by a switched third-order model that captures changes in the powertrain dynamics across different operating modes. To estimate the attack signals that are injected into the communication channels, each vehicle is equipped with an auxiliary system, and a dedicated observer is implemented for each communication link. The resulting attack estimates are then used to mitigate the effects of the attacks through our proposed resilient controller. For attacks with bounded rates but potentially unbounded amplitudes, the closed-loop platoon is shown to be uniformly ultimately bounded. For a predecessor-following topology, string stability is established for the nominal switched platoon, while the effect of nonzero attack-estimation errors on acceleration propagation is shown to be bounded. Numerical case studies demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [64] [Agentic Artificial Intelligence for Power Systems: Strategies to Identify and Close Capability Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28822)
*Eve Tsybina,Samim Konjicija,Slaven Peles*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文评估了当前最先进的Agentic AI在电力系统规划任务中的成熟度，发现其仅能解决两个最低复杂度的任务级别，且仅在部分电网规模上可行，并指出了所需的具体能力升级方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的数据中心等用电设施快速增长，对电力系统造成前所未有的压力，而输电扩容缓慢，导致新增负荷和电源只能在现有网络约束下部署。Agentic AI被认为可以自动化大量重复性的并网流程，但其在复杂任务和大规模系统中的成熟度尚未被系统验证。

Method: 复现当前最先进的Agentic AI用于电力系统规划，并针对六层任务复杂度和四个电网规模的结构化节点规划问题套件进行评估。

Result: 只有两个最低复杂度级别的任务在部分测试电网规模上可解，识别出了弥合这一差距所需的具体能力升级。

Conclusion: 采用更严格的测试协议和可复现的评估基准对于评估Agentic AI的真实进展和运营就绪程度至关重要。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of AI-driven information infrastructure, particularly data centers, is placing unprecedented pressure on power systems and accelerating the pace at which new assets must interconnect with the grid. As bulk transmission expansion rolls out slowly, new loads and generation are increasingly deployed within existing network constraints. Agentic AI is urgently needed to automate the numerous and repetitive connection processes, but its maturity has not been systematically validated on complex tasks and large-scale systems. We replicate the current state of the art in agentic AI for power systems planning and evaluate it against a structured suite of nodal planning problems spanning six levels of task complexity and four grid scales. We find that only the two lowest complexity levels are solvable on some of the test grid sizes, and identify the specific capability upgrades required to close this gap. Adopting stricter testing protocols and reproducible evaluation benchmarks is essential for assessing both genuine progress and the operational readiness of agentic AI.

</details>


### [65] [Closed-Loop Model-Based Control Barrier Functions with Application to Robust Flight Envelope Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28830)
*Johannes Autenrieb,Mark Spiller,Peter A. Fisher,Junhyeok Yoon,Spilios Theodoulis,Anuradha Annaswamy*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于闭环模型的控制障碍函数（CLM-CBF）框架用于飞行包线保护，通过在参考层而非控制输入层施加安全约束，保留了底层控制器的稳定性与鲁棒性，并支持模块化集成到现有飞行控制架构中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CBF方法基于开环模型在控制输入层施加安全约束，虽然实现了模块化设计，但会改变闭环系统动力学，可能损害鲁棒性保证并增加与现有飞行控制架构集成的复杂性。

Method: 提出闭环模型控制障碍函数（CLM-CBF）框架，核心思想是利用显式的闭环系统模型在参考信号层施加安全约束，使安全滤波不影响底层控制律。

Result: 该框架在不修改控制律的前提下实现安全滤波，保留底层控制器的稳定性与鲁棒性特性，便于模块化集成和系统改造。

Conclusion: CLM-CBF为飞行包线保护提供了一种保留原有控制器性能的安全滤波方案，实现安全性与控制性能的兼顾。

Abstract: Ensuring operation of aerospace systems within prescribed flight envelope limits is a fundamental requirement for modern flight control architectures. Flight envelope protection aims to prevent violations of aerodynamic and structural constraints, thereby mitigating risks such as stall and excessive load factors. Control barrier functions (CBFs) have emerged as a principled tool for enforcing safety by ensuring that the system state remains within a prescribed safe set. In most existing approaches, safety constraints are imposed at the control input-level based on an open-loop model of the system. While this open-loop model-based CBF formulation enables modular design, it may alter the closed-loop system dynamics, potentially compromising robustness guarantees and complicating integration into existing flight control architectures. This paper proposes a closed-loop model-based control barrier function (CLM-CBF) framework for flight envelope protection. The key idea is to enforce safety at the reference-level using an explicit model of the closed-loop system, thereby preserving the stability and robustness properties of the underlying controller. This formulation enables safety filtering without modifying the control law, facilitating modular integration and retrofitting into existing systems.

</details>


### [66] [Hidden Economic Consequences of Adapting to Fast Ramping Datacenter Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28833)
*Willa Gutowski,Charles Foltz,Nicholson Koukpaizan,Slaven Peles,Eve Tsybina*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了人工智能负载对数据中心基础设施扩张带来的电力系统压力，重点揭示了高峰期之外被低估的高电价威胁。通过在基于IEEE 118-bus改进的5000节点可拥塞系统中模拟不同爬坡条件，发现快速爬坡的数据中心负载与慢速爬坡的发电机组相互作用会加剧潜在负载口袋问题，导致非数据中心高峰时段出现意外的高系统成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能负载驱动数据中心基础设施快速扩张，对美国能源系统造成巨大压力。虽然高峰时段的高电价是可预期的结果，但非高峰时段的高电价是一个被严重低估的威胁，作者旨在揭示这一被忽视的成本。

Method: 作者在基于IEEE 118-bus电网改进的5000节点可拥塞系统上，模拟不同爬坡条件，测试两种不同负载条件下数据中心的影响。通过耦合仿真观察系统在峰时和非峰时的表现，分析慢速爬坡发电机组为应对数据中心负载变化而进行的预防性爬坡所引发的成本。

Result: 在系统重合峰值场景下，耦合仿真导致慢速高成本机组达到高达100%的负载率，平均边际成本增加8%。结果表明数据中心负载会加剧潜在负载口袋问题，在数据中心非高峰时段也引发异常高的系统成本。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了为预防数据中心负载变化而提前爬坡慢速发电机组的隐藏成本，这一发现对于理解人工智能基础设施扩张对电力系统经济性的全面影响具有重要意义。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence workloads are driving the rapid expansion of datacenter infrastructure, which imposes substantial stress on the US energy system. While high peak electricity prices are an anticipated outcome, measurable under peak hour simulations, the high off peak prices are a significantly underestimated threat. We simulate different ramping conditions on a congestible 5000-bus system, based on a modified IEEE 118-bus grid, to show that, in the presence of fast ramping loads and slow ramping generation, datacenters can aggravate latent load pockets. This results in unexpectedly high system costs during periods outside of datacenter peak. We test the datacenter effects using two distinct load conditions. We find that in the system coincident peak, coupled simulations result in up to 100% loading of slow expensive units, with an average marginal cost increase of 8%. These findings are of extreme importance as they reveal the hidden costs of preventively ramping slow generation in anticipation of datacenter load changes.

</details>


### [67] [Model Predictive Path Integral Control as a Quantum Query Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28851)
*Goutam Das,Takashi Tanaka*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文重新表述了有限系综MPPI（模型预测路径积分控制）更新，将其各组件转化为有界路径期望的比值，并通过可逆rollout预言机将其编码为成功概率，从而可利用量子振幅估计直接估计该更新，在精度和罕见事件偏好上相较经典蒙特卡洛采样获得二次方的查询复杂度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI控制算法通过对成本加权轨迹样本进行更新，但在罕见事件或高精度场景下需要大量经典rollout，计算开销巨大。作者希望利用量子计算加速这一过程，实现查询复杂度层面的二次方加速。

Method: 作者将有限系综MPPI更新的每个组件重新表述为有界路径期望的比值，构造可逆rollout预言机将这些期望编码为成功概率，从而使得更新可以直接通过量子振幅估计来估计。该方法采用逐坐标（coordinatewise）构造方式，对控制输入数量呈线性依赖关系。

Result: 该方法在精度和罕见事件偏好上相较经典蒙特卡洛采样实现了二次方改进，匹配了已知的标量问题的下界；在穷举评估阈值以下成立。对于固定系综，低温权重集中在最小成本轨迹上，当最小化器唯一时，极限控制与量子最小值搜索相关联。全可枚举的引导实例验证了预测的估计器缩放，操作计数模型也给出了查询优势与建模实现优势的分离条件。

Conclusion: 量子振幅估计可以有效地加速MPPI更新估计，提供查询复杂度上的二次方加速，但加速效果受控制输入维度的线性开销影响，且优势主要体现在查询层面而非实际实现层面。

Abstract: Model predictive path integral control computes its update from cost-weighted trajectory samples and may require many classical rollouts in rare-event or high-accuracy regimes. We reformulate each component of the finite-ensemble MPPI update as a ratio of bounded path expectations and construct reversible rollout oracles encoding them as success probabilities, making the update directly estimable by quantum amplitude estimation. This gives a quadratic improvement in the query dependence on accuracy and rare-event desirability over classical Monte Carlo sampling, matching known lower bounds for the underlying scalar problem below the exhaustive-evaluation threshold, while our coordinatewise construction incurs a linear dependence on the number of control inputs. For a fixed ensemble, the low-temperature weights concentrate on the minimum-cost trajectories, connecting the limiting control to quantum minimum finding when the minimizer is unique. A fully enumerable guidance example validates the predicted estimator scalings, and an illustrative operation-count model with a crossover condition separates query advantage from modeled implementation advantage.

</details>


### [68] [Adaptive Attitude Estimation for Multiple-Surface Object Using Light Curve Glints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28912)
*Yasuhiro Yoshimura,Toshiya Hanada*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于交互式多模型（IMM）算法的多面体空间目标姿态估计方法，通过利用光变曲线中的闪光（glint）事件来约束姿态估计，解决了多表面目标闪光源不唯一导致的姿态估计歧义问题。数值仿真表明，对于地球同步轨道上的箱式卫星，在初始姿态误差高达80°的情况下，该方法将收敛率从单面滤波器的10%提升至73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光变曲线反演是估计空间目标轨道、姿态、光学特性和形状的重要手段，但问题通常是病态的，因为同一光变曲线可能对应多个姿态解，导致卡尔曼滤波对初始估计非常敏感，不准确的初始值可能导致滤波器发散。尽管已有研究利用闪光（glint）来约束姿态估计范围，但对于多表面目标，多个表面都可能产生闪光，姿态估计仍然不唯一。

Method: 本文采用交互式多模型（IMM）算法，运行多个并行滤波器，每个滤波器假设闪光发生在对应表面上。通过各滤波器的似然函数更新模式概率，判断各假设的正确性。IMM中的混合步骤通过转移概率矩阵实现滤波器间的交互，从而适应时变的闪光源。

Result: 对地球同步轨道箱式卫星进行数值仿真，在初始姿态误差高达80°的蒙特卡洛试验中，所提方法将收敛率从单表面滤波器的10%提升至73%；移除混合步骤后收敛率降至40%，证明了混合步骤的关键作用。

Conclusion: IMM算法能够有效解决多表面目标姿态估计中闪光源不唯一的问题，显著提高滤波器在较大初始姿态误差下的收敛性能，且混合步骤对于算法性能至关重要。

Abstract: Light curve inversion enables the estimation of orbit, attitude, optical properties, and shape of space objects. Because a light curve is the temporal evolution of a scalar apparent magnitude, the estimation problem can be ill-posed owing to the non-uniqueness of the attitude that reproduces a given light curve. The initial estimate in Kalman filtering can therefore be sensitive, and, depending on the object properties, observation geometry, and number of estimated parameters, an inaccurate initial estimate may lead to divergence of the filter. A previous study uses a sudden change of light curves, called glint, to constrain the range of attitude estimate. The current paper extends the attitude estimation method using glint for multiple-surface objects. Such objects have multiple attitudes to yield glint, and the attitude estimate is not uniquely determined. To address this issue, this paper employs the interacting multiple model (IMM) algorithm that runs multiple parallel filters with model interaction in the estimation sequence. Each filter assumes that the glint occurs on the corresponding surface. The mode probability is updated by the likelihood of each filter, determining the correctness of the hypotheses. Furthermore, the mixing step in the IMM allows interaction among the filters through a transition probability matrix, enabling adaptation to the time-varying glint source. Numerical simulations are conducted for a box satellite in a geosynchronous orbit. Monte Carlo trials with initial attitude errors of up to 80~deg show that the proposed method improves the convergence rate from 10\% for a single surface filter to 73%, and the mixing step is shown to be essential, since the convergence rate drops to 40% when it is removed.

</details>


### [69] [Learning Structural Eigenmodes with Modal Operator Network (ModalONet)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28926)
*Saad Waheed,Shabbir Ahmed*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出ModalONet，一种利用神经算子直接从系统响应场中恢复模态基（振型、固有频率、阻尼比）的方法，无需特征值求解器和标注模态。通过在四种结构系统上的验证，实现MAC≥0.998、频率误差<5%、阻尼比误差<7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经算子（如DeepONet、LNO）几乎只用于学习系统的正向响应，而非挖掘系统的内在结构（如模态特性）。作者希望将算子学习应用于模态识别，直接从响应场中恢复模态基，避免传统特征值求解器和对标注模态的依赖。

Method: ModalONet结合了DeepONet的branch-trunk分解与LNO的极-留数形式：trunk网络提供连续、无网格的振型（模态叠加的可学习形式），LNO branch提供模态坐标（极点-留数形式），每个学习到的极点对应一个固有频率和阻尼比。训练仅使用响应场，采用重构损失、正交归一性损失和时间投影一致性损失的复合损失。退化（等频率）模态通过可分离的trunk和共享频率参数解决，并通过事后对数包络回归改进阻尼比估计。

Result: 在四种结构系统（简支和悬臂Euler-Bernoulli梁、矩形及退化的方形Kirchhoff板）上，ModalONet恢复的解析模态基在所有振型上MAC值≥0.998，固有频率误差<5%，阻尼比误差<7%。

Conclusion: ModalONet证明了神经算子可以作为准确且可解释的模态识别工具，将算子学习从正向响应预测扩展到结构内在模态属性的提取。

Abstract: Neural operators such as the deep operator network (DeepONet) and the Laplace neural operator (LNO) are effective surrogates, but they are almost exclusively trained to reproduce the forward response of a system rather than its intrinsic structure, such as the modal properties of a structural system. We introduce ModalONet, which puts operator learning to a different use, such as recovering the modal basis, namely: mode shapes, natural frequencies, and damping ratios of a dynamical system directly from its response field, with no eigensolver and no labeled modes. Our key observation is that the DeepONet branch-trunk factorization is itself a learnable form of modal superposition: the trunk supplies continuous, mesh-free mode shapes, while an LNO branch supplies the modal coordinates in pole-residue form, so that each learned pole yields a natural frequency and a damping ratio. Training uses the response field alone, under a composite loss of reconstruction, orthonormality, and temporal projection consistency. The degenerate (equal-frequency) modes are resolved by a separable trunk and a shared frequency parameter, with post-hoc log-envelope regression improves the damping ratio estimates. Across four structural systems, namely: simply supported and cantilever Euler-Bernoulli beams and rectangular and (degenerate) square Kirchhoff plates, the ModalONet recovers the analytical modal basis with modal assurance criterion (MAC) values of at least 0.998 for every mode shape, natural frequency errors within 5%, and damping ratio errors within 7%, demonstrating the potential of neural operators as accurate and interpretable tools for modal identification.

</details>


### [70] [Boundary-layer control with unstructured uncertainties with application to adaptive autopilots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28951)
*Peng Li,Di Liu,Simone Baldi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对具有非结构化不确定性的控制系统（即参数及其在动力学中的出现方式均未知），提出了一种仅依赖基本欧拉-拉格朗日性质的通用自适应控制方法，不依赖于不确定性的结构知识，并在ArduPilot的ArduPlane模块中验证，同时适用于ArduCopter、ArduRover、ArduSub等模块，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶仪（autopilots）领域，需要为不同结构（空中/地面/水上）的运载器寻求统一控制架构，但现有方法常常依赖不确定性的结构知识，无法处理参数出现方式未知的非结构化不确定性。

Method: 仅利用大多数机械系统中普遍成立的基本欧拉-拉格朗日性质，设计不依赖不确定性结构知识的自适应控制方案，实现对非结构化不确定性的自适应补偿。

Result: 该方法在ArduPilot的ArduPlane模块中得到验证，并适用于ArduCopter、ArduRover、ArduSub等其他模块；数值与实验结果表明性能优于处理非结构和状态依赖不确定性的现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应设计为具有非结构化不确定性的运载器控制提供了统一架构，具有良好的通用性和增强的控制性能。

Abstract: Control with unstructured uncertainties refers to controlling systems where not only the parameters are unknown, but also the way the parameters appear in the dynamics. This problem becomes pivotal in autopilots, where a unified control architecture is sought for aerial/ground/marine vehicles with different structures. By only making use of basic Euler-Lagrange properties valid in most mechanical systems independently of their specific structure, this brief proposes an adaptive design that does not rely on structural knowledge of the uncertainties. The proposed adaptive method, here validated in the ArduPlane module of ArduPilot, applies also to other modules like ArduCopter, ArduRover, ArduSub. Enhanced performance with respect to state-of-the-art methods addressing unstructured and state-dependent uncertainties is verified.

</details>


### [71] [A Cooperative Implementation of Mesh Stability in Vehicular Platoons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28953)
*Meng Qiu,Di Liu,He Wang,Wenwu Yu,Simone Baldi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究网联自动驾驶车辆中的网格稳定性（mesh stability，即二维弦稳定性）问题，提出了一种协作式方案，通过车对车通信增强车载感知，在纵向和横向控制中分别提出新的非相同控制增益协议，显著优于现有分散式方法，并验证了对通信延迟和执行器时滞的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网格稳定性研究大多依赖分散式控制（仅依靠车载传感），性能有限。本文旨在通过引入车对车通信的协作方式，提升纵向和横向控制中的扰动抑制能力，突破分散式方法的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出协作式网格稳定性框架：(1) 纵向控制中设计非相同增益的协作协议，改善控制增益的可扩展性和强弦稳定性；(2) 横向控制中证明非相同增益非必要，提出协作协议实现强弦稳定性；(3) 考虑车对车通信延迟和执行器时滞的鲁棒性设计，并通过CarSim仿真验证。

Result: 协作式协议显著优于现有分散式非相同协议；纵向控制中增益可扩展性和强弦稳定性均有提升；横向控制实现了另一种强弦稳定性概念；数值实验和CarSim仿真验证了协议在延迟和时滞条件下的鲁棒性与有效性。

Conclusion: 通过车对车通信增强车载传感的协作式控制策略，能够大幅提升网联自动驾驶车辆编队的网格稳定性性能，并为实际部署中的通信延迟与执行器时滞提供了鲁棒性保障。

Abstract: This work studies the problem of mesh stability in connected and automated vehicles. Mesh stability, also known as 2D string stability, refers to studying how disturbances propagate in vehicular platoons in both longitudinal and lateral direction. As opposed to available decentralized results only relying on on-board sensing, the distinguishing feature of this work is a cooperative version of mesh stability, where onboard sensing is augmented by vehicle-to-vehicle communication. This cooperative version dramatically improves the state-of-the-art decentralized performance: for longitudinal control, a new cooperative non-identical protocol (i.e. with non-identical control gains) is proposed that improves the state-of-the-art decentralized non-identical protocol in terms of scalability of the control gains and strong notion of string stability. For lateral control, after showing that the non-identical approach is not necessary, a cooperative non-identical protocol is proposed to achieve another strong notion of string stability. Robustness of the proposed implementation against vehicle-to-vehicle communication delays and actuation time lags is considered. Numerical experiments, also performed with the vehicle simulator CarSim, validate the robustness and effectiveness of the proposed protocol.

</details>


### [72] [A Configurable Thermal-Dynamic Model for AI Data Center Cooling Load Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28962)
*Cletus Ngwerume,Lang Tong,Chee-Wooi Ten,Yi Hu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可配置的混合风冷/液冷AI数据中心热动态仿真模型，用于生成功率系统长期研究所需的动态冷却电力曲线。该模型在Marconi100超算上验证，MAE从95.80kW降至20.88kW，RMSE从109.79kW降至27.27kW，能更好地复现日峰值需求和日内波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI数据中心的冷却用电是重要且灵活的组成部分，但缺乏时间同步的测量数据，且恒定COP（性能系数）模型无法表征热动态特性，难以支撑功率系统研究。

Method: 提出一种可配置的热动态仿真模型，涵盖混合空气与液体冷却架构，能够生成动态冷却电力曲线，适用于长时间尺度的功率系统研究；采用Marconi100超算的实际运维遥测数据进行验证。

Result: 与基线相比，MAE从95.80kW降至20.88kW，RMSE从109.79kW降至27.27kW。在约520个日曲线评估中，日峰值需求与日内变异性的复现能力均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一种计算高效且物理可解释的手段，用于生成冷却负荷曲线，可支撑功率系统相关研究。

Abstract: Cooling demand constitutes a significant and flexible component of AI data center electricity consumption, but time-synchronized measurements are scarce and constant coefficient-of-performance models cannot represent thermal dynamics. This letter proposes a configurable thermal dynamic simulation model for hybrid air- and liquid-cooled data centers. Unlike existing models centered on temperature prediction or equipment-level cooling analysis, the proposed model is designed to generate dynamic cooling electricity profiles for long-duration power system studies. The model is validated using operational telemetry from the Marconi100 supercomputer. Compared with the baseline, the proposed model reduces the mean absolute error from 95.80 to 20.88~kW and the root-mean-square error from 109.79 to 27.27~kW. Evaluation over approximately 520 daily profiles further shows improved reproduction of daily peak demand and intraday variability. The proposed model provides a computationally tractable means of generating physically interpretable cooling load profiles for power system studies.

</details>


### [73] [An ArcGIS Framework for Mapping Human-Centered Noise Annoyance for AAM Infrastructure Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29017)
*Swapnil Saha,Zubin Mistry,Neelakshi Majumdar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于GIS的框架，将eVTOL航空器的声学输出转化为居民高度烦恼百分比(%HA)地图，并应用于阿肯色州西北部的医疗配送航线案例，以支持社会可持续的先进空中交通(AAM)基础设施规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AAM的成功实施高度依赖公众接受度，而噪声是低空eVTOL运行的主要关注点。现有研究多使用A计权声级和昼夜平均声级等声学指标描述噪声，未能充分连接声学评估与人类主观反应（如烦恼度），因此需要一个将声学输出转化为人口烦恼响应空间分布的框架来支持更合理的航线规划。

Method: 本研究构建了一个基于GIS的框架，将eVTOL声学输出转化为表示高度烦恼人口百分比(%HA)的地图，应用于阿肯色州西北部一条具有代表性的医疗配送航线。框架集成了人口普查数据以估算高度烦恼个体数量并识别空间影响热点，随后将噪声-烦恼结果与航线距离及空域因素相结合，用于评估替代航线并识别平衡的航路策略。

Result: 结果表明噪声和烦恼度通常随距航线距离的增加而降低；最高烦恼度出现在下降阶段，其次为爬升和巡航阶段。通过人口普查数据集成，成功估算出高度烦恼个体数量并识别出空间影响热点，实现了替代航线的比较和平衡路线的选择。

Conclusion: 该框架将声学评估与人类响应联系起来，支持识别噪声敏感区域、比较航线备选方案以及社会可持续的AAM基础设施规划，为低空城市空中交通的规划和决策提供了更具人文关怀的分析工具。

Abstract: Advanced Air Mobility (AAM) represents a transformative shift in urban transportation; however, successful implementation depends strongly on public acceptance, with noise emerging as a major concern for low-altitude electric vertical takeoff and landing (eVTOL) operations. Existing studies commonly describe eVTOL noise using acoustic metrics such as A-weighted sound level and day-night average sound level. This study develops a Geographic Information System (GIS)-based framework that translates eVTOL acoustic outputs into maps representing the percentage of the population that is highly annoyed (%HA) for a representative medical delivery route in Northwest Arkansas. The results show that noise and annoyance generally decrease with distance from the route, while the highest annoyance occurs during descent, followed by climb and cruise. This paper investigates the practical influence of the parameterization of the individual controllers on the performance of the hierarchical flexibility provision in three case studies. One case study includes a two-level controller cascade acting on one medium and two low voltage grids, and the other one includes a three-level cascade acting on low to high voltage levels. The results show that the behavior of one controller is highly dependent on the choice of control parameters of the other controllers in the cascade. Additionally, the choice of parametrization has a significant impact on the accuracy and speed of flexibility provision. Abstract: Interturn short circuits are among the most critical faults in permanent magnet synchronous motor drives, as they combine localized heating in the shorted stator phase with electrical asymmetry that distorts the current feedback used for torque-producing control. This article proposes a control-based mitigation method enabling the post-fault operation of standard three-phase motor drives without additional dedicated hardware. Using the diagnostic features inferred from standard control-loop signals, the method augments the field-oriented control structure with two mechanisms: resistive-loss-limited current-reference generation and reconstruction of the torque-producing current components in the feedback path. The reference generator is derived from a discrete-time post-fault model and minimizes resistive losses, whereas the feedback reconstruction provides fault-free torque-producing current components as controlled variables of the current loop. The experimental validation has demonstrated reductions of up to 23-36% in the fault-induced increase in the input power and 18-27% in the local segment-loss increase, while confirming real-time adaptation to progressive fault aggravation emulated by stepped changes in the short circuit resistance.

### [79] [Fleming-Viot Selection of the Yaglom Limit for Age-Structured Bellman-Harris Processes, with Application to Livestock Epidemic Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29251)
*Ouerdia Arezki,Paul-Marie Grollemund,Ali Zemouche*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文为亚临界Bellman-Harris过程构建了Fleming-Viot粒子系统，证明其以多项式速率选择Yaglom极限。由于寿命非指数分布，群体规模非马尔可夫，分析须在年龄构型空间上进行。作者建立了强于经典结果的Yaglom定理：条件分布在全变差范数下以指数速率收敛，衰减率由Malthusian参数给出。同时证明了漂移条件在自然Lyapunov函数类中的必要性，并通过牲畜流行病监测的应用展示了估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Galton-Watson过程的Fleming-Viot粒子系统研究已成熟，但Bellman-Harris过程由于寿命分布非指数，群体规模过程不再具有马尔可夫性，使得标准的Lyapunov函数方法失效（不再具有norm-like性质）。因此需要发展新的理论框架来处理这一类更一般的分支过程。

Method: 在年龄构型空间上构建Fleming-Viot粒子系统，利用年龄结构来弥补群体规模非马尔可夫性的缺陷。通过设计适合年龄配置的Lyapunov函数，建立耦合与漂移条件（将风险率与后代分布联系起来），从而证明条件分布的全变差收敛速度，并在自然函数类中证明漂移条件的必要性。

Result: (1) 证明了粒子系统以多项式速率选择Yaglom极限；(2) 建立了强化的Yaglom定理：条件分布在全变差范数下以指数速率收敛，衰减率由Malthusian参数给出；(3) 证明了漂移条件在自然Lyapunov函数类中是必要的，表明这是测度值提升的固有特征而非估计缺陷；(4) 通过牲畜流行病监测中的变化检测检验应用进行了说明。

Conclusion: 该工作将Fleming-Viot粒子系统方法从Galton-Watson过程推广到更一般的Bellman-Harris过程，克服了非马尔可夫性带来的理论困难，并证明了所得结果是本质上最优的（漂移条件的必要性），同时具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct a Fleming-Viot particle system for a class of subcritical Bellman-Harris processes. We prove that it selects the Yaglom limit at a polynomial rate in the number of particles. Since lifetimes are non-exponential, the population size is not Markov, and the analysis must therefore be carried out on the space of age configurations. In this setting, the Lyapunov functions used for Galton-Watson processes are no longer norm-like. Nevertheless, we establish a Yaglom theorem that strengthens the classical result: the conditional laws converge in total variation at an exponential rate, with decay rate given by the Malthusian parameter. We also prove that the drift condition, which links the hazard rate to the offspring law, is necessary within a natural class of Lyapunov functions, showing that it is a feature of the measure-valued lift rather than a defect of the estimates. Finally, we illustrate the estimator through an application to livestock epidemic surveillance, where the Yaglom limit is the null distribution of a change-detection test.

</details>

Method: 提出一种凸鲁棒优化模型，通过最坏情况下的电量状态约束来强制执行有界的需求不确定性，并与"到站即充"基准策略对比，使用瑞士四个包含7-35辆公交车的实际场站运营时刻表进行验证。

Result: 小型场站从优化中获得最大相对收益，总电费降幅超过50%；所有场站的节省随电价波动性增加而增加；优化可将所需电网容量降低40%以上；针对10%能源需求偏差的鲁棒保护使总电费增加不到0.1%。

Abstract: Depot charging of electric bus fleets must minimize electricity costs, respect grid limits, and remain feasible despite uncertain trip energy demand. While cost-optimal charging is well studied, its value under different electricity prices and grid connection capacities, as well as the economic cost of robustness, remain poorly quantified. We address these gaps with a convex robust formulation in which bounded demand uncertainty is enforced through worst-case state-of-energy constraints. The formulation is evaluated against charge-on-arrival using realistic service schedules for four Swiss depots containing 7-35 buses. In the depots studied, smaller depots achieve the greatest relative benefit from optimization, with total electricity cost reductions exceeding 50%, because optimization mitigates charging peaks that strongly affect their costs. For all depot sizes, the savings from optimization increase with electricity price volatility. Optimization can also lower the grid capacity required for feasible operation by over 40%, although tight limits reduce peak shaving potential. Protection against energy-demand deviations of 10% increases total electricity cost by less than 0.1%. The resulting charging power profiles exhibit interpretable price-threshold and peak-shaping behavior, providing practical guidance for real-world implementations.

</details>


### [81] [Directional Conformal Uncertainty Quantification from Learned Model Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29344)
*Cesare Donati,Fabrizio Dabbene,Martina Mammarella*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于控制系统中基于物理的预测器误差量化的共形预测框架。该方法利用数据驱动的差异估计来定义非对称非一致性分数，使得与学习到的差异一致的误差比相反方向的同等大小误差受到更小的惩罚。生成的集合保持在名义模型的误差坐标中，且具有物理一致性（包含原点处的球）。该构造对差异模型类型无关，在可交换性条件下保持有限样本边际有效性，并在可表征的状态-输入区域上可证明地收窄区间。对于RKHS模型，证明了幂函数可作为自适应分数设计的局部置信度度量，并通过Minkowski-gauge分数扩展到多变量情形，生成联合校准的扰动集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在控制系统中，物理基础预测器（简单模型）常因计算效率、综合认证等需求而被选用，但这类模型不是为了最大化预测精度而设计的，其误差同时包含过程噪声和状态相关的系统性偏差（discrepancy）。现有的误差量化方法未能有效区分这两种误差来源，需要一种既能利用数据驱动的差异估计，又能在名义模型坐标下提供有效且物理一致的误差集合的方法。

Method: 1) 将预测器误差分解为过程噪声和状态依赖性差异；2) 利用数据驱动的差异估计定义非对称非一致性分数；3) 通过共形预测框架构造误差集合，集合包含原点处的球（物理一致性）；4) 方法对差异模型（核、神经网络等）类型无关；5) 在可交换性条件下证明有限样本边际有效性；6) 对RKHS模型利用幂函数提供局部置信度用于自适应分数设计；7) 通过Minkowski-gauge分数扩展到多变量情形，生成联合校准的扰动集。

Result: 该框架保持了有限样本边际有效性（在可交换性假设下），在可表征的状态-输入区域上可证明地收窄误差区间，对差异模型选择具有通用性，并成功推广到多变量扰动集的联合校准场景。

Conclusion: 该工作为控制系统中的物理基础预测器提供了一种理论保证完善、计算可行的误差量化工具，兼顾了物理一致性和统计有效性，在简单模型与安全保障之间架起了桥梁。

Abstract: We propose a conformal prediction framework for quantifying the error of physics-based predictors used in control, where simple models are preferred for synthesis, certification, and real-time use. Because these models are selected for compatibility with the intended application rather than for maximal predictive accuracy, their error combines process noise with a state-dependent discrepancy. A data-driven discrepancy estimate defines an asymmetric nonconformity score: errors consistent with the learned discrepancy are penalized less than equally large in the opposite direction. The sets remain in the nominal model's error coordinates and are physics-consistent, i.e., they contain a ball at the origin. The construction is agnostic to the discrepancy model (kernel, neural-network, or other), preserves finite-sample marginal validity under exchangeability, and provably narrows the interval over a characterizable state-input region. We further show that, for RKHS models, the power function provides a local confidence measure for adaptive score design and we extend the construction to the multivariate case via a Minkowski-gauge score yielding a jointly calibrated disturbance set.

</details>


### [82] [Memristor-Based Pulse Width Modulation Circuit for Power Converters with Programmable Frequencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29399)
*Fanfu Wu,Xiaoyi Lei,Yunting Liu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于忆阻器的可编程PWM电路，工作频率约200kHz，用于开关电源转换器控制。实验验证实现了144.7kHz至204.2kHz的可编程开关频率，与常用DSP实现相比功耗降低92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻器在神经形态和存内计算中可降低高达1000倍能耗，但尚未完全集成到电源转换器控制中。现有方案要么将忆阻器输出转换为数字信号后由DSP生成PWM（重新引入计算开销），要么使用可编程性受限的模拟PWM电路。由于PWM生成是电源转换器控制的最终关键环节，需要可编程的忆阻器基PWM电路来充分实现忆阻计算的能效优势。

Method: 该设计将数字控制器的可编程性与模拟PWM发生器的低功耗相结合。使用商用忆阻器构建可编程PWM电路，工作在约200kHz频率用于开关电源转换器。

Result: 实验验证使用商用忆阻器实现了144.7kHz至204.2kHz的可编程开关频率。与电源转换器控制中常用的DSP实现相比，功耗降低了92%。

Conclusion: 该忆阻器基可编程PWM电路成功弥合了忆阻计算与电源转换器控制之间的集成鸿沟，在保持可编程性的同时实现了显著的功耗降低。

Abstract: Memristors can achieve up to a 1000-times reduction in energy consumption in neuromorphic and in-memory computing, but their integration into power converter control remains incomplete. Existing implementations either convert memristor outputs into digital signals for DSP-based pulse-width modulation (PWM), reintroducing computational overhead, or use analog PWM circuits with limited programmability. Since PWM generation is the final essential stage of power converter control, a programmable memristor-based PWM circuit is needed to fully realize the energy-efficiency benefits of memristive computing. This paper presents a programmable memristor-based PWM circuit operating at approximately two hundred kilohertz for switching power converters. The design combines digital-controller programmability with the low power consumption of analog PWM generators. Experimental validation using commercially available memristors achieves a programmable switching frequency from 144.7 kHz to 204.2 kHz. Compared with a commonly used DSP implementation in power converter control, the proposed design achieves a 92% reduction in power consumption.

</details>


### [83] [Hybrid Virtual Space Vector Modulation With Extended Power Factor Range for Coupled Eight-Switch Three-Phase Three-Level Inverter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29416)
*Xiaojun Deng,Hongliang Wang,Tianfu Sun,Yang Jiang,Yibo Si,Wenbo Tang,Mingrui Yang,Xiumei Yue*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对耦合八开关三相三电平逆变器（TP-TLI）在光伏和电机驱动应用中功率因数范围受限的问题，提出了一种混合虚拟空间矢量调制（HVSVM）策略，将功率因数角范围从约[-30°, 30°]扩展到[-90°, 90°]，并设计了基于双系数的主动中点电压控制（NPVC）方案，通过3 kVA实验样机验证了其正确性与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合八开关三相三电平逆变器因其拓扑优势在光伏和电机驱动领域受到越来越多关注，但现有调制策略仅适用于高功率因数工况，无法满足光伏和电机驱动中宽功率因数范围的应用需求，因此需要提出一种能够扩展功率因数范围的调制策略。

Method: 本文首先深入分析了功率因数受限的机理；然后每个扇区构造两个虚拟矢量，并根据虚拟小矢量使用数量提出三种具有不同功率因数范围的虚拟空间矢量调制（VSVM）；通过检测三相电流极性将三种VSVM组合形成HVSVM。此外，利用两个系数调整两对冗余小矢量的驻留时间，实现主动中点电压控制，该控制同时考虑中点电流和中点电压导数，控制精度更高。

Result: 所提出的HVSVM将功率因数角范围从约[-30°, 30°]扩展到[-90°, 90°]，主动NPVC能准确控制中点电压。通过3 kVA实验样机验证了所提方法的正确性和可行性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的HVSVM显著扩展了耦合八开关三相三电平逆变器的功率因数适用范围，配合主动中点电压控制策略，使该拓扑能够更好地满足光伏和电机驱动等实际应用需求。

Abstract: With the development of photovoltaic (PV) and motor drive, the coupled eight-switch three-phase three-level inverter (TP-TLI) is receiving more attentions. However, the previous modulation strategies for coupled eight-switch TP-TLI are suitable for high power factor (PF), which cannot satisfy the requirement for PV and motor drive. Aiming at this, this paper proposes a hybrid virtual space vector modulation (HVSVM) for the coupled eight switch TP-TLI with extended PF range. The mechanism of PF limitation is analyzed in deep. Two virtual vectors are constructed in each sector, and three basic VSVMs with different PF range are proposed according to the number of virtual small vectors used. Combined with three VSVMs, the HVSVM is proposed by detecting the three-phase current polarities. The PF angle range of the HVSVM is extended from about [-30°, 30°] to [-90°, 90°]. Besides, an active neutral-point voltage control (NPVC) is also presented by using two coefficients to adjust the dwell times of the two pairs of redundant small vectors. This approach considers the NP current and the derivation of NPV, and thereby it is accurate to control the NPV. The correctness and feasibility of the proposed HVSVM and active NPVC are verified by experiments through a 3 kVA prototype.

</details>


### [84] [Optimal shaping filter design for data-driven feedforward controller tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29480)
*Yusuke Fujimoto*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了二自由度控制系统中的数据驱动模型匹配问题，重点设计前馈控制器。作者提出了一种最优整形滤波器来补偿直接数据辨识控制器与原问题之间的差距，并通过数值示例和实际实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二自由度控制系统中，从输入输出数据设计前馈控制器时，直接基于数据辨识最优控制器并不能精确解决原始模型匹配问题，两者之间存在差距，需要整形滤波器进行补偿。

Method: 在合理假设下，利用可获取的信息构造最优整形滤波器以弥补数据驱动辨识控制器与精确解之间的差距；同时与ERIT方法进行了理论对比分析。

Result: 通过数值示例和实际实验验证了所提整形滤波器的有效性；此外证明ERIT在特殊情况下是该最优整形滤波器的一个特例。

Conclusion: 本文给出了数据驱动模型匹配问题中补偿控制器辨识误差的最优整形滤波器，并在理论上阐明了其与ERIT的关系，为二自由度控制系统中的数据驱动前馈控制器设计提供了更精确的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper discusses the data-driven model matching problem. In particular, this paper focuses on two-degree-of-freedom control systems, and consider to design feedforward controller from input-output data. An intuitive solution to this problem would be identifying the optimal controller using data, but this does not give the exact solution to the original problem. A shaping filter is required to compensate for this gap, and the main contribution of this paper is to give the optimal shaping filter. The proposed shaping filter is constructed from available information under reasonable assumptions, and its effectiveness is shown through a numerical example and a practical experiment. The relation between the proposed shaping filter and Estimated Response Iterative Tuning (ERIT) is also discussed, and it is shown that ERIT is optimal for a special case.

</details>


### [85] [Quantum Inspired QUBO Assisted ALNS for Reliability Driven Hurricane Restoration of Distribution Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29544)
*Hooman Torkaman,Jignesh Solanki,Anurag Srivastava*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种量子启发式QUBO辅助的自适应大邻域搜索(ALNS)框架，用于台风后配电系统恢复调度。在IEEE 123节点测试馈线上，100 m/s风灾场景下较传统ALNS降低SAIDI和ENS 2.24%，将恢复完工时间缩短50.71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 台风后配电系统恢复需要快速安排修复调度，该问题受馈线拓扑、现场物流和电气可行性约束，是一个复杂的组合优化问题。传统方法在大规模损坏场景下计算效率低、解质量差。

Method: 采用量子启发式QUBO辅助的ALNS框架。每个恢复阶段，局部CPU模拟退火采样器对带电前沿附近的单个维修任务和多任务组合进行排序；确定性解码器维护卡车物流约束，确保充分的劳动力利用率并拒绝不可行批次；最终调度通过OpenDSS电力系统仿真进行验证。

Result: 在IEEE 123节点测试馈线（无分布式发电）上，针对80、90和100 m/s风速场景进行评估。在100 m/s压力测试中，相比经典ALNS，平均SAIDI和ENS降低2.24%，恢复完工时间(makespan)降低50.71%。QUBO辅助在严重损坏导致更大组合修复空间时价值最大。

Conclusion: QUBO辅助的ALNS框架在配电系统灾后恢复调度中有效，尤其在严重损坏场景下优势显著。量子启发式方法能更好地应对大规模组合优化问题，为配电系统韧性提升提供新思路。

Abstract: Post hurricane distribution system restoration requires rapid repair scheduling subject to feeder topology, field logistics, and electrical feasibility. This paper presents a quantum inspired quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) assisted adaptive large neighborhood search (ALNS) framework. At each restoration stage, a local CPU simulated annealing sampler ranks individual repairs and multi job combinations near the energized frontier. A deterministic decoder preserves crew truck logistics, enforces full useful crew utilization, and rejects infeasible batches. Final schedules are validated through OpenDSS replay. The framework is evaluated on the IEEE 123 node test feeder without distributed generation under 80, 90, and 100 m/s wind scenarios. In the 100 m/s stress test, the proposed method reduces mean system average interruption duration index and energy not supplied by 2.24% and restoration makespan by 50.71% relative to classical energized ALNS. Results show that QUBO assistance is most valuable when severe damage creates a larger combinatorial repair space.

</details>


### [86] [Profit-Driven Pricing and SLA-Aware Reserve Sizing for Multi-Tenant Satellite O-RAN Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29594)
*Manobendu Sarker,Gunes Karabulut Kurt,Wael Jaafar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究卫星开放无线接入网（O-RAN）批发场景下的多租户资源分配问题，提出联合定价与储备分配优化方案，通过确定性重构和交替优化算法实现接近最优的利润（差距约1%）且计算速度快22倍，同时满足SLA约束并提升30%资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在卫星O-RAN批发场景中，异构流量类别共享时变的有限卫星容量，同时面临定价（经济维度）和储备分配（可靠性维度）的耦合瓶颈。租户需求具有价格弹性且服务需求随机，并受严格SLA约束，现有方法难以同时协调经济与可靠性目标。

Method: 将随机SLA约束通过确定性重构近似为可处理的边际约束；将问题建模为跨租户、时间窗口和服务类别的非凸联合优化问题；开发交替优化（AO）方案，将定价与分配决策分离求解，同时保持可行性和SLA保证。

Result: 所提方法达到全局基准约1%以内的近最优利润，运行时间最多减少22倍；基线方案利润损失超15%或违反SLA约束；所提方法持续保持非正的经验SLA差距，相比无自适应储备控制的价格优化基线，资源利用率提升高达30%。

Conclusion: 联合经济与资源控制使服务提供商能够高效利用稀缺的卫星网络容量，同时保证可靠的服务交付和可扩展的计算性能，验证了经济决策与资源管理协同优化的价值。

Abstract: This paper studies a multi-tenant resource allocation problem in a satellite open radio access network (O-RAN) wholesale setting, where heterogeneous traffic classes share a time-varying limited satellite capacity over a finite planning horizon. We formulate a joint pricing and reserve allocation problem from a service provider perspective, where tenant-specific demand exhibits price elasticity and stochastic service requirements subject to strict service-level agreement (SLA) constraints, leading to a coupled economic and reliability-driven bottleneck. A deterministic reformulation is adopted to approximate probabilistic SLA requirements through tractable margin constraints, enabling coordinated control of horizon-wide contract prices and time-varying reserves. The resulting problem is non-convex due to interdependent decisions across tenants, time windows, and service classes. To address this, an alternating optimization (AO) scheme is developed separating pricing and allocation decisions while preserving feasibility and SLA guarantees. Numerical results show that the proposed method achieves near-optimal profit within approximately $1\%$ of a global benchmark, while reducing runtime by up to $22\times$. In contrast, considered baseline schemes incur profit losses exceeding $15\%$ or fail to satisfy SLA constraints. The proposed approach consistently maintains non-positive empirical SLA gaps and achieves up to $30\%$ higher resource utilization than a price-optimization baseline without adaptive reserve control. These results demonstrate that joint economic and resource control enables the provider to efficiently exploit scarce satellite network capacity with reliable service delivery and scalable computation.

</details>

### [87] [Effort-Centric Fairness in Lending Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28847)
*Shiqi Fang,Zexun Chen,Jake Ansell*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以"努力"（effort）为中心的信贷评分公平性框架，测量被拒申请人跨过审批边界所需的最小加权成本，揭示预测性公平标准掩盖的"掩藏不平等"（masked inequality），并通过特征无关与因果正则化减少努力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测性公平标准（predictive-parity）仅评估决策点上的结果，无法捕捉被拒申请人在未来获得批准过程中承受的不平等负担。算法信贷评分必须同时满足公平性与可解释性要求，但现有标准存在盲区。

Method: 将申请人努力定义为跨过审批边界所需可行改变的最小加权成本；区分特征无关动作与通过因果模型传播的可加结构变化；按保护群体比较平均最小努力定义公平；为一般可微分类器推导局部表达式，为逻辑回归推导精确表达式，嵌入处理中公平目标，并约束组合信用风险变化。

Result: 基于抵押贷款数据，发现即使满足标准预测性公平标准，被拒女性申请人仍需更大努力；特征无关正则化可将努力差距降低50%以上且预测变化较小；因果正则化在测试的正惩罚权重下降低90%以上，但预测与风险收益权衡更大。特征无关正则化下预期与非预期损失保持稳定，因果正则化下增加；RAROC下降但仍为正。

Conclusion: 努力公平（effort parity）补充预测性公平，揭示并缓解未来信贷准入的隐藏障碍，同时使相关运营权衡显性化。

Abstract: Algorithmic credit scoring must satisfy fairness and explanation requirements, yet prevailing predictive-parity criteria assess only outcomes at the decision point. They can therefore overlook whether rejected applicants face unequal burdens in reaching future approval, a phenomenon we call masked inequality. We develop an effort-centric framework that measures an applicant's effort as the minimum weighted cost of feasible changes required to cross the approval boundary. The framework distinguishes feature-independent actions from additive structural shifts that propagate through a causal model and defines parity by comparing average minimum effort across protected groups. We derive tractable local expressions for general differentiable classifiers and exact expressions for logistic regression, embed them in an in-processing fairness objective, and bound changes in portfolio credit risk. The same optimisation yields actionable pathways to approval. Using mortgage data with continuous and discrete features, we find that rejected female applicants require greater effort even when standard predictive-parity criteria are satisfied. Feature-independent regularisation reduces the effort gap by more than 50\% with modest predictive changes. Causal regularisation yields reductions above 90\% at the tested positive penalty weights, but with larger predictive and risk-return trade-offs. Expected and unexpected losses remain broadly stable under feature-independent regularisation and increase under causal regularisation; RAROC declines but remains positive. These results show that effort parity complements predictive fairness by revealing and mitigating hidden barriers to future credit access while making the associated operational trade-offs explicit.

</details>

### [88] [OpenClaw and Ollama in Agentic AI: Toward Fully Autonomous and Scalable AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28629)
*Konstantinos I. Roumeliotis,Ranjan Sapkota*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于Agentic AI的分层统一架构，将系统划分为推理层、编排层和执行层，并通过OpenClaw-Ollama原型系统验证了该架构的有效性，证明持久记忆、工具使用和自适应决策等能力源自系统级集成而非单一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从反应式LLM向持久化、可执行系统的快速转变暴露了Agentic AI架构理解上的关键空白，特别是在推理、编排和执行层的分离方面，缺乏统一的框架来设计和评估全栈Agentic系统。

Method: 提出分层的Agentic AI架构，明确划分推理层（LLM）、编排层（agent runtime）和执行层（工具/动作），并基于OpenClaw作为编排层、Ollama作为推理层的组合构建原型系统进行实验验证。

Result: 实验表明持久记忆、工具利用和自适应决策等能力源自系统级集成而非独立模型，且随着架构复杂度增加性能持续提升；同时识别了可扩展性、安全性、隐私、治理和评估方面的挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Agentic AI的统一架构基础，验证了全栈自主AI智能体的有效性，并为构建可扩展、安全、可信的Agentic系统提供了路线图，同时公开所有模型、代码和数据集以支持可复现性和基准测试。

Abstract: The rapid transition from reactive large language models (LLMs) to persistent, action-capable systems has exposed critical gaps in the architectural understanding of Agentic AI, particularly in separating inference, orchestration, and execution layers for autonomous AI agents. Despite recent advances, unified frameworks for designing and evaluating full-stack agentic systems remain limited. This paper presents a comprehensive, layered architecture for Agentic AI, outlining the evolution from reactive LLM interfaces to persistent, goal-driven autonomous AI agents with memory, planning, and continuous execution. We analyze OpenClaw and Ollama as a full-stack Agentic AI system, where Ollama serves as the LLM inference layer and OpenClaw enables agent runtime orchestration, integrating reasoning, tool use, and action execution. A prototype experimental validation of the OpenClaw-Ollama architecture demonstrates that capabilities such as persistent memory, tool utilization, and adaptive decision-making emerge from system-level integration rather than standalone models, with performance improving consistently as architectural complexity increases. The study further examines challenges in scalability, security, privacy, governance, and evaluation of agentic systems, highlighting the need for robust benchmarking and system-level design. Future directions include scalable multi-agent architectures, distributed autonomous systems, and human-aware Agentic AI frameworks for responsible deployment. Overall, this work establishes a unified architectural foundation for Agentic AI, validates the effectiveness of full-stack autonomous AI agents, and provides a roadmap for building scalable, secure, and trustworthy agentic systems. All models, code, and datasets are publicly released to support reproducibility and benchmarking.

</details>


### [89] [Can AI Evaluate AI Scientists? A Benchmarking Study of Autonomous Research Generation Systems Using Automated Multi-Model Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28631)
*Vaibhava Lakshmi Ravideshik,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个AI科学家系统的自动化同行评审基准协议，利用前沿大语言模型从原创性、科学性、清晰度和重要性四个维度评估论文。对四个主流AI Scientist框架（Sakana AI v1/v2、CycleResearcher、Data-to-Paper）和FARS基准论文共75篇进行评估，发现FARS论文显著优于其他框架，且Gemini与Claude评审一致性高（ρ=0.907），验证了多模型LLM评估的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主科研AI系统有望加速科学发现，但如何评估和比较AI生成论文的质量仍是一个未解决的问题，缺乏标准化的评测基准。

Method: 构建自动化同行评审系统，使用三个前沿LLM（GPT-5.4、Gemini、Claude）作为独立评审者，从原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度和重要性四个维度对论文打分（1-5分制）。对四个AI Scientist框架各运行15个研究提案，生成60篇论文，与15篇FARS基准论文一同评估，并进行评审者间一致性分析。

Result: FARS基准论文在所有评审中显著胜出（平均分2.14-2.47），是次优系统的2倍以上；其他框架得分仅1.00-1.87。Gemini与Claude评审高度一致（ρ=0.907），且与综合评分极强相关（ρ=0.961）；但GPT-5.4与其他评审者一致性弱（ρ≈0.32），表明其采用不同评审标准。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了AI Scientist系统的首个定量基准，证明多模型LLM评审能提供可扩展、一致的自动化科研质量评估框架，为未来自主科研系统的比较和优化奠定了基础。

Abstract: AI Scientist systems capable of autonomous research have the potential to significantly accelerate scientific discovery. However, evaluating and comparing the quality of AI-generated papers remains an open challenge. We propose and implement a rigorous benchmarking protocol using an automated peer-review system that harnesses frontier large language models to assess scientific papers across four core dimensions: originality, scientific rigor, clarity, and significance. We evaluate four leading AI Scientist frameworks: \textit{Sakana AI (v1 & v2)}, \textit{CycleResearcher}, and \textit{Data-to-Paper}. Each framework was run on a consistent set of 15 research proposals published by a commercial autonomous AI scientist company (FARS), generating 60 papers that we evaluate alongside 15 FARS benchmark papers. Using three independent LLM reviewers (GPT-5.4, Gemini, and Claude), we find that FARS benchmark papers significantly outperform all competing frameworks, achieving mean scores of 2.14--2.47 on a 1--5 scale compared to 1.00--1.87 for other systems. Notably, FARS scores are more than 2$\times$ higher than the next-best systems on Gemini and Claude evaluations. We find strong agreement among Gemini and Claude ($ρ$ = 0.907, $p < 0.001$), and both correlate extremely strongly with the synthesis score ($ρ$ = 0.961, $p < 0.001$), validating the reliability of automated evaluation. However, GPT-5.4 exhibits weaker agreement ($ρ\approx 0.32$), suggesting it evaluates papers using different criteria. These results establish the first quantitative benchmark for AI Scientist systems and demonstrate that multi-model LLM evaluation provides a scalable, consistent framework for assessing autonomous research quality.

</details>


### [90] [LLM Framework for Discovering Major Mathematical Conjectures: AI's Quest for the Next Riemann Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28632)
*Alizer Wong,Zixin Zeng,Yi Tan,Wenyuan Li,Xuhang Chen,Xingru Lai,Yang Shi,Liangsi Lu,Yanhui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段pipeline用于重大数学猜想的系统性发现与验证：区域搜索（从局部证据模块出发）、反思性验证（评估基础性、新颖性与潜在重要性）、以及Lean 4和Mathlib中的形式化验证。在二十个候选猜想上实验，全部通过Lean解析和类型检查，未被exact?直接吸收，未被aesop自动解决，且无重复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重大数学猜想仍高度依赖专家直觉，缺乏统一的方法来系统性生成和验证具有实质数学潜力的猜想。

Method: 三阶段pipeline：(1) 区域搜索——从显式局部证据模块中搜索猜想区域；(2) 反思性验证——评估猜想的基础性、新颖性和潜在意义；(3) 形式化验证——在Lean 4和Mathlib中进行形式化验证。目标是发现具有高"问题品味"的数学问题。

Result: 在20个候选猜想上的实验显示：(1) 20/20通过Lean解析和类型检查；(2) 20/20未被exact?直接吸收；(3) 20/20未被aesop自动解决；(4) 无显式重复或近似重复。

Conclusion: 该pipeline展示了从自然语言到形式化检查的稳定链路，通过形式化工具筛选出未被自动定理证明器直接解决的、具有新颖性的数学猜想，为大规模猜想发现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Major mathematical conjectures still depend heavily on expert intuition, so a unified method for the systematic generation and validation of conjectures with substantial mathematical potential remains unavailable. We present a three stage pipeline for major conjecture discovery, with region search from explicit local evidence modules, reflective validation for foundationality, novelty, and potential significance, and formal validation in Lean 4 and Mathlib. The objective is the discovery of mathematical problems with high problem taste, namely problems whose proofs could reorganize the language of a research area and provide durable help to human mathematical research. Experiments on twenty candidates showstable passage from natural language to formal checks, with twenty out of twenty candidates passing Lean parsing and type checking, twenty out of twenty candidates not directly absorbed by exact?,twenty out of twenty candidates not automatically discharged by aesop, and no explicit duplicates or near duplicates.

</details>


### [91] [ThinkReset: Learnable Intermediate Interface Construction for Bounded-Context Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28642)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThinkReset框架，通过可复用的中间接口（interface writeback and reset）来解决长链推理中上下文窗口受限的问题，直接优化重置后的继续求解成功率，在多个长程推理基准上一致提升固定上下文窗口下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链推理虽然能提升复杂问题性能，但存在冗余累积、上下文溢出和错误锚定问题。作者认为在受限上下文窗口下，核心瓶颈不是轨迹压缩或测试时控制，而是缺乏可复用的中间接口来替代被丢弃的历史并支持继续求解。此外，识别出结果奖励驱动的长链强化学习的关键失败模式：当模型在窗口耗尽前未完成求解时，最终答案奖励会鼓励过早猜测而非继续仔细推理。

Method: 提出ThinkReset框架，在文本空间中实现上述观点：(1) 通过interface writeback显式构建可复用的中间接口；(2) 通过reset机制替换丢弃的历史，支持继续求解；(3) 直接优化post-reset continuation success，即重置后继续求解的成功率。

Result: 在多个长程推理基准上，ThinkReset在固定上下文窗口条件下一致地提升了成功率。

Conclusion: 可复用中间接口是解决长链推理上下文受限问题的关键方向，直接优化重置后的继续求解能力比追求完美压缩或测试时控制更有效。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning improves performance on complex problems, but it also introduces redundancy accumulation, context overflow, and error anchoring. We argue that under bounded context windows, the core bottleneck is not trajectory compression or test-time control, but the absence of a reusable intermediate interface that can replace discarded history and support continued solving. We further identify a key failure mode of outcome-reward-driven long-chain reinforcement learning: when the model has not solved the task before the window is nearly exhausted, the final-answer reward encourages premature guessing rather than continued careful reasoning. We propose ThinkReset, a text-space instantiation of this view. ThinkReset explicitly constructs reusable intermediate interfaces through interface writeback and reset, and directly optimizes post-reset continuation success. Across multiple long-horizon reasoning benchmarks, this perspective consistently improves success rates under fixed context windows.

</details>


### [92] [TAPR: Enhancing LLM Performance with a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28657)
*Oliver Savolainen,Emanuele Bastianelli,Hosein Azarbonyad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出任务感知提示重写器（TAPR），利用GRPO强化学习训练一个模型，将用户提示重写为任务优化的提示以提升下游LLM性能，在问答、摘要和算术推理等任务上取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）通常需要精心设计的提示才能充分发挥潜力，这对非专业用户构成了使用障碍。现有方法缺乏自动将用户提示优化为任务特定提示的有效手段，因此需要一个能够自动重写提示以提升下游性能的模型。

Method: 提出TAPR模型，采用GRPO强化学习方法进行训练。奖励信号来自LLM-as-judge对重写后提示及对应任务输出的双重评估。以Phi-4-mini-instruct作为基础模型进行微调。

Result: 在问答（Natural Questions）、算术推理（GSM8K）和摘要等多类任务上，TAPR生成的提示包含更清晰、更具指导性的语言，相较于基础模型在提示重写能力和下游任务准确率上均有持续提升。

Conclusion: TAPR通过强化学习实现了自动化的任务感知提示优化，能够帮助非专业用户获得更好的LLM使用体验，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often require carefully crafted prompts to unlock their full potential, which can be a barrier for non-expert users. This work addresses the challenge by introducing a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter (TAPR), a model that reformulates user prompts into task-optimized prompts with the explicit goal of improving downstream LLM performance. We train TAPR using reinforcement learning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), where rewards are derived from LLM-as-judge evaluations of both the reformulated prompt and the corresponding task output. Experimental results on diverse tasks, such as question answering, summarization, and arithmetic reasoning, show that our method yields consistent gains over base models in prompt rewriting ability. Fine-tuning Phi-4-mini-instruct (as the base model for TAPR) produces prompts that contain clearer and more instructive language, leading to higher accuracy on established benchmarks such as Natural Questions and GSM8K. Our code is available at: https://github.com/OliverSavolainen/task-specific-prompt-rewriter

</details>


### [93] [Empowering Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation with Hybrid Tokenization and Serial-Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28659)
*Yuxuan Hu,Yuhao Wang,Tianbo Huang,Chao Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Lihua Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GenCDSR是一个面向跨域序列推荐的高效生成式框架，通过跨域混合分词机制和串并行解码策略，在保持生成一致性的同时大幅降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法在跨域场景下存在两个关键问题：(1) 分词过程中忽略了跨域协同相关性；(2) 采用波束搜索等低效解码策略，阻碍实时部署。因此需要一种既能捕获跨域协同信息又能高效生成的框架。

Method: (1) 设计跨域混合分词机制与多塔架构，通过层级共享专属码本和细粒度码本联合捕获跨域共性与域间特异性；(2) 提出跨域串并行解码策略，利用层级SID结构实现部分并行生成，显著降低推理延迟同时保持生成一致性。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，与最先进的基线相比，GenCDSR实现了平均1.5%的准确率提升和平均85.1%的推理延迟降低。

Conclusion: GenCDSR证明了在生成式推荐中融合跨域协同信息与高效解码策略是可行且有效的，为CDSR的实时应用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-domain sequential recommendation (CDSR) aims to model users' dynamic interest transitions and sequential patterns across multiple domains. Recently, generative recommendation (GR) has emerged. It first learns semantic identifiers (SIDs) from item semantics and formulates recommendation as autoregressive generation. However, existing methods face two critical issues: (1) they ignore collaborative correlations across domains during tokenization, and (2) they adopt inefficient decoding strategies, such as beam search, during generation, which hinders real-time deployment. To address these limitations, we propose GenCDSR, an effective and efficient generative framework for CDSR. Specifically, we design a cross-domain hybrid tokenization mechanism with a multi-tower architecture to jointly capture cross-domain commonalities and domain-specific distinctions through hierarchical shared-specific and fine-grained codebooks. Furthermore, we develop a cross-domain serial-parallel decoding strategy that leverages the hierarchical SID structure to partially parallelize generation, significantly reducing inference latency while preserving generation consistency. Experiments on three public datasets show that GenCDSR achieves an average accuracy improvement of 1.5 percent and an average inference latency reduction of 85.1 percent compared with state-of-the-art baselines. The implementation code and datasets are available online: https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/RecSys2026_GenCDSR.

</details>


### [94] [An Ontology-Guided, Deduplication-Aware Extraction Layer for Knowledge Graph Construction from Heterogeneous Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28662)
*Vaibhav Dangaich,Kevin Lewis,Kundeshwar Pundalik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个生产级知识图谱抽取层的设计与实现，该系统利用本地托管的Qwen3.5-9B模型和本体引导的抽取策略，将实时文档流（PDF、电子表格、Office、图片等）转换为与正式本体对齐的验证知识图谱，通过五阶段精炼管线将搜索召回率从约70%提升至95%且无错误合并，并修复了七类隐性质量缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在从非结构化文档中抽取实体和关系时虽然流畅但存在不一致性问题：类型词汇表在不同文档间割裂、同一个人以多种名称变体出现、关系重复、以及同名不同人的混淆风险。这些不一致性严重阻碍了从海量文档中构建可靠知识图谱。

Method: 系统从Kafka消费文档元数据，针对PDF、电子表格、Office和图像内容构建专用处理器，采用两轮抽取（两遍式）并使用基于本体微调的本地Qwen3.5-9B模型。核心创新是本体引导的抽取：通过嵌入相似度从图数据库实时检索相关知识本体切片并注入抽取提示，将目录开销降低约94%。抽取结果经过五阶段精炼管线：确定性清洗、跨块合并、第二轮关系抽取、六种无需模型推理的去重算法、以及具有冲突守卫（任何相似度分数都不能覆盖）的嵌入解析子系统。

Result: 在情报语料库上的评估显示：搜索召回率从约70%提升至95%且无误合并，并纠正了七类隐性质量缺陷，包括将源文本截断单个字符的Bug，以及携带标题前缀的实体被系统性重复的问题。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过本体引导抽取与多阶段规则精炼相结合，可以在生产环境中实现高一致性的知识图谱构建，同时大幅降低计算和目录开销，为大规模文档流的LLM抽取提供了可行的工业化方案。

Abstract: Large language models extract entities and relationships from unstructured documents fluently but inconsistently: type vocabularies fracture across documents, the same person surfaces under several name variants, relationships duplicate, and distinct individuals who share a name risk silent conflation. This paper presents the design, implementation, and empirical refinement of a production extraction layer that converts a live document stream into a validated knowledge graph aligned to a formal ontology. The system consumes document metadata from Kafka, routes PDF, spreadsheet, Office, and image content through handlers built for each format, and extracts entities and relationships in two passes using a locally hosted Qwen3.5-9B model tuned on the ontology. Its distinguishing component is ontology-guided extraction: the relevant slice of a curated ontology is retrieved live from a graph database by embedding similarity and injected into the extraction prompt, reducing catalog overhead by about 94 percent relative to static domain slices. Extracted results then pass through a refinement pipeline of five stages: deterministic cleaning, merging across chunks, a second pass for relationships, six deduplication algorithms that require no model inference, and an embedding resolution subsystem whose conflict guard no similarity score can override. Evaluation on intelligence corpora improved search recall from roughly 70 to 95 percent with no false merges, and corrected seven classes of silent quality defect, ranging from a bug that truncated source text by a single character to the systematic duplication of entities that carried title prefixes.

</details>


### [95] [Identifying Informative Environments for Cognition Parameter Inference via Bayesian Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28894)
*Manisha Dubey,Rimvydas Rubavicius,N. Siddharth,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将认知规划实验设计形式化为贝叶斯实验设计（BED）问题，将实验环境作为设计变量，并引入摊销BED框架以实现高效的后验推断和设计评估。在Mouselab-MDP范式上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯逆规划为认知机制推断提供了原则性框架，但其效果严重依赖实验环境。现有方法将环境视为固定不变的，忽视了哪种认知实验环境对参数推断最具信息量这一关键问题。

Method: 将认知规划实验设计形式化为BED问题，以实验环境为设计变量；建立精确蒙特卡洛BED基准；引入摊销贝叶斯实验设计框架用于高效后验推断和设计评估。

Result: 在Mouselab-MDP过程追踪范式上的实验表明，摊销BED与精确蒙特卡洛BED的环境排序高度一致，同时大幅降低计算成本；且没有任何单一环境在所有认知推断目标上统一最优，揭示了三类目标（期望信息增益、后验可恢复性、信息效率）之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 为贝叶斯参数推断设计信息丰富的认知实验提供了原则性框架，并揭示了不同推断目标下环境选择的权衡关系。

Abstract: Computational cognitive modeling seeks to infer latent cognitive mechanisms underlying observed behavior. Bayesian inverse planning provides a principled framework for such inference, but its success depends critically on the experimental environment. Existing approaches typically treat environments as fixed, leaving open the question of which cognitive experiments are most informative for cognition parameter inference. We formulate the design of cognitive planning experiments as a Bayesian Experimental Design (BED) problem, treating the experimental environment as the design variable. We establish an exact Monte Carlo BED benchmark and introduce an amortized Bayesian experimental design framework for efficient posterior inference and design evaluation. Experiments on the Mouselab-MDP process-tracing paradigm show that amortized BED closely matches the environment rankings of exact Monte Carlo BED while substantially reducing computational cost. We further show that no single environment is uniformly optimal across cognitive inference objectives, revealing trade-offs between expected information gain, posterior recoverability, and information efficiency. These results provide a principled framework for designing informative cognitive experiments for Bayesian parameter inference.

</details>


### [96] [How Hard Does It Think? Analyzing Step-Aware Reasoning Energy in LLM Chain-of-Thought Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28674)
*Hui Wei,Junda Wu,Sheldon Yu,Sizhe Zhou,Yizhu Jiao,Ming Zhong,Bowen Jin,Tong Yu,Shijia Pan,Jiawei Han,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SARE（Step-Aware Reasoning Energy）框架，通过相邻Transformer层间token隐状态的Gram矩阵CKA相似度，在单个CoT步骤粒度上量化推理能量，揭示推理步骤间存在非均匀的阶段性转换，且推理能量可在多个基准上超越输出级置信度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法要么依赖输出级信号，要么将处理深度压缩为单个轨迹级标量，无法揭示CoT推理中每个步骤的计算努力分配情况。步骤级的推理努力分配仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: SARE采用几何框架，通过计算相邻Transformer层间token隐状态的Gram矩阵之间的CKA（Centered Kernel Alignment）来量化每个CoT步骤的努力程度，无需特征向量对齐或聚类对应。同时将推理轨迹建模为潜在语义状态间的转换，以语义进展为背景来情境化推理能量。

Result: 在六个推理基准和三个开源LLM上发现：推理能量在步骤类型间高度非均匀，存在轨迹级指标无法捕捉的阶段性转换；错误轨迹在关键推理节点上系统性地表现出更低的能量；SARE特征在大多数设置中匹配或超越输出级置信度基线。

Conclusion: 内部几何动力学编码了超越表面信号的预测信息，SARE为理解CoT推理的逐步计算分配提供了一个细粒度、可解释的新视角。

Abstract: Understanding how computational effort is allocated across individual chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning steps remains an open challenge: existing interpretability methods rely on output-level signals or collapse processing depth into a single trajectory-level scalar, leaving step-wise effort opaque. We propose Step-Aware Reasoning Energy (SARE), a geometric framework that quantifies effort at the granularity of individual CoT steps via Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between Gram matrices of token hidden states across adjacent transformer layers, capturing inter-token relational structure without requiring eigenvector alignment or cluster correspondence. SARE further contextualizes this energy within reasoning's semantic progression by modeling CoT trajectories as transitions among latent semantic states. Across six reasoning benchmarks and three open-weight LLMs, we find that reasoning energy is highly non-uniform across step types, exhibiting phase-like transitions invisible to trajectory-level metrics; incorrect trajectories show systematically lower energy at critical reasoning junctions; and SARE-based features match or outperform output-based confidence baselines in most settings, indicating that internal geometric dynamics encode predictive information beyond surface-level signals.

</details>


### [97] [A Generalized-Bayes Perspective on Counterfactual Explanations: Posterior-Based Decision-Making and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29077)
*Keita Kinjo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从贝叶斯视角重新审视反事实解释（CEs），证明距离最小化的CE等价于广义贝叶斯框架中Gibbs后验的MAP估计，并在此统一框架下引入贝叶斯决策与CVaR-CE两种决策规则，同时提出贝叶斯模型加权以应对模型多重性，最后通过实验量化各决策规则间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释是提升机器学习模型可解释性的重要工具，但传统的距离最小化公式缺乏理论基础，其理论依据未得到充分研究。

Method: 基于距离先验提出Distance-Prior Generalized Bayes CE (DP-GBCE)框架；引入贝叶斯决策和CVaR-CE两种超越MAP的决策规则；使用贝叶斯模型权重混合多个模型的后验分布以处理模型多重性；定义评价单个CE及整体后验分布的指标。

Result: 通过模拟数据和Google Trends数据的实验，量化了不同决策规则之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 距离最小化CE在数学上等价于Gibbs后验的MAP估计，统一的贝叶斯框架为CE提供了坚实的理论基础，并拓展了更丰富的决策规则来满足不同应用需求。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations (CEs) enhance the interpretability of machine learning models by identifying the smallest change to an input required to obtain a desired output. Although CEs are conventionally formulated as a distance-minimization problem, the theoretical basis of this formulation has received limited attention. We show that a distance-minimization-based CE is mathematically equivalent to the maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimate of a Gibbs posterior within the generalized Bayes framework, specifically when a distance-based prior is used. We call this formulation the Distance-Prior Generalized Bayes CE (DP-GBCE). Building on this posterior perspective, we introduce two decision rules beyond MAP within a unified framework: a Bayes decision that minimizes expected decision loss and CVaR-CE, a risk-averse decision rule. We also propose an extension that uses Bayesian model weights to mix the posterior distributions of multiple models, thereby accounting for model multiplicity, where several models have comparable predictive performance. Finally, we define metrics for evaluating both individual CEs and the posterior distribution as a whole, and use experiments on simulated data and Google Trends data to quantify the trade-offs among the decision rules.

</details>


### [98] [Reasoning in Real World Clinical Care: Why Large Language Models Are Not Yet Safe for Autonomous Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28677)
*Shayndhan Sivanathan,Shravan Nageswaran,Mehdi Zadem,Ryaan Sultan,Nicolas von Mallinckrodt,Max Solovyev,Alexey Matyushkin,Sumon Sadhu,Gabriele C DeLuca,Sanjeeva Jeyaretna,James Hillis,Manoj Ramachandran,Prakash Jayakumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇Perspective文章，指出LLM在通过医学执照考试后正被加速应用于自主分诊（autonomous triage）场景，即对未分化、自主就诊的患者进行几乎无临床医生参与的分流决策。作者认为，目前尚未存在支持这一应用的安全性证据，核心缺陷不在于医学知识不足，而在于临床评估的保真度——LLM是为生成最可能的文本而优化，而非为在"不能漏诊"的不对称代价下安全行动而优化。安全的自主分诊本质上是一个不对称代价下的序贯决策问题，需要信息收集、风险容忍度调整和升级处理的能力，而现有评估（多基于完整、精选的模拟数据）难以暴露这些失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已通过医学执照考试，甚至在某些个案中可与医生媲美，这加速了其在症状评估和临床决策支持中的应用。但最需警惕的是自主分诊——即几乎没有临床医生介入、对自我呈现的未分化患者进行分流。作者指出，这一最严重后果的应用目前缺乏安全性证据，存在一个被忽视的鸿沟：模型的知识测试通过不等于临床实践安全，因为临床分诊需要的是在信息不完全、成本不对称条件下做出安全决策，而这与LLM的训练目标（预测最可能的下一段文本）存在根本冲突。

Method: 本文为Perspective（观点性文章），采用论证分析的方法：首先指出自主分诊与传统诊断测试的本质差异——分诊不是选出最可能诊断，而是在不对称代价下的序贯决策（一次灾难性漏诊胜过多次假警报）；其次分析LLM在此任务上的核心缺陷是信息收集的不确定性——在不完整病史下可能无法扩展鉴别诊断、主动追问缺失的红色警示信号、降低升级门槛、延迟判断，或对未排除的高危害诊断保持警惕；最后指出这些失败模式在现有评估体系中难以暴露，因为评估常使用完整、精选、置信度门槛控制的模拟案例，且LLM的顺从性、随和性和校准不良等"助手型"偏差可能放大风险。

Result: 本文的主要"结果"是识别并系统化阐述了LLM在自主分诊场景下的关键安全风险：(1) 现有安全性证据不存在；(2) 失败根源是模型优化目标（文本延续）与分诊任务需求（不对称代价下的安全决策）不匹配；(3) 核心缺陷在于不确定条件下的信息收集能力不足；(4) 现有评估方法（完整病史、精选案例）无法检测这些失败模式；(5) LLM固有的助手型行为（轻信、迎合、过度自信）在无临床分诊逻辑约束时可能放大风险。

Conclusion: 作者认为，虽然LLM在医学知识测试中表现优异，但将其用于无临床医生参与的自主分诊为时过早且证据不足。安全的分诊需要模型具备在信息不完全条件下主动收集信息、对不对称风险做出正确权衡、并在高危害诊断未被排除时升级警惕的能力——这些都不是当前LLM的优化目标所天然支持的。现有评估方法的局限性使得这些缺陷难以被事先发现，因此需要开发更贴近真实分诊场景的评估框架，并认识到LLM的"友好性"偏差在临床高风险场景中可能成为安全隐患而非优势。

Abstract: LLM now pass medical licensing examinations and, in curated cases, can rival physicians at diagnostic reasoning. These developments have accelerated the use of LLMs for symptom assessment and clinical decision support in diagnostic and treatment guidance, administrative documentation, and rules-based alert enhancement. This Perspective concerns the most consequential of these applications: the autonomous triage of self-presenting, undifferentiated patients, with little or no clinician in the loop. For that task, the evidence of safety does not yet exist. The gap is not in medical knowledge but in the fidelity of clinical evaluation: a model optimized to continue the most probable text is not optimized to act safely when the safe answer is the improbable must-not-miss diagnosis. Safe triage is not the selection of the most likely diagnosis; it is a sequential decision under asymmetric cost, in which the single catastrophic miss outweighs many false alarms, and the decisive signal may be one the patient has not volunteered - and that the model has not been trained to seek. The core deficit is therefore one of information gathering under uncertainty. Under incomplete histories, LLM systems may fail to show the behaviors safe triage requires: broadening the differential; seeking the missing red flag; lowering the threshold for escalation; deferring judgement until sufficient information is obtained; and escalating concern where high-harm diagnoses remain unexcluded. These modes of failure for LLMs can be difficult to detect considering that evaluations to date often use complete, well-curated, confidence-gated simulations. The application of LLMs under these conditions may be amplified by assistant-like behaviors and positive bias, including credulity, agreeableness, and miscalibration - when these are not constrained by clinical triage logic.

</details>


### [99] [ViSAGE: Constructing Self-Correcting Memories for Long-Form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28678)
*Xinkui Zhao,Enbo Chen,Yifan Zhang,Chang Liu,Guanjie Cheng,Naibo Wang,Yueshen Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ViSAGE是一个多模态智能体记忆框架，通过跨模态绑定锚定实体身份、双向记忆细化传播延迟的身份证据，以及多智能体交叉验证实现基于身份-证据对齐约束的检索评估与弃权机制，在长时程多模态任务中比最强基线提升5.9%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态智能体记忆方法在长时程环境中存在两个核心问题：(1) 攻击性压缩和分段式处理会丢失细粒度的身份线索；(2) 过度依赖向量相似性检索，可能返回语义相关但身份不匹配的证据，导致实体混淆、错误传播和幻觉回答。

Method: ViSAGE框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 通过跨模态绑定在长时间跨度上锚定实体身份；(2) 采用双向记忆细化机制传播延迟的身份证据，回溯性统一历史记录并改善未来推理；(3) 引入多智能体交叉验证，在身份-证据对齐约束下评估检索到的证据，当证据缺失时允许弃权而非给出无依据的回答。

Result: 在多项实验中，ViSAGE持续优于最强基线方法，准确率提升5.9%。

Conclusion: ViSAGE通过构建自纠正、以实体为中心的记忆，有效解决了长时程多模态任务中身份混淆和幻觉问题，验证了跨模态身份绑定与多智能体验证机制在智能体记忆中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal agents operating in long-horizon environments must build and continually update multimedia memories to support entity-consistent, temporally grounded reasoning. However, existing agentic memory approaches often discard fine-grained dentity cues under aggressive compression and segment-wise processing. They also rely heavily on vector similarity retrieval, which can surface semantically related yet identity-mismatched evidence, leading to entity confusion, error propagation, and hallucinated answers.
  We propose ViSAGE, a multimodal agentic memory framework that constructs self-correcting, entity-centric memories. Specifically, ViSAGE anchors entity identity via cross-modal binding over long temporal ranges. It then applies bidirectional memory refinement to propagate delayed identity evidence, retroactively unifying historical records and improving future reasoning. We also introduce multi-agent cross-verification to assess retrieved evidence under an identity-evidence alignment onstraint, enabling abstention instead of unsupported answers when evidence is missing. Extensive results demonstrate that ViSAGE consistently outperforms the strongest baseline, achieving 5.9% higher accuracy.

</details>


### [100] [Multi-Agent Planning with Spatio-Temporal and Topological Constraints using STL-GO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28679)
*Sheryl Paul,Vidisha Kudalkar,Anand Balakrishnan,Lars Lindemann,Alberto Speranzon,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了满足时空逻辑（STL-GO）约束的多智能体路径规划问题，提出了两种编码方法（基于混合整数规划的MIP和基于可满足性模理论的SMT），并为这两种方法提供了统一的接口，在无人机搜索救援基准上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体规划问题在工程应用中广泛存在（如多机器人灭火、工厂无人机巡检），其难点在于需同时处理时空约束（智能体何时何地做什么）和拓扑约束（智能体间如何交互）。虽然已有多种时空逻辑框架，但STL-GO是一种支持多智能体及其拓扑推理的新形式化方法，其路径规划问题因需要编码多个可能随时间变化的图而极具挑战性。

Method: 本文提出了两种将STL-GO约束的多智能体路径规划问题编码为可求解问题的方法：1）基于混合整数规划（MIP）的编码；2）基于可满足性模理论（SMT）的编码。两种编码均具有可靠性保证。同时，论文提供了一个统一的接口来指定智能体约束、图拓扑和STL-GO规格，支持两种方法的无缝使用和直接比较。

Result: 在多无人机搜索救援基准上对两种编码进行了评估，在不同团队规模和图复杂度下进行消融实验，结果突出了所提编码在动态多图交互下的表达能力。

Conclusion: 本文成功为STL-GO约束下的多智能体路径规划提供了两种可行的求解编码方案，并通过统一接口实现了灵活的方法选择与比较，为处理多智能体系统中复杂时空拓扑约束的规划问题提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Multi-agent planning problems arise in a variety of engineering applications, such as multi-robot wildfire fighting and unmanned aerial inspection in factories. A particular challenge is the existence of spatio-temporal (i.e., when and/or where an agent should do what) and topological constraints (i.e., how agents should interact), as typically formalized via the notion of graphs. Over the last years, various frameworks have been proposed that can capture such constraints via spatio-temporal logics. We focus here on spatio-temporal logic with graph operators (STL-GO), a recent formalism that supports reasoning about multiple agents and their topologies, such as sensing, communication, and task topologies. In this paper, we consider the problem of planning multi-agent paths that satisfy constraints written in STL-GO. This problem is particularly challenging due to the need of encoding multiple, potentially time-varying graphs via the graph operators inherent to STL-GO. We present two encodings of this problem, one based on mixed-integer programming (MIP) and another based on satisfiability modulo theory (SMT), with soundness guarantees. We provide a unified interface for specifying agent constraints, their graph topologies, and the STL-GO specification, enabling seamless use of both methods and facilitating direct comparison between them. We evaluate both encodings on a multi-UAV search-and-rescue benchmark, ablating over team size and graph complexity, highlighting the expressiveness of the proposed encodings under dynamic multi- graph interactions.

</details>


### [101] [Library Reachability in LSR-Synth: How Anti-Memorization Design Changes the Measurement of Symbolic Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28684)
*Zhan'ao Yao,Liang Yin,Zhihao Gao,Boxuan Zhang,Xiaoyu Wu,Linjing Li,Rongyan Wang,Tingwei Chen,Youwei Wang,Xiaolin Zhao,Jiahui Shi,Jianjun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文检验LSR-Synth基准任务能否区分语言模型提供的科学先验与不访问任务语义的常规算子搜索。研究发现固定词汇表已能覆盖大多数任务，语言模型生成的候选仅在词汇覆盖被选择性破坏时才显著扩展可解实例集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学方程发现基准多由公知方程组成，难以判断模型是从数据中发现规律还是从训练语料中记忆答案。LSR-Synth通过引入新合成项缓解了完整公式记忆问题，但作者进一步追问：这些任务能否区分语言模型的科学先验与常规算子搜索的贡献？

Method: 构建一个使用固定词汇、具有公开来源记录的无语义基线，并通过三种方式检验候选覆盖的作用：语义盲化、词汇库削弱、匹配算子家族敲除。同时在严格分布外（OOD）条件下进行评估。

Result: 在当前任务快照、搜索预算和评分协议下，固定词汇已覆盖大多数任务；语言模型生成的候选很少扩展可解实例集，仅在词汇覆盖被选择性破坏时边际贡献显著。严格OOD评估降低所有方法的绝对成功率，但不改变这一关系。

Conclusion: 结论是有限的：大多数当前任务仍适合评估对未见表达式的拟合与重组能力，但不足以独立识别超越固定搜索空间的先验贡献。这既不完全否定LSR-Synth对完整公式记忆的防控，也不暗示语言模型先验普遍无用。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for scientific equation discovery are largely composed of well-known equations available in the public domain, making it difficult to determine whether a model is discovering laws from data or merely recalling answers from its training corpus. LSR-Synth mitigates this problem by introducing novel synthetic terms into established scientific mechanisms and filtering the resulting tasks for novelty, solvability, and scientific plausibility. This paper examines a narrower measurement question: can these tasks further distinguish scientific priors supplied by language models from conventional operator search that does not access task semantics? We construct a semantics-free baseline using a fixed vocabulary with publicly documented provenance, and assess the role of candidate coverage through semantic blinding, library weakening, and matched operator-family knockouts. Under the current task snapshot, search budget, and scoring protocol, the fixed vocabulary already covers most tasks, while language-model-generated candidates rarely expand the set of solvable instances. Their marginal contribution becomes substantial only when vocabulary coverage is selectively disrupted. Strict out-of-distribution evaluation lowers the absolute success rates of all methods but does not alter this relationship. These findings neither invalidate LSR-Synth's controls against memorization of complete formulas nor imply that language-model priors are generally unhelpful. Rather, they support a more limited conclusion: most current tasks remain suitable for evaluating the fitting and recombination of previously unseen expressions, but are insufficient on their own to identify contributions from priors beyond a fixed search space.

</details>


### [102] [Safety, or Just Capability? A Validity Audit of Agent-Safety Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28685)
*Youting Wang,Xiao Han,Dingyan Shang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对四个智能体安全基准（R-Judge, InjecAgent, AgentHarm, AgentDojo）进行了系统的测量验证，在多达22-41个模型上评估其官方实现与评分器。研究发现：二元轨迹判断基准的F1指标存在"总是正向"策略的基线偏差问题；小规模模型面板会导致基准间排名不一致；能力与安全的相关性方向取决于所选择的安全结果指标。论文呼吁安全声明必须明确基准、指标、目标行为和模型面板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体安全基准分数被当作"智能体安全性"的通用指标互相引用，但这些基准实际测量的是不同的行为。作者希望将这些基准视为需要验证的测量工具，检验其在不同模型上的可复现性、一致性和外部效度。

Method: 对四个安全基准（R-Judge, InjecAgent, AgentHarm, AgentDojo）在其官方实现和作者提供的评分器下运行，最多覆盖22个模型；同时用统一协议测量MMLU和GPQA作为能力综合指标。通过F1指标基线分析、不同规模模型面板的相关性比较、留一组织验证和聚类自助法检验结果稳健性。

Result: 1) F1指标存在基线缺陷："总是正向"策略在R-Judge上取得F1=0.690，超过21个有判别力模型中的5个。2) 基准间排名一致性受小面板影响：R-Judge特异度与AgentHarm安全性在n=7时相关系数为-0.64，在n=18时仅为+0.02。3) 能力与安全的关系取决于指标：能力预测任务成功（ρ=+0.60）但与错位安全负相关（ρ=-0.44）。4) 在扩展面板上，错位安全相关性减弱至-0.16，越狱攻击相关性增强至+0.34。5) AgentHarm显示出最强的外部关联（ρ=+0.72），但这是与越狱安全的一致性证据（两者都测量有害合规），而非一般安全性的证据。

Conclusion: 安全基准的分数不能互换使用。安全声明必须明确命名：基准名称、指标、目标行为以及模型面板。评估结果在很大程度上取决于这些选择，小面板不可靠，能力与安全的关系取决于具体测量哪种安全行为。

Abstract: Agent-safety benchmarks measure different behaviors, and their scores get quoted interchangeably as an agent's safety.
