### [217] [Decoupled Probabilistic Forecasting and Arbitrage-Aware Refinement of Implied Volatility Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29220)
*Lifeng Hao,Shaolin Ji*

Main category: q-fin.CP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个解耦的生成式精化框架用于隐含波动率曲面（IVS）预测，分为两个阶段：第一阶段用条件扩散模型学习未来曲面的条件分布，第二阶段引入表面感知注意力模块（SAAM）对代表性曲面进行横截面精化，以同时满足分布学习与静态无套利约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐含波动率曲面预测对期权估值、对冲和风险管理至关重要，但由于未来曲面是随机的，且定价输入必须满足静态无套利形状约束，使得预测十分困难。现有方法难以同时捕捉随机市场动态并保证无套利约束。

Method: 提出解耦的生成式精化框架：第一阶段使用条件扩散模型学习未来曲面的条件分布，生成集合捕获预测分布变化，取中位数作为代表性曲面；第二阶段引入Surface Aware Attention Module（SAAM），一种横截面精化算子，改善对市场观测和静态无套利诊断的拟合。该设计将分布学习与曲面精化解耦。

Result: 在CSI 300指数期权（2020年6月至2024年9月）的日频和分钟频预测协议下评估：扩散阶段提升预测精度并产生随钱性、到期日和采样频率变化的预测区间；精化阶段改善市场观测拟合精度并降低静态无套利残差违规，在分钟频下增益更强。注意力诊断表明SAAM执行自适应横截面精化而非固定局部平滑。

Conclusion: 解耦的两阶段框架能够分别处理随机市场动态学习与静态无套利残差控制，在提高预测精度的同时保证曲面经济约束，是一种有效的运营风险曲面建模方法。

Abstract: Implied volatility surface forecasting is essential for option valuation, hedging,and risk management, but remains difficult because future surfaces are stochastic while pricing inputs must satisfy static no-arbitrage shape restrictions. We propose a decoupled generative refinement framework for IVS forecasting as an operational risk surface modeling problem. The first stage uses a conditional diffusion model to learn the conditional distribution of future surfaces. The generated ensemble captures predictive distributional variation, and its median provides a robust representative surface for subsequent refinement. The second stage introduces a Surface Aware Attention Module (SAAM), a cross sectional refinement operator that improves fit to market observations and staticno-arbitrage diagnostics for the representative surface. This design separates distribution learning from surface refinement, allowing the diffusion model to capture stochastic market dynamics while SAAM controls static no-arbitrage residual violations on the final surface. We evaluate the framework on CSI 300 index options from June 2020 to September 2024 under daily and minute level forecasting protocols. The diffusion stage improves forecasting accuracy and produces predictive intervals that vary across moneyness, maturity, and sampling frequency. The refinement stage improves fitting accuracy against market observations and reduces measured static no-arbitrage residual violations, with stronger gains at the minute level. Attention diagnostics suggest that SAAM performs adaptive cross sectional refinement rather than fixed local smoothing

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [Accelerated Random-Sweep Gibbs Sampling for Gaussian Graphical Models via Dual Normal Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28706)
*Borna Khodabandeh,Mehdi Molkaraie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯图模型中薄膜先验下随机扫描Gibbs采样的收敛性质，发现通过对原模型的因子图进行傅里叶变换得到的对偶模型能显著加速收敛。作者推导了齐次k-正则图的精确收敛速率，并证明含环齐次模型在对偶域中的收敛速率是普适的、与图拓扑无关，且由图的代数连通性决定。文章还建立了原模型与对偶模型协方差结构之间的显式代数关系，使原模型的边际统计量可直接从对偶模型恢复。数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs采样器（特别是随机扫描版本）在高斯图模型中的收敛速度通常依赖于图的结构，这在实际应用中存在收敛慢、难以分析的问题。作者希望通过构建对偶模型来加速收敛，并深入理解其收敛性质。

Method: 1) 对原高斯图模型中正态因子图的局部因子应用傅里叶变换构造对偶模型；2) 推导齐次k-正则图的精确收敛速率；3) 证明含环齐次模型的收敛速率的普适性；4) 建立原模型与对偶模型协方差结构之间的显式代数关系。

Result: 1) 对偶域的收敛速率明显快于原始域；2) 对含环齐次模型，对偶域收敛速率与图拓扑无关（普适性）；3) 有效收敛速率由图的代数连通性决定，不会增加每次扫描的计算复杂度；4) 可以从对偶模型直接恢复原模型的边际统计量；5) 数值实验证实了理论结果的正确性。

Conclusion: 傅里叶变换构造的对偶模型为高斯图模型中Gibbs采样的加速提供了一种理论上有保证且计算上高效的方法，其收敛速率具有普适性并由代数连通性控制，同时保持了恢复原模型统计量的能力。

Abstract: We study the convergence properties of the random-sweep Gibbs sampler for Gaussian graphical models with a thin-membrane prior. We demonstrate that the convergence rate of the Gibbs sampler is significantly accelerated in the dual model, which is obtained by applying the Fourier transform to the local factors of the normal factor graph representing the original model. In both domains, we derive the exact convergence rates for homogeneous $k$-regular graphs. We prove that, for all homogeneous models whose graphical representations contain cycles, the convergence rate in the dual domain is universal and independent of the underlying graph topology. Moreover, we show that the effective convergence rate in the dual domain is governed by the algebraic connectivity of the graph, providing an additional acceleration without increasing the computational complexity per sweep. We further establish an explicit algebraic relation between the covariance structures of the primal and dual models, enabling marginal statistics of the primal model to be recovered directly from those of the dual model. Finally, numerical experiments on several graph families confirm our theoretical results and demonstrate substantial improvements in the convergence rates in various settings.

</details>


### [219] [Conditioning Tree-Based Diffusions and Flows for Probabilistic Tabular Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28864)
*Silas Koemen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffGBM，将基于树的扩散模型用于表格回归。作者发现神经网络扩散模型的默认设计（噪声路径、参数化、训练分布、特征、采样器）是树基扩散模型的瓶颈，并沿两个轴进行改进：(1) 高斯路径流匹配训练器直接学习速度场并代数恢复得分，支持少步确定性ODE采样；(2) 将得分侧的配方（残差化、EDM预条件、log-sigma时间采样等）作为可联合调节的轴。在11个表格基准上，score-flex配置在所有数据集上优于基线（配对Wilcoxon检验显著），流匹配采样器比基线快5.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树基扩散模型在无需神经密度估计器的情况下拟合表格回归的条件预测分布，但它们直接继承了神经网络扩散模型的默认设计选择，这些默认设计是性能的约束瓶颈。作者旨在明确这些设计选择并将其作为可调节的轴来释放树基扩散模型的全部潜力。

Method: DiffGBM包含两个核心贡献：(1) 高斯路径流匹配训练器，直接学习p(y|x)的速度场并代数恢复得分，允许少步确定性ODE采样；(2) score-flex空间，将残差化、EDM风格预条件、log-sigma时间采样、噪声级特征、损失加权和直方图分辨率作为在共享LightGBM表面上可联合调节的轴。

Result: 在11个表格基准上（fold-0调参、folds-1-5评估、40次试验预算），score-flex配置在所有数据集上优于发布基线（配对Wilcoxon检验11/0，p<10⁻³），最佳聚合CRPS技能为0.725 vs 0.699。流匹配是最便宜的采样器（比基线快5.2倍）且校准最好。非扩散基线在个别数据集上仍胜出，随机流采样器不帕累托支配确定性方案。

Conclusion: 树基扩散模型的默认设计选择确实是性能瓶颈，通过将设计选择显式化为可调节轴，DiffGBM在准确性（score-flex）和效率（流匹配）两个维度上都超越了发布基线。但非扩散基线仍具竞争力，且随机采样不总是优于确定性采样，说明扩散模型配方仍需谨慎设计。

Abstract: Tree-based diffusion models fit flexible conditional predictive distributions for tabular regression without a neural density estimator, but they inherit their design defaults---noising path, parameterization, training distribution, features, sampler---from the neural setting. We show these defaults are the binding constraint: what a gradient-boosted ensemble actually solves is a supervised regression problem whose conditioning they determine. We present DiffGBM, which makes them explicit along two axes. First, a Gaussian-path flow-matching trainer for $p(y \mid x)$ that learns a velocity field directly and recovers the score algebraically, admitting few-step deterministic ODE sampling. Second, we expose the score-side recipe---residualization, EDM-style preconditioning, log-sigma time sampling, noise-level features, loss weighting, and histogram resolution---as jointly tunable axes over a shared LightGBM surface rather than one frozen bundle. This \emph{score-flex} space represents the published recipe as a special case; across eleven tabular benchmarks under fold-0 tuning, folds-1--5 evaluation, and a matched 40-trial budget and sampler, the selected configurations beat that baseline on \emph{every} dataset (paired Wilcoxon $11/0$, $p<10^{-3}$), with the best aggregate CRPS skill (0.725 vs.\ 0.699) of any row. The two rows are complementary: score-flex buys accuracy with a stochastic sampler and is the slowest row, while flow matching is the cheapest sampler ($5.2\times$ faster than the published baseline) and the best-calibrated DiffGBM row. Tuned non-diffusion baselines still win individual datasets, and stochastic ($\varepsilon>0$) flow samplers do not Pareto-dominate the deterministic corner.

</details>


### [220] [Structured Neural Chaos: An Adaptive Surrogate Modeling Framework for Functional Uncertainty Quantification and Global Sensitivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28903)
*Isabel Corona Guevara,Yeping Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化神经混沌（sNC）展开作为代理模型框架，用于基于方差的全局敏感性分析（GSA）。该方法借鉴多项式混沌展开（PCE）的正交结构和可解释性，同时利用神经网络的表达力，通过镜像截断的函数ANOVA分解来构建代理模型，每个交互分量采用可分离低秩近似，从而在高维随机输入和函数响应场景下实现高效且可解释的敏感性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于方差的全局敏感性分析（GSA）对不确定量化至关重要，但重复的模型评估计算成本高昂。代理模型是高效的替代方案，然而在高维随机输入和函数响应的系统中，构建兼具可扩展性和可解释性的代理模型仍具挑战，尤其是需要在空间或时间域上获取敏感性估计时。PCE虽具有正交结构和与方差敏感性度量的直接关系，但遭受维度灾难，且在函数响应问题中计算负担被进一步放大。

Method: 提出的sNC展开框架：1）镜像截断的功能ANOVA分解，其中每个交互分量采用可分离低秩近似；2）基函数和系数由神经网络参数化，保留PCE的可解释性和正交结构的同时利用神经网络的表达力；3）通过顺序构建和自适应识别每个ANOVA子空间中的主导模式，确定表示的有效复杂度。

Result: sNC展开的结构使得能够直接从其系数中提取统计量和敏感性度量，且计算成本可忽略不计，从而在高维随机输入和函数响应的GSA任务中兼顾可扩展性与可解释性。

Conclusion: sNC为PCE在高维函数响应场景下的维度灾难问题提供了一种有效解决方案，通过神经网络参数化的低秩ANOVA分解实现了可解释且可扩展的敏感性分析代理建模。

Abstract: Variance-based global sensitivity analysis (GSA) plays a key role in uncertainty quantification by identifying the contributions of uncertain inputs to the variability of the model response. The repeated model evaluations required for these tasks are often prohibitively expensive; surrogate models provide an efficient alternative by constructing inexpensive approximations of the underlying system response. Constructing surrogate models that combine scalability and interpretability for systems with high-dimensional stochastic inputs and functional responses remains challenging, particularly when sensitivity estimates are required across spatial or temporal domains. Polynomial chaos expansion (PCE) provides an effective framework for uncertainty propagation and sensitivity analysis due to its orthogonal structure and direct relationship with variance-based sensitivity measures. However, PCE suffers from the curse of dimensionality, whose computational burden is amplified for problems with functional responses. In this work, we introduce the Structured Neural Chaos (sNC) expansion as a surrogate modeling framework for variance-based GSA, inspired by the interpretability and orthogonal structure of PCE. The proposed framework retains the interpretability of structured decompositions while leveraging the expressive power of neural networks. The sNC expansion mirrors a truncated functional ANOVA decomposition, where each interaction component admits a separable low-rank approximation whose basis functions and coefficients are parameterized by neural networks. The expansion is constructed sequentially, adaptively identifying the dominant modes within each ANOVA subspace and determining the effective complexity of the representation. The resulting structure enables the extraction of statistical and sensitivity quantities directly from the coefficients of the sNC expansion at negligible cost.

</details>


### [221] [Persistent Convolution: A Topological Framework for AI Alignment Testing and Semantic Space Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29008)
*Tyler Ashoff,Jordan Rodu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑学的多模态对齐测试方法，利用人类策划的知识结构对不透明AI模型的嵌入空间进行统计检验，以表征语义结构、概念分离和知识图谱对齐，从而增强模型部署、选择和比较的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代不透明AI模型追求性能而牺牲可解释性，导致测试困难。模型开发者需要一种利用人类策划知识结构来测试模型对齐的比较技术，而简单任务的输入空间规模之大也促使需要超越标准结果推理的对齐检查。

Method: 开发了一种基于拓扑学的多模态对齐测试方法，对模型的嵌入空间进行正式的统计检验，通过形式化统计测试来表征语义结构、概念分离和知识图谱对齐，并统一连接到可能性理论和决策理论框架。

Result: 该方法被开发和验证用于使不透明模型的部署、选择和比较更加可解释，提供了与可能性理论的直观联系以及从数据到部署的统一决策理论框架。

Conclusion: 拓扑学驱动的多模态对齐测试为不透明AI模型的比较和评估提供了新的可解释途径，使得模型的选择、部署和比较在形式上更加严谨，同时与可能性理论和决策理论框架形成统一。

Abstract: Modern opaque AI models prize performance over interpretability, which makes testing difficult. However, formal statistical tests conducted on a model's embedding space can provide robust characterizations of semantic structure, concept separation, and knowledge graph alignment. Model developers would benefit from a model comparison technique that leverages human-curated knowledge structures to test alignment. The scale of the input space for even relatively simple tasks motivates the need for alignment checks that augment standard outcome reasoning. This work develops and demonstrates a topology-based multi-modal alignment test to make deployment, selection, and comparison of opaque models more interpretable. These methods also offer an intuitive connection to possibility theory and a unified decision theoretic framework from data to deployment.

</details>


### [222] [Simple-regret rates and minimax optimality of fixed-prior expected improvement in Matérn and squared-exponential RKHSs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29245)
*Emmanuel Vazquez,Sébastien Petit*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了确定性函数最小化问题中期望改进（EI）策略的理论保证。在RKHS框架下，作者推导了EI策略的简单遗憾（simple regret）有限预算界：对于各向同性Matérn核，后验查询后简单遗憾为O(N^{-ν/d})；对于平方指数核，为指数衰减率。此外，证明了精确EI策略在Matérn核上达到极小极大最优速率，在平方指数核上达到常数指数级最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 期望改进（EI）是贝叶斯优化中最广泛使用的采集函数之一，但其理论收敛性质长期缺乏严格分析。已有工作主要关注置信区间型策略（如GP-UCB）的遗憾界，而对EI策略的有限预算理论保证研究不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，为EI策略提供严格的有限样本遗憾界，并建立其最优性。

Method: 作者采用了几何与函数分析相结合的技术路线：(1) 将候选点x处的归一化后验标准差与特征空间中对应的创新（innovation）范数联系起来，即k(x,·)在先前评估表示张成子空间上的正交分量；(2) 引入"序列分离半径"（sequential separation radii）来刻画任意查询序列上有序创新范数的界限；(3) 利用Gram行列式和Kolmogorov宽度估计不同维度子空间的这些半径；(4) 将其与单步遗憾不等式结合，导出有限预算的简单遗憾界。

Result: 主要结果为：(1) 各向同性Matérn核（光滑度ν>0）在N次后验查询后简单遗憾为O(N^{-ν/d})；(2) 各向同性平方指数核简单遗憾为O(exp[-c₁·min{N, N^{1/d}·log(eN)}])；(3) 精确EI最大化时为O(exp[-c₂·N^{1/d}·log(eN)])；(4) 这些界对RKHS球内所有函数均匀成立；(5) 精确EI策略在Matérn核上达到极小极大最优速率，在平方指数核上达到指数中对数常数意义上最优。

Conclusion: 本文首次为期望改进策略建立了严格的有限预算理论保证，证明了EI策略在Matérn和平方指数核上均达到（或几乎达到）极小极大最优收敛速率。这为EI这一实践中最常用的贝叶斯优化策略提供了坚实的理论基础，澄清了其理论地位与GP-UCB等策略的可比性。

Abstract: We study the expected improvement (EI) policy for minimizing a deterministic objective function $f$ on a nonempty compact set $\mathcal X \subset\mathbb R^d$. We assume that $f$ belongs to the RKHS $\mathcal H_k$ of a continuous positive-semidefinite kernel $k$ on $\mathcal X$. Function values are observed exactly, and EI is computed from a fixed zero-mean Gaussian-process model with covariance $σ^2k$. After an initial design, the policy queries a point whose EI is at least a fixed positive fraction of its maximum.
  We identify the normalized posterior standard deviation at a candidate point $x$ with the norm of the corresponding innovation in the canonical feature space, namely the component of $k(x,\cdot)$ orthogonal to the span of the preceding evaluation representers. Sequential separation radii bound the ranked innovation norms along arbitrary query sequences. We estimate these radii using Gram determinants and Kolmogorov widths for subspaces of different dimensions, then combine the estimates with a one-step regret inequality to obtain finite-budget bounds for simple regret.
  After $N$ post-initial queries, simple regret is $O(N^{-ν/d})$ for isotropic Matérn kernels of smoothness $ν>0$. For the isotropic squared-exponential kernel, simple regret is $O(\exp[-c_1\min\{N, N^{1/d}\log(eN)\}])$ for some $c_1>0$. With exact EI maximization, it is $O(\exp[-c_2N^{1/d} \log(eN)])$ for some $c_2>0$. For every fixed $B\geq0$, these bounds are uniform over the RKHS ball of radius $B$.
  If $\mathcal X$ has nonempty interior and $B>0$, then, among deterministic methods whose final recommendation may be any point of $\mathcal X$, the exact EI policy is minimax-rate optimal over the RKHS ball of radius $B$ for Matérn kernels and minimax-rate optimal up to constants in the exponent for squared-exponential kernels.

</details>


### [223] [The Greedy Advantage in Finite-Horizon Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29375)
*Kai Zhou,Michael Lingzhi Li,Kai Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对有限时域下的多臂伯努利老虎机问题，提出了一类正则化贪心算法，首次推导出有限时域后悔界的解析表达式，证明后悔可分解为瞬态探索成本和随正则化强度指数衰减的次优收敛项，并据此给出了正则化参数的标定规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多臂老虎机研究主要关注渐近后悔界，但实际应用中许多场景面临有限且外部施加的时间范围（finite horizon）。在有限时域下，传统算法的理论保证可能不再适用，因此需要专门针对有限时域设计的算法与分析框架。

Method: 提出一类用于多臂伯努利老虎机的正则化贪心算法（regularized greedy algorithms）。推导了这些算法在有限时域下的后悔界（regret envelopes），将后悔分解为瞬态探索成本（transient exploration costs）和随正则化强度指数衰减的次优收敛项两部分。基于该分解，提出了正则化参数的原理性标定规则，并以贪心策略作为极限情况获得了更紧的后悔保证。

Result: 通过大量数值实验表明，标定后的正则化贪心策略在性能上持续与最先进算法持平或更优。同时获得了有限时域后悔界的理论刻画以及贪心策略的更紧后悔保证。

Conclusion: 正则化贪心策略为有限时域老虎机问题提供了一种有效且可理论保证的方法，兼具理论上的后悔界刻画与实际中的良好性能表现。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly rely on sequential experimentation to improve decision-making. While the multi-armed bandit literature has developed algorithms with strong asymptotic regret guarantees, many practical applications operate over finite and externally imposed horizons. Motivated by the finite-horizon setting, we develop a class of regularized greedy algorithms for multi-armed Bernoulli bandits. We derive the first finite-horizon regret envelopes for regularized greedy bandits, showing that finite-horizon regret decomposes into transient exploration costs and a suboptimal convergence term that decays exponentially with the regularization strength. This characterization yields principled calibration rules for the regularization parameters and, as a limiting case, sharper regret guarantees for the classical greedy policy. Across extensive numerical experiments, calibrated regularized greedy policies consistently match or outperform state-of-the-art algorithms. These results suggest that regularized greedy policies can provide an effective approach for finite-horizon bandit problems.

</details>


### [224] [Analytical and Bootstrap Confidence Intervals of Double Machine Learning: Simulation studies and an application to rural-urban difference in obesity prevalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29456)
*Haozheng Xu,Siyuan Ma,Qingyan Xiang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过全面模拟研究比较了双机器学习（DML）在不同机器学习算法（OLS、LASSO、随机森林、LightGBM、神经网络）下的置信区间覆盖率，发现学习者选择对DML推断的可靠性有显著影响，且样本量增加时覆盖率反而下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DML是一种流行的治疗效果估计方法，允许使用灵活的机器学习方法估计干扰参数同时保持有效推断。然而，实际应用中研究者需要在众多机器学习算法中做出选择，而这种选择对DML方差估计和推断的影响尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 通过全面的模拟研究，比较DML理论推导的分析置信区间与自助法（bootstrap）置信区间，使用五种机器学习算法（OLS、LASSO、随机森林、LightGBM、神经网络）在不同数据生成设置下，从偏差、置信区间宽度和覆盖率三个维度评估性能。同时使用美国县域城乡差异真实数据进一步验证。

Result: 结果显示分析置信区间和自助法置信区间的覆盖率存在显著差异，学习者选择对可靠的DML推断至关重要。令人惊讶的是，在许多设置中样本量增加时，DML分析置信区间和自助法置信区间的覆盖率都下降。真实数据分析发现模型性能因学习者选择而异，且更高的乡村化程度对县域肥胖率有统计学上显著的递增效应。

Conclusion: 机器学习算法的选择对DML推断的可靠性有决定性影响，且存在反直觉的样本量增加导致覆盖率恶化的现象，这提示需要更加谨慎地选择学习者并关注DML推断的稳健性。

Abstract: Double Machine Learning (DML) is a popular approach for treatment effect estimation in various settings, which allows a wide range of flexible machine learning methods to be used for nuisance parameter estimation while preserving valid inference. In practice, however, applied researchers must choose among many machine learning algorithms for nuisance models, and the impact of this choice on the variance estimation of DML is not well characterized. We conduct a comprehensive simulation study to compare the coverage probability of DML confidence intervals across different machine learning algorithms. In this study, we compare (1) analytical confidence intervals derived by DML theory versus (2) bootstrap confidence interval. We use a set of learners including ordinary least squares, LASSO, Random Forest, LightGBM, and Neural Networks under different data generation settings. We evaluate the performance across difference settings by bias, confidence interval width, and most importantly, coverage probability. Our results show substantial variability in coverage performance across analytical and bootstrap confidence intervals, highlighting that learner choice plays a critical role in reliable DML inference. Surprisingly, we find that in many settings, when sample size increases, the coverage probability of both DML analytical and bootstrap confidence interval decreases. We further investigate coverage probabilities using a real dataset on rural urban differences among U.S. counties. The real data analysis discovers that (1) the model performance still varies by the learner choices and (2) greater rurality has a statistically significant increasing effect on county level obesity prevalence.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [Global and local error bounds: characterizations via directional derivatives and tangent cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29004)
*Yu Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一般（非凸、非光滑）函数，利用方向导数与切锥建立了全局和局部误差界的全新刻画，给出了误差界与扰动稳定性、子水平集Hausdorff稳定性之间的联系，并揭示了极小单位球面方向导数与全局斜率之间的精确关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 误差界在优化理论中具有核心地位，但以往的研究多依赖于凸性假设或线性结构。本文旨在突破这些限制，为一般函数建立更普适的误差界刻画条件。

Method: 作者利用方向导数和切锥这两个几何分析工具，不施加凸性或线性结构假设。具体地：(1) 为非负下半连续函数建立了全局误差界的多个等价条件；(2) 引入极小单位球面方向导数φ(x)并建立其与全局斜率的关系；(3) 对Lipschitz函数证明sup φ(x)<0是误差界的充分条件，对凸函数证明其必要性；(4) 在有限维空间中建立了尖锐的局部误差界结果，最优常数为1/φ(x̄)。

Result: (1) 建立了全局误差界的多个等价刻画，并与扰动稳定性、子水平集Hausdorff稳定性及逆子水平集估计联系起来；(2) 精确刻画了φ(x)与全局斜率的关系；(3) 对Lipschitz函数给出误差界的充分条件（凸情形下亦为必要条件）；(4) 有限维下证明φ(x̄)>0时局部误差界成立且最优常数为1/φ(x̄)，φ(x̄)=0且存在切锥外合适方向时误差界失败。

Conclusion: 本文为一般（非凸、非光滑）函数提供了统一的误差界理论框架，填补了已有理论对凸性依赖的空白，其局部最优常数的精确刻画对实际应用中的误差界估计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We develop new characterizations of both global and local error bounds for general functions, using directional derivatives and tangent cones without imposing convexity or linear structure. We first establish several equivalent conditions for the global error bound of a nonnegative lower semicontinuous function. These equivalences hold for general, possibly nonconvex and nonsmooth functions. We further link the error bound with perturbation stability, Hausdorff stability of sublevel sets, and an inverse-sublevel-set estimate. Turning to directional derivatives, we introduce the minimal unit-sphere directional derivative \(\varphi(x)\) on the tangent cone and clarify its exact relation with the global slope. For Lipschitz continuous functions we prove that \(\sup_{x\notin S_0} \varphi(x) < 0\) is sufficient for an error bound, and for convex functions on convex sets this condition is also necessary, In finite dimensions we obtain sharp local results: if \(\varphi(\bar{x}) > 0\) at a solution \(\bar{x}\), then a local error bound holds and the optimal constant is exactly \(1/\varphi(\bar{x})\); if \(\varphi(\bar{x}) = 0\) and a suitable direction exists outside the tangent cone of the solution set, the local error bound fails. A general estimate relating the directional derivative to the distance from the tangent cone of the solution set is also derived.

</details>


### [226] [A Policy Iteration Scheme for Semilinear Stochastic Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman Equations with Exponential Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29024)
*Hasib Uddin Molla,Jinniao Qiu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了非马尔可夫随机最优控制问题，其中值函数是由随机Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman（SHJB）方程描述的随机场。当随机积分系数不受控制时，SHJB方程呈半线性形式，由于可测随机性而面临计算挑战。作者提出了基于连续线性化的策略迭代算法，将非线性SHJB方程转化为一系列线性方程，并证明所得逼近序列在均方意义下以指数速率单调收敛到值函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非马尔可夫随机最优控制问题的值函数是随机场，需要由SHJB方程刻画。与马尔可夫情形相比，由于方程中引入了可测量的随机性，半线性SHJB方程的数值求解面临显著的计算挑战，需要开发有效的算法来应对这一问题。

Method: 提出一种基于连续线性化的策略迭代算法：将非线性SHJB方程逐步简化为一系列线性SHJB方程，通过迭代求解这些线性方程来逼近原始问题的解。

Result: 证明了所提出的策略迭代算法产生的逼近序列在均方意义下单调收敛到值函数，且收敛速率是指数级的。

Conclusion: 该策略迭代算法为非马尔可夫随机最优控制问题提供了理论上有保证的高效数值求解方案，兼具单调性和指数收敛速度的良好性质。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the non-Markovian stochastic optimal control problems in which the value function is a random field characterized by a stochastic Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (SHJB) equation. When the stochastic integration coefficients are not controlled, the SHJB equation takes a semilinear form, which is subject to computational challenges compared to the Markovian case due to the measurable randomness. We introduce a policy-iteration algorithm based on successive linearization that reduces the nonlinear SHJB equation to a sequence of linear ones. Furthermore, we prove that the resulting approximation sequence converges monotonically to the value function in the mean-square sense with an exponential rate.

</details>


### [227] [Baseball, An Extensive-Form Game-Theoretic Duel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29041)
*Sebastian E. Ferrando,Eli Kohn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将棒球打席建模为有限两人零和扩展式博弈的方法，使用序列形式表示和线性规划求解精确的极小极大均衡，并通过动态规划解释和MLB Statcast数据估计实现均衡策略与策略成本的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 棒球打席是一个天然的博弈场景：投手和击球手在信息不对称（击球手无法观察到投手当前投球选择）下互动，且状态随球数（count）演化。传统寻常形式（normal form）的博弈树规模过大，难以精确求解，因此需要更高效的表示和求解方法。

Method: 1) 将打席建模为有限两人零和扩展式博弈，含机会节点和不完美信息；2) 采用序列形式（sequence-form）表示博弈，其线性规划规模随博弈树线性增长；3) 从MLB Statcast数据估计机会转移概率；4) 建立了两个动态规划解释：对一般完美记忆两人零和博弈，证明序列形式对偶变量可分解为到达权重和条件延续值；在棒球的状态马尔可夫假设下，证明可通过12个非终局球数的逆向归纳计算完整极小极大均衡。

Result: 论文给出了精确的极小极大均衡计算方法，产生了均衡策略、延续值，以及非最优行动的策略成本的条件测量和到达权重测量。序列形式方法能够处理远超寻常形式实际范围的大规模博弈树。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅为棒球打席提供了理论上的精确均衡求解框架，还建立了序列形式博弈对偶变量与动态规划之间的联系，在方法论上对一般的两人零和完美记忆博弈具有推广价值。

Abstract: We formulate a baseball plate appearance as a finite two-person, zero-sum, extensive-form game with chance moves and imperfect information. Successive pitch cycles are connected through the evolving count, while the Batter chooses an action without observing the Pitcher's current pitch selection. Terminal outcomes are valued through a run-expectancy utility that incorporates both runs scored during the plate appearance and the continuation value of the half-inning. We implement the sequence-form representation of the game, whose linear-programming formulation grows linearly with the game tree and permits exact minimax equilibrium computations for trees far beyond the practical range of the normal form. Nature's transition probabilities are estimated from MLB Statcast data. We also establish two complementary dynamic-programming interpretations. For general finite two-person zero-sum games with perfect recall, we show that the dual variables of the sequence-form best-response programs decompose into reach weights and conditional continuation values. Under the additional state-Markov assumptions of the baseball model, we prove that the full minimax equilibrium can be computed by backward induction over the twelve non-terminal counts. The resulting computations produce equilibrium strategies, continuation values, and conditional and reach-weighted measures of the strategic cost of non-optimal actions.

</details>


### [228] [Newton Method for Fixed-Support Doubly Entropic Wasserstein Barycenter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29109)
*Jianting Pan,Sirong Dai,Lei Yang,Yaomin Wang,Ji'an Li,Ming Yan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究固定支撑的双正则化Wasserstein重心问题，通过半对偶公式将其转化为光滑无约束凸优化问题，并提出精确Newton方法及稀疏Newton变体以提高可扩展性，在合成与真实数据上验证了收敛速度优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wasserstein重心在最优传输领域有广泛应用，但现有方法在大规模问题上的计算效率有限，需要更高效的求解算法来实现高精度重心计算。

Method: 利用熵最优传输的半对偶公式，将双正则化Wasserstein重心问题重构为对偶变量中的光滑无约束凸优化问题；推导出梯度和Hessian的显式表达式，开发精确Newton方法；进一步提出稀疏Newton变体，通过对传输概率矩阵进行稀疏化来降低Hessian-向量乘积的计算成本。

Result: 建立了Hessian逼近界和收敛性等理论结果；在合成及真实数据集上的实验表明，稀疏Newton方法的收敛速度快于对比方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为双正则化Wasserstein重心问题提供了可扩展、高精度的Newton型求解框架，稀疏化策略在保持理论保证的同时显著提升计算效率。

Abstract: We study the fixed-support doubly regularized Wasserstein barycenter problem. Using the semi-dual formulation of entropic optimal transport, we reformulate the problem as a smooth, unconstrained, convex optimization problem in the dual variables. We then derive explicit expressions for the gradient and Hessian and develop an exact Newton method for high-accuracy barycenter computation. To improve scalability, we propose a sparse Newton variant that sparsifies the transport probability matrices, thereby reducing the cost of Hessian-vector products. We establish theoretical results for the proposed methods, including Hessian approximation bounds and convergence results. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets show that the sparse Newton method converges faster than

</details>


### [229] [Anisotropic Higher-Order Semiregularity of Degenerate Generalized Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29114)
*Tomáš Roubal*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了各向异性高阶覆盖和逆估计的三角公式化框架，涵盖光滑映射和广义方程。通过固定的目标分解和三角因子条件，利用三角p-因子算子的满射性建立分块固定尺度包含关系，从而获得各块上的逆估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高阶覆盖与逆估计理论缺乏统一的、自包含的三角化表述，尤其是在各向异性情形下，需要处理不同块具有不同阶数的覆盖需求。本文旨在填补这一空白，为光滑映射和广义方程提供统一的三角公式化框架。

Method: 本文采用分块三角化方法：对C^p映射固定索引目标分解，施加三角因子条件，选择满足三角p-核条件的方向。通过建立三角p-因子算子的满射性来推导固定尺度包含关系，各非零块Y_i按阶t^i被覆盖，得到指数1/i的逆估计。对于广义方程，引入辅助精确模型定理隔离范围传递机制（带无环修正纤维），主要充分判据利用闭凸过程内逼近与光滑三角因子算子之和的满射性来处理集值项的缺失方向。

Result: 建立了各向异性高阶覆盖和逆估计的三角公式化理论：在光滑情形下，从C^p导数直接验证模型并记录分块固定尺度包含；在广义方程情形下，通过闭凸过程内逼近与三角因子算子的联合满射性，允许集值项补充丢失方向，获得覆盖和逆估计结果。

Conclusion: 该三角公式化提供了一个自包含且统一的框架，能够处理各向异性高阶覆盖问题，允许零块存在，并将光滑分析中的导数级分阶表述与广义方程的集值分析有效结合。

Abstract: We give a self-contained triangular formulation of anisotropic higher-order
  covering and inverse estimates for smooth mappings and generalized equations.
  For a $C^p$ mapping into a finite-dimensional target, an indexed target
  decomposition is fixed, the corresponding triangular factor condition is
  imposed, and a direction satisfying the associated triangular $p$-kernel
  condition is chosen. Surjectivity of the resulting triangular $p$-factor
  operator yields a fixed-scale inclusion in which each nonzero block $Y_i$ is
  covered at order $t^i$, and hence an inverse estimate with exponent $1/i$ on
  that block. Zero blocks are allowed, and $p$ denotes the highest active grade.
  The smooth result is a derivative-level graded formulation: it verifies the
  model directly from the $C^p$ derivatives and records the blockwise
  fixed-scale inclusion needed for the set-valued analysis.
  For generalized equations, an auxiliary exact-model theorem isolates the
  range-transfer mechanism with acyclic correction fibres. The main sufficient
  criterion uses a closed convex-process inner approximation whose sum with
  the smooth triangular factor operator is surjective, allowing the set-valued
  term to supply missing directions.

</details>


### [230] [Robust Airfoil Design Optimization via a Bilevel Model-Based Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29161)
*Alpaslan Kurt,Dilara Buk,Yusuf Yazicioglu,Figen Oztoprak,Onur Son,Gorkem Demir*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出GLORO，一种用于昂贵函数评估的鲁棒优化双层求解方法。该方法在参数空间使用高斯过程代理模型和贝叶斯优化（下层）近似计算鲁棒优化目标/约束，在变量空间使用局部模型（上层）指导优化。方法在RAE2822翼型的鲁棒气动外形优化问题（在马赫数和攻角不确定性下优化升阻比）上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒优化通常涉及昂贵的目标函数评估（如计算流体力学仿真），直接求解计算代价过高。现有的鲁棒优化方法往往难以在计算效率和优化质量之间取得平衡，需要一种能够将昂贵评估引导至对优化过程关键区域的求解策略。

Method: GLORO采用双层优化结构：下层在参数空间中利用高斯过程代理模型和贝叶斯优化来近似计算鲁棒优化的目标函数和约束；上层在变量空间中使用基于这些近似评估构建的局部模型来推进优化。通过将昂贵的函数评估引导至优化感兴趣的区域，实现计算效率的提升。

Result: 方法在RAE2822翼型的鲁棒气动设计优化问题中得到测试，该问题在来流马赫数和攻角存在运行不确定性的条件下，优化翼型形状以保证鲁棒的升阻比性能。

Conclusion: GLORO通过双层优化架构和代理模型的结合，为昂贵函数评估下的鲁棒优化问题提供了一种高效求解途径，并通过实际翼型设计案例验证了其可行性和有效性。

Abstract: We propose GLORO, a bilevel solution methodology for robust optimization involving expensive function evaluations. The methodology is carefully designed to achieve satisfactory optimization results in a computationally efficient manner. It is based on Gaussian Process surrogates in the parameter space for approximate computations of the objective / constraints of the robust optimization problem via Bayesian optimization (lower level), and local models constructed in the variable space using these approximate evaluations (upper level). Both the use of Bayesian optimization (in parameter space) and the use of local models (in variable space) are motivated by the idea of guiding the expensive function evaluations to the regions of interest for the optimization process. The methodological work is motivated by and tested on a robust airfoil design optimization problem. This application focuses on the RAE2822 airfoil, optimizing its shape to ensure a robust lift-to-drag ratio under operational uncertainties in Mach number and angle of attack.

</details>


### [231] [Volume of quasi-homogeneous sublevel sets: Two linear algebra deterministic algorithms with convergence rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29269)
*Didier Henrion,Jean B Lasserre*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出两种确定性算法，用于计算正拟齐次多项式单位下水平集的Lebesgue体积。通过将包围盒的Lebesgue测度前推穿过多项式，将高维体积问题转化为一维矩问题，从而消除环境维度对优化的影响。两种算法均绕过半定优化，仅依赖标准数值线性代数，分别以多项式速率和指数速率收敛，且产生的单变量多项式对多元矩-SOS体积层级可行，从而改善了该层级已知的最佳收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算多项式单位下水平集的体积是高维几何与多项式优化中的核心难题，传统方法受限于环境维度，计算代价高昂。现有矩-SOS体积层级的收敛速率不够理想，且依赖半定规划，难以扩展到高维场景。

Method: 第一步，通过前推测度将高维体积问题约化为一维矩问题，环境维度仅出现在预处理阶段（计算多项式在盒子上的矩，对稀疏或可分多项式复杂度为多项式级）。第二步，提出两种算法：(1) 用切比雪夫多项式逼近分段常数函数，每次松弛化简为快速余弦变换，以多项式速率收敛；(2) 从涉及矩矩阵和局部化矩阵的广义特征值问题中提取体积界，矩阵大小随松弛阶数线性增长，以指数速率收敛，速率比由多项式上界决定。两种算法产生的单变量多项式对多元矩-SOS体积层级可行。

Result: 两种算法均能返回体积的认证上界和下界，分别为多项式速率收敛和指数速率收敛（后者速率由多项式上界的先验估计决定）。此外，算法产生的单变量多项式可注入多元矩-SOS层级，将其代数与几何收敛速率传递到该层级本身，改进其已知的最佳收敛速率。

Conclusion: 通过将高维体积问题转化为一维矩问题，并设计两种仅依赖数值线性代数的确定性算法，本文显著降低了计算复杂度，同时改善了矩-SOS体积层级的收敛速率保证，为该类问题提供了高效且可认证的计算框架。

Abstract: We consider the problem of computing the Lebesgue volume of the unit sublevel set of a positive quasi-homogeneous polynomial. Pushing the Lebesgue measure of an ambient bounding box forward through the polynomial reduces this high-dimensional volume to a one-dimensional moment problem. This removes the ambient dimension from the optimization and confines the dimension to a single preprocessing stage, computing the moments of the polynomial over the box, which is polynomial in the ambient dimension for sparse or separable polynomials. We propose two deterministic algorithms for the resulting univariate relaxations, each returning certified upper and lower bounds on the volume. Both bypass semidefinite optimization entirely and rely only on standard numerical linear algebra. The first approximates a piecewise-constant function by a Chebyshev polynomial, so that each relaxation reduces to a fast cosine transform, and converges at a polynomial rate in the relaxation order. The second extracts the volume bounds from a single generalized eigenvalue problem involving moment and localizing matrices whose size grows linearly with the relaxation order, and converges at an exponential rate; the ratio governing this rate is determined by an a priori upper bound on the polynomial over the bounding box. Finally, the univariate polynomials produced by either algorithm are feasible for the multivariate moment-SOS volume hierarchy. The algebraic and geometric rates therefore transfer to the hierarchy itself, improving on its best known convergence rates.

</details>


### [232] [Rational Jacobi Rotations and the Complexity of Approximating Mixed Integer Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29386)
*Alberto Del Pia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在Turing机上以多项式时间求解混合整数二次规划（MIQP）ε-近似解的算法，前提是整数变量数目和Hessian负特征值数目固定。除非P=NP，这两个限制都是必要的，因此该结果完善了MIQP近似复杂度的刻画。核心创新是一种多项式时间的同步对角化算法，使用有理Jacobi旋转将椭球精确映射到球，同时使目标函数近似可分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIQP的近似求解复杂度此前未被完全刻画。经典方法虽然能使目标函数精确可分离，但所需的基变换通常是无理数，无法在Turing机上执行。本文旨在填补这一空白，完成关于整数变量数和Hessian惯性（负特征值数）的MIQP近似复杂度表征。

Method: 提出一种在Turing机上多项式时间内运行的算法，该算法基于一种新的有理同步对角化技术：利用有理Jacobi旋转构造有理坐标变换，将给定椭球精确映射到单位球，同时使目标函数近似可分离（扰动可任意小），并保持Hessian的惯性不变。相比经典构造（需要无理基变换实现精确可分离），这一方法可在Turing机上实现。

Result: 该算法在整数变量数和Hessian负特征值数固定的条件下，可在多项式时间内找到MIQP的ε-近似解。作者证明了除非P=NP，这两个限制均为必要，从而完整刻画了MIQP近似的复杂度。该结果在纯连续情形下也是新的。

Conclusion: 本文通过有理Jacobi旋转这一新颖工具完成了MIQP近似复杂度的完全刻画，证明了在固定整数变量数和Hessian负特征值数的条件下可多项式时间近似求解，并指出有理同步对角化技术本身具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: We present an algorithm that finds an epsilon-approximate solution to a mixed integer quadratic programming (MIQP) problem, and that runs on a Turing machine in time polynomial in the size of the instance and in 1/epsilon, provided that the number of integer variables and the number of negative eigenvalues of the Hessian of the objective function are fixed. Unless P=NP, both restrictions are necessary, so this completes the characterization of the complexity of approximating MIQP in terms of the number of integer variables and the inertia of the Hessian; the result is new already in the purely continuous case. The main ingredient is a polynomial-time simultaneous diagonalization algorithm: it computes a rational change of basis that maps a given ellipsoid, presented in factored form, exactly to a ball, while making the objective function separable up to an arbitrarily small perturbation and preserving the inertia of its Hessian. The classical construction, in which the objective function is made exactly separable, requires a change of basis that is in general irrational, and cannot be carried out on a Turing machine; ours rests instead on rational Jacobi rotations, which we believe to be of independent interest.

</details>


### [233] [Extrapolation-based Direct Search for Nonsmooth Stochastic Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29408)
*Anthony Palmieri,Francesco Rinaldi,Sara Shashaani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种用于无约束零阶优化的随机直接搜索方法，该方法结合随机轮询方向与基于p阶充分下降检验的随机外推线搜索。在随机估计的条件精度假设下，证明了几乎必然收敛到Clarke稳定点，并建立了期望迭代复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对局部Lipschitz且可能非光滑的目标函数，现有的直接搜索方法在随机非光滑环境下的收敛性和复杂度分析尚属空白，特别是基于外推策略的方法缺乏严格的理论保证。

Method: 采用随机轮询方向与随机外推线搜索相结合的策略，基于p阶充分下降测试，利用超鞅停时论证建立期望迭代复杂度分析。

Result: 证明了几乎必然收敛到Clarke稳定点；建立期望迭代复杂度为O(max{r^{-p}, ε^{-p/(p-1)}})达到(r,ε)-Goldstein稳定点；期望测试点复杂度为O(ε^{1-n}·max{r^{-p}, ε^{-p/(p-1)}})。数值实验表明在DFO基准测试集上性能优于现有随机直接搜索方法。

Conclusion: 这是首次对非光滑随机设置下基于外推的直接搜索方法进行收敛性和期望复杂度分析，填补了该领域的理论空白。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a stochastic direct-search method for unconstrained zeroth-order minimization of locally Lipschitz, possibly nonsmooth, objectives. The method combines random polling directions with a stochastic extrapolating line search based on a sufficient-decrease test of order $p$. Under conditional accuracy assumptions on the stochastic estimates, we prove almost-sure convergence to Clarke stationary points. We further establish an expected iteration complexity bound. Specifically, using a supermartingale stopping-time argument, we prove that $\mathcal O\left(
  \max\left\{
  r^{-p},
  \varepsilon^{-p/(p-1)}
  \right\}
  \right) $ iterations are sufficient in expectation to reach an $(r,\varepsilon)$-Goldstein stationary point. Moreover, we derive a corresponding expected tested-point complexity bound of order $\mathcal O\bigl(\varepsilon^{1-n} \max\{r^{-p},\varepsilon^{-p/(p-1)}\}\bigr)$. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first convergence and expected-complexity analysis for an extrapolation-based direct-search method in a nonsmooth stochastic setting. Numerical experiments on a DFO benchmark suite highlight competitive performance against well-established stochastic direct-search methods.

</details>


### [234] [When does admission control reduce congestion? A stochastic ordering approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29439)
*Royi Jacobovic,Daniel Stiebel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究状态依赖行为下接纳控制（admission control）是否必然能降低随机服务系统中的拥塞。通过随机序（stochastic ordering）方法分析工作负载过程，发现由于内生反馈效应，接纳控制可能反而无法降低拥塞，并构造了反例。进一步，作者识别出使接纳控制在初始忙期内有效的一般条件：客户类型与服务需求之间的独立性可消除不利反馈机制，恢复随机占优关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接纳控制是随机服务系统中常用的拥塞调节机制，直觉上限制到达应能降低工作负载。但现实中客户会基于观察到的系统状态做决策，形成控制动作与未来到达之间的反馈回路。作者质疑这一基本假设：在状态依赖行为下，接纳控制是否必然降低拥塞？

Method: 使用工作负载过程的随机序（stochastic ordering）作为理论工具。通过构造显式反例展示接纳控制失败的情形，并推导出一个一般性条件（客户类型与服务需求独立）来保证接纳控制在初始忙期内的有效性。

Result: （1）证明接纳控制可能因内生反馈效应而无法降低拥塞，并给出显式反例；（2）识别出客户类型与服务需求之间的独立性可消除不利反馈机制，恢复随机占优关系，保证接纳控制在初始忙期内有效。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了接纳控制的基本局限性，强调了在设计随机服务系统时必须考虑行为响应（behavioral responses）的重要性。

Abstract: Admission control is a widely used mechanism for regulating congestion in stochastic service systems, where restricting arrivals is expected to reduce workload and improve performance.
  In many settings, however, customers make decisions based on the observed system state, creating a feedback loop between control actions and future arrivals. This raises a fundamental question: does admission control necessarily reduce congestion under state-dependent behavior?
  We address this question using stochastic ordering of workload processes. We show that admission control may fail to reduce congestion due to endogenous feedback effects, and construct explicit counterexamples illustrating this phenomenon.
  We then identify a general condition under which admission control is effective over the initial busy period. In particular, independence between customers' types and service requirements eliminates the adverse feedback mechanism and restores stochastic dominance.
  These results highlight fundamental limitations of admission control and the importance of accounting for behavioral responses in the design of stochastic service systems.

</details>


### [235] [Robust mean field control: an application to optimal execution under composite uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29514)
*Huafu Liao,Shuhui Liu,Chenchen Mou,Defeng Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个鲁棒平均场控制框架，用于解决多维最优清算问题，同时考虑随机过程和确定性模型参数的不确定性。通过Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs (HJBI)方程建立验证结果，并利用新颖的先验估计证明了方程的良好适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维最优清算问题在实际金融市场中面临双重不确定性：一方面来自底层随机过程的随机性，另一方面来自确定性模型参数的不确定性。现有方法难以同时处理这两种不确定性，尤其是当哈密顿量非线性地依赖于位置和动量的联合分布时，且哈密顿量既不是位移凸也不是位移凹的情况。

Method: 1. 构建鲁棒平均场控制框架处理多维最优清算问题；2. 通过HJBI方程建立验证结果，变量为概率测度；3. 提出新颖的先验估计方法，处理一般或二次哈密顿量（不要求位移凸性或凹性）；4. 将先验估计和适定性结果推广到最优清算问题的实际应用中，允许哈密顿量具有线性增长的导数；5. 在复合不确定性下求解约束多维线性二次最优清算问题。

Result: 建立了HJBI方程的适定性（well-posedness），涵盖一般或二次哈密顿量，且不要求哈密顿量在动量方面具有位移凸性或凹性。成功将理论结果应用于最优清算问题，解决了约束多维线性二次最优清算问题在复合不确定性下的求解。

Conclusion: 该框架为多维最优清算问题提供了一套完整的鲁棒解决方案，通过严格的数学证明（HJBI方程适定性和先验估计）保证了方法的理论可靠性，能够同时处理随机过程和参数双重不确定性。

Abstract: We provide a framework for robust mean field control problems that describe multi-dimensional optimal liquidation problems under uncertainty from both the underlying stochastic process and the deterministic model parameters. The verification results are established with Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs (HJBI) equations where the variables are probability measures and the Hamiltonian nonlinearly involves the joint distribution of position and momentum. Using novel a priori estimates, we establish the well-posedness of the HJBI equations featuring general or quadratic Hamiltonians that are neither displacement convex nor concave in their momentum. The a priori estimates and well-posedness results are extended during their application to optimal liquidation problems, where we allow the Hamiltonian to have derivatives of linear growth and solve the constrained multi-dimensional linear quadratic optimal liquidation problem under composite uncertainty.

</details>


### [236] [Physics Matters in PnP: Recovery Guarantees with the MMSE and NN Denoisers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29525)
*Tobias Wolf,Jalal Fadili,Jin Guo,Roy Y. He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性病态逆问题中基于MMSE估计器的前向-后向分裂PnP方法，不同于现有文献，考虑了针对（退化的）非对角协方差高斯噪声特化的估计器，并通过线性算子替代部分下降步骤以关联观测噪声与MMSE估计器噪声。在温和假设下，推导了去噪器的性质，并证明了迭代在逐点和Wasserstein距离下的恢复保证，结果表明去噪器不能与物理模型无关地选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PnP文献中使用的去噪器通常是假设独立同分布高斯噪声，且与正向模型无关；然而实际成像问题中的MMSE估计器需要处理退化噪声（可能非对角协方差），且去噪器与正向模型的耦合关系尚未被充分理论分析。

Method: 采用前向-后向分裂的PnP框架，将MMSE估计器作为去噪器，并引入线性算子将观测噪声与MMSE估计器噪声联系起来，替代经典迭代中的部分下降步骤。在温和假设下分析去噪器性质，并通过点态收敛和Wasserstein距离给出恢复保证，同时扩展到神经网络参数化的MMSE去噪器情形。

Result: 证明了去噪器在温和假设下的若干性质，获得了迭代恢复的逐点和分布（Wasserstein距离）两方面的保证；关键发现是去噪器不能以物理无关的方式选择，必须与正向模型匹配；对神经网络参数化MMSE去噪器也导出了相应的恢复界。

Conclusion: 论文论证了在PnP框架中，MMSE去噪器必须与正向模型联系统一起来，否则无法保证恢复质量，这为设计物理感知的PnP算法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the forward-backward-splitting version of the Plug and Play (PnP) method for linear ill-posed problems with MMSE estimators as denoisers. In contrast to existing literature, we consider estimators which are specialized for (degenerate) Gaussian noise with possibly non-diagonal covariance matrices. We further deviate from the classical iteration by replacing parts of the descent step with a linear operator that relates the observation noise to that of the MMSE estimator. Under mild assumptions, we derive several properties of the denoiser and prove recovery guarantees of the iteration both pointwise and in the Wasserstein distance of the underlying probability distributions. Crucially, our analysis shows that the denoiser cannot be chosen in a physics-agnostic way, that is, independently of the forward model. We extend our results to the case where the MMSE denoiser is parametrized by a neural network and derive the corresponding recovery bounds.

</details>


### [237] [Tube MPC for Bilinear Koopman Models using Robust Control Contraction Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29538)
*Thomas de Jong,Mircea Lazar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双线性Koopman模型的鲁棒管式模型预测控制（MPC）框架。通过推导离散时间鲁棒控制收缩度量（RCCM），并利用神经网络在稀疏采样的高维提升状态空间上学习度量与反馈控制器，构建了带收紧约束的鲁棒管式MPC方案，建立了递归可行性、鲁棒约束满足和输入-状态稳定性（ISS）保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性系统的鲁棒MPC通常面临两大挑战：一是非线性优化问题求解困难，二是基于数据辨识的Koopman模型与真实动力学之间存在失配，需要显式考虑这种不确定性以保证闭环稳定性。现有方法难以在高维提升状态空间上有效综合收缩性证书。

Method: 1) 为双线性Koopman模型推导离散时间鲁棒控制收缩度量（RCCM），显式刻画模型与真实动力学之间的失配；2) 提出可扩展的神经网络学习方法，在稀疏采样的提升状态空间子集上训练度量参数化与关联的反馈控制器；3) 利用得到的证书构建鲁棒管式MPC方案并收紧约束；4) 利用双线性Koopman模型的线性参数变化（LPV）结构，使MPC问题在固定调度序列下变为凸问题；5) 通过Chebyshev伪谱离散化将测地线计算重构为一系列二次规划。

Result: 在具有状态依赖输入增益的非线性摆基准上验证了完整框架的有效性，证明了递归可行性、鲁棒约束满足以及对模型失配的输入-状态稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文通过将鲁棒收缩度量的学习、测地线的凸化重构与双线性Koopman模型的优良结构相结合，为数据驱动的非线性鲁棒MPC提供了一条完整的、可扩展且具有理论保证的解决路径。

Abstract: This paper presents a robust tube model predictive control (MPC) framework for nonlinear systems represented by bilinear Koopman models identified from data. We derive discrete-time robust control contraction metrics (RCCMs) for bilinear Koopman models, which certify a contraction property that explicitly accounts for the mismatch between the Koopman model and the true dynamics. To synthesize such certificates for the typically high-dimensional lifted state, we propose a scalable learning approach that trains neural network parameterizations of the metric and of an associated feedback controller over a sparsely sampled subset of the lifted state space. The resulting certificates are used to construct a robust tube MPC scheme with tightened constraints, for which we establish recursive feasibility, robust constraint satisfaction and input-to-state stability (ISS) of the closed-loop system with respect to the mismatch between the Koopman model and the true dynamics. By exploiting the linear parameter-varying structure of bilinear Koopman models, the MPC problem becomes convex for a fixed scheduling sequence, while the geodesic computation underlying the contractive feedback is reformulated, via a Chebyshev pseudospectral discretization, as a sequence of quadratic programs. The complete framework is validated on a nonlinear pendulum benchmark with a state-dependent input gain.

</details>


### [238] [Node-Wise Dynamic Optimal Control for Evolutionary Games on General Multilayer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29563)
*Rio Aurachman,Giuliano Punzo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了多层网络中促进合作行为的最优激励策略，通过求解Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程，为一般网络结构下的结构化群体提供了解析最优激励方案，该方案是节点特有的、动态的，且可能同时包含奖励和惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究仅在单层网络上识别了最优分散式激励策略，而现实中的社会系统往往通过多层网络结构运作——玩家在一个邻居集合中进行博弈，同时从另一邻居集合中扩散观点。对于多层网络这一尚未探索的情形，缺乏最优激励策略的解决方案。

Method: 通过将激励分配问题建模为最优控制问题，求解Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程获得解析解；基于网络化囚徒困境博弈实现动态反馈控制回路，使激励随时间最优分配，依赖于当前合作状态、博弈层网络、策略扩散层网络及收益矩阵设计。

Result: 最优激励是节点特有的，每个节点的激励由其相对位置及两层网络的邻接矩阵决定；最优解并非纯粹的奖励或惩罚，同一时刻不同节点可能分别收到奖励和惩罚，取决于其相对于其他节点的合作水平；提供了解析解和数值验证。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了多层网络中激励优化策略的空白，表明最优激励需要在节点层面动态调整，且奖励与惩罚可同时混合使用，为促进复杂社会系统中合作行为的长期可持续性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Promoting cooperative behaviour amongst decision makers has key implications for the long term sustainability of social systems. Incentives can promote cooperation in situations where defection is more favourable. Previous research has identified optimal decentralised incentives in structured populations where the social environment is described by a generic single-layer networks. Optimality is here intended in the sense of cost minimisation. Here, we provide and optimal incentive strategy for structured populations in general network, a rather unexplored case. Further, we look at populations where, through the network structure, each player interact with one set of neighbours while contributing to an opinion diffusion dynamics from a second set of neighbours. Hence we cover the case of multilayer networks. To fill this gap, we provide a solution to the optimal control problem by solving the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation and derive an analytic solution for distributing incentives in multilayer networks. By implementing the dynamics of the networked Prisoner's Dilemma Game, we provide a feedback control loop that yields an optimal incentive distribution over time. The dynamic incentive depends on the current state of cooperation, the game-layer network, the strategy-diffusion layer, and the payoff matrix design. We found that the optimal incentive is node-wise, unique to each node on the network, influenced by its relative position according to the adjacency matrices of both layers. We also found that the optimal solution is not exclusively reward or punishment. While one node can receive a reward, the other node may receive punishment at the same time, depending on its current level of cooperation, relative to other nodes. We provide analytic solutions and numerical validations for the cases studied, comparing our results to the existing literature.

</details>


### [239] [The maximum number of Pareto eigenvalues of a real matrix of order four is 23](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29580)
*Samir Adly*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意4×4实矩阵至多有23个不同的Pareto特征值。通过不动点论证、支持轮廓分析和精确符号计算，将此前已知的23-26区间上界收紧至23，并构造了恰好达到23个Pareto特征值的显式矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pareto特征值问题在Pareto优化、多目标优化和非光滑动力系统中具有重要应用。对于4阶矩阵，此前已知Pareto容量介于23和26之间，本文旨在确定精确的上界。

Method: 首先对满足两个条件（主子矩阵的实特征值均为单特征值、不同主子矩阵无公共实特征值）的4阶矩阵，利用不动点论证证明Pareto特征值个数为奇数，从而排除25这个偶数上限。然后利用3阶支持轮廓和主子矩阵特征向量的恒等式排除25。最后通过扰动和不动点指数论证将结论推广到所有4阶实矩阵，并用精确符号计算验证一个显式矩阵恰好有23个不同的正则Pareto特征值。

Result: 证明了任意4×4实矩阵至多有23个不同的Pareto特征值，并构造了一个恰好具有23个不同正则Pareto特征值的显式矩阵，通过精确符号计算予以验证。

Conclusion: 4阶实矩阵的Pareto容量恰好为23，这是对既有上下界（23至26）的最终确定，结果表明所提出的组合论证与不动点技术能有效地刻画Pareto特征值的计数问题。

Abstract: For a given real matrix $A\in\R^{n\times n}$, a Pareto eigenvalue of $A$ is a real number $λ\in\R$ for which there exists a nonzero vector $x\in\R^n\setminus\{0\}$ such that $$ 0\leq x\perp Ax-λx\geq0. $$ We prove that every matrix $A\in\R^{4\times4}$ has at most $23$ distinct Pareto eigenvalues. We first prove the result for matrices of order $4$ satisfying two conditions: every real eigenvalue of a principal submatrix is simple, and two different principal submatrices have no real eigenvalue in common. For these matrices, a fixed point argument shows that the number of Pareto eigenvalues is odd. Previous known results show that the Pareto capacity of order $4$ is between $23$ and $26$. Thus only $25$ remains to exclude. We exclude this case by using the support profiles in order $3$ and identities involving eigenvectors of principal submatrices. A perturbation and fixed point index argument then extends the bound to all real matrices of order $4$. An exact symbolic computation certifies that an explicit matrix of order $4$ has exactly $23$ distinct regular Pareto eigenvalues.

</details>


### [240] [Admissible Set for Linear Systems under Linear State Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29664)
*Jean Lévine,Philipp Rumschinski,Franz Rußwurm,Stefan Streif*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种计算连续时间线性控制系统许可集内部多胞形逼近的方法，基于势垒理论和线性系统显式解，通过结构化采样直接生成许可集边界样本，避免了状态空间的大规模离散化，并可扩展到Hammerstein系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许可集（admissible sets）的计算对控制系统验证至关重要，但现有可达性/生存性方法需要对状态空间大面积区域进行离散化，计算开销大且可扩展性差。

Method: 1) 基于势垒理论和线性系统显式解，设计结构化采样程序直接生成许可集边界样本；2) 分别计算各状态约束的许可集，再通过交集组合内部逼近，避免同时激活约束的显式处理；3) 将边界样本转换为半空间表示，并对采样和多胞形重建算法进行运行时复杂度分析；4) 扩展到Hammerstein系统。

Result: 在三重积分器和高维质量-弹簧-阻尼链系统上进行了验证，展示了几何构造过程并证明了对高维系统的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能够高效计算许可集的内部多胞形逼近，避免了大范围状态空间离散化的瓶颈，且在处理高维系统时具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: This paper presents a method for computing inner polytopic approximations of admissible sets for continuous-time linear control systems subject to affine state constraints. Building upon barrier theory and the explicit solution of linear systems, a structured sampling procedure is developed to generate boundary samples of the admissible set directly, thereby avoiding the discretization of large regions of the state space required by many viability and reachability methods.
  The proposed framework computes admissible sets for the individual state constraints separately and combines the resulting inner approximations by intersection, avoiding the explicit treatment of simultaneous active constraints. The boundary samples are converted into a half-space representation, and runtime complexity analyses are derived for both the sampling and polytope reconstruction algorithms. An extension of the framework to Hammerstein systems is also presented.
  The proposed approach is demonstrated on a triple integrator and a higher-dimensional mass-spring-damper chain, illustrating the geometric construction of the admissible set approximation and demonstrating scalability to higher-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [241] [ArcLP: A Matlab implementation of an $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{n}L)$ arc-search infeasible interior-point algorithm for linear programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29673)
*Yang,Yaguang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于Matlab实现的弧搜索不可行内点算法（Arc-Search Infeasible Interior Point Algorithm）用于求解线性规划问题。该算法具有$O(\sqrt{n}L)$的多项式复杂度上界，是所有内点算法中最优的。软件在PC和Linux平台上通过了Netlib基准测试问题的广泛验证，包括包含数万个约束和数十万变量的难题。数值结果与流行的Mehrotra预测-校正算法进行了比较，表明该算法不仅在理论上具有最佳多项式界，在实际计算中也具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已经有了具有最佳多项式复杂度界$O(\sqrt{n}L)$的弧搜索不可行内点算法理论，但缺乏一个实际可用的、经过充分测试的软件实现来验证该算法在实践中的表现，并将其与广泛使用的Mehrotra预测-校正算法进行对比。

Method: 在Matlab中实现了弧搜索不可行内点算法，讨论了软件架构和主要功能，总结了关键策略，并通过简单例子说明其易用性。使用Netlib基准测试问题在PC和Linux平台上进行了广泛测试。

Result: 该软件在所有测试的Netlib基准问题（包括包含数万个约束和数十万变量的大规模问题）上都成功找到了最优解。数值结果与Mehrotra预测-校正算法进行了比较，表明弧搜索算法不仅在理论上具有最优的多项式复杂度界，在计算性能上也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 实现的弧搜索不可行内点算法既拥有内点算法中最佳的多项式复杂度界$O(\sqrt{n}L)$，又在实际计算中与流行的Mehrotra预测-校正算法相当，是理论与实践兼备的线性规划求解方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a Matlab implementation of an arc-search infeasible interior point algorithm for linear programming (LP), which has a proven polynomial bound of $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{n}L)$, the best among all interior-point algorithms for LP. Software architecture and major functions are discussed. Its ease of use is described by a simple example. Crucial strategies are summarized. Quality of the software is assured because this software has been extensively tested on both PC and Linux for the widely used Netlib benchmark linear programming problems in standard form. Some benchmark test problems involve tens of thousands of constraints and hundreds of thousands of variables. For all tested problems, the code found the optimal solution. The numerical results have been compared to those obtained by the popular Mehrotra's predictor-corrector algorithm. We conclude that the implemented algorithm not only has the best polynomial bound but also is computationally competitive compared to the popular Mehrotra's predictor-corrector algorithm.

</details>


### [242] [Sign compression for Muon: SignMuon, MuonSign, and the Limits of Error Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29674)
*Maria Smirnova,Alexey Kravatskiy*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究 SignMuon 优化器——将 Muon 更新压缩为每个参数一位（取符号）。理论上证明 SignMuon 及其变体（MuonUSign、MuonSign）在一般情形下可能发散（即使在线性函数上也会上升），误差反馈（EF）应用于 Muon 输出时无法修复，但应用于梯度时有效，得到 EF21-MuonUSign 和 EF21-MuonSign 的 O(T^{-1/2}) 收敛率。然而实验表明，实践中表现最好的恰恰是被证明会发散的"sign-after-LMO"变体，展示了理论与实践的反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极低通信预算下运行矩阵感知优化器（matrix-aware optimizer），将 Muon 更新压缩到每参数一位是最直接的方式。但需要回答一个关键问题：这种极端压缩是否会导致算法发散？以及如何修复（误差反馈）？

Method: 1) 对 SignMuon（sign-after-LMO）、MuonUSign（sign-before-LMO）和 MuonSign（sign-on-both-sides）进行理论分析和反例构造，展示其发散行为。2) 分析误差反馈在 Muon 输出和梯度两个不同位置的应用效果。3) 在中央化/联邦 CIFAR-10 和 nanoGPT speedrun 上进行实验验证。

Result: 理论上：SignMuon 即使在线性函数上也可上升；sign-before 和 sign-on-both-sides 也不修复（构造了显式反例）；EF 应用于 Muon 输出时对任意平滑常数、步长和动量都可能失效；EF 应用于梯度时有效，EF21-MuonUSign 和 EF21-MuonSign 达到 O(T^{-1/2}) 的平方梯度范数收敛率。实验上：sign-after-LMO（被证明发散的变体）在三个任务上一致表现最好，可证明收敛的变体反而次之。

Conclusion: 理论与实践之间存在显著反转：被理论证明发散的压缩位置（sign-after-LMO）在实际大规模训练中表现最佳，而可证明收敛的变体反而落后。这表明在使用压缩矩阵感知优化器时，启发式方法在当前规模下比理论保证更重要。最佳实践选择（sign-after-LMO）恰好是最不安全的理论选择。

Abstract: SignMuon compresses the Muon update to one bit per parameter by taking its elementwise sign, providing the most direct way to run a matrix-aware optimizer under an extremely low communication budget. It outperforms SignSGD in practice, yet it can ascend even on a linear function. Signing the gradient before the Linear Minimization Oracle (LMO), rather than after, does not repair this: we construct a small explicit instance on which sign-before (MuonUSign) and sign-on-both-sides (MuonSign) ascend as well, so no placement of the sign around the oracle descends in general. Error feedback, the standard remedy for a biased compressor, does not rescue SignMuon: when applied to Muon's output, error feedback can fail for every smoothness constant, step size, and momentum. Applied to the gradient, error feedback does work, and EF21-MuonUSign and EF21-MuonSign attain the standard $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ rate for the squared gradient norm on smooth nonconvex problems, the latter at one bit in each direction. Experiments then reverse the ordering: across centralized CIFAR-10, federated CIFAR-10, and the nanoGPT speedrun, the strongest compressed method is consistently sign-after-the-LMO, precisely the placement we prove divergent, with the provably convergent variants trailing it. Compressing after the LMO, a heuristic, matters more at these scales than the guarantee does.

</details>
